Chapter 901: Welcome Back, Mount Hua's Sword Saint (1)

The world was filled with a suffocating silence.

Even though there were over a hundred people gathered, not even the slightest sound could be heard. Their eyes, as if forgetting to blink, were fixed on one spot.

This was because a strange sight had unfolded, which could only be described as bizarre.

A dagger, a little longer than a span, was literally floating in the air. It seemed as if it had been tied to a transparent thread and held in place, defying all the laws of the world.

Soon, the dagger began to fly slowly, at a speed as boring as a snail crawling.

There was not a single smile on the faces of those watching that sight.

Instead, their faces turned pale, their mouths shut tightly, and their lips trembled. Their appearance reflected the intense feeling of those witnessing this unusual moment.

“That...”

Baek Cheon unconsciously clenched his fist.

The dagger was moving through the air so slowly that it was questionable whether it was moving at all.

But Baek Cheon could feel it. The qi that the dagger was radiating was so strong that Baek Cheon’s internal qi began to protect his body by moving on its own, beyond his will.

He could tell.

How dangerous that dagger was. How many things were inside it?

A cold sweat broke out on Baek Cheon’s forehead.

‘And Chung Myung?’

His gaze quickly left the dagger and turned to where it was aimed.

Chung Myung’s feet also stopped. Chung Myung’s feet, which seemed like they would advance while cutting down everything in the world, were now on the ground for the first time since they started the fight.

More than the dagger floating in the air and overwhelming everything in all directions, or Tang Gunak’s face that had turned pale from releasing the dagger, Chung Myung’s face, with lips tightly shut and all his nerves focused on the dagger flying towards him, brought Baek Cheon a greater sense of crisis.

“Fa...ther.”

At that moment, a low voice emerged. When he turned around, he saw Tang Soso with her hands clasped together and eyes tightly shut.

Of course, it would be complicated. She wouldn’t be happy with anyone’s victory.

But Baek Cheon wanted to see the result of this spar with his own eyes. The others, too, felt the same.

Tang Gunak, the famous and strong warrior.

How much meaning was contained in his name?

To Baek Cheon and the younger warriors, that name was nothing less than a symbol of the Order of the Murim World and the strength of the current murim world.

Could Chung Myung really surpass that heavy and intimidating presence?

‘Overcome it!’

Baek Cheon bit his lips tightly.

‘Chung Myung!’

With a burning intention, he cast his gaze at Chung Myung. Chung Myung was still staring at the flying daggers approaching him.

Woong!

As if he wasn’t going to counterattack yet, the dagger slowly moved towards Chung Myung.

Chung Myung’s entire body felt tense.

‘This is...?’

It was as if the viper with the strongest venom, capable of taking an opponent’s life with just a brush, was staring at him from inside its hole.

The tension was such that if he moved even a hair, its venomous fangs would come flying to bite him down.

‘Dark Explosion Dagger?’

... No.

This was a move even he didn’t know.

At least, there was no move like this among the twelve techniques that Tang Bo used.

Could it have been something that Tang Bo hadn’t learned?

No, it couldn’t be so.

Tang Bo had perfectly mastered all the martial arts. Wasn’t that why he was also called the Dark Saint?

There couldn’t have been a technique among the twelve arts that Tang Bo hadn’t mastered, and there couldn’t be a technique that he wasn’t shown by Tang Bo.

So...

Kuaak.

Chung Myung’s hand gripped the sword’s handle tightly.

‘I have no choice but to confirm this.’

As Chung Myung moved, the speed of the slowly moving dagger increased slightly.

Woong!

At the same time, the dagger began to cry.

The dagger, which had been moving like a snail, flew in. The dagger, which had been increasing its speed little by little, very little by little, had now turned so fast that it was clearly visible to the eye.

And.

Rumble!

The moment a rough sword sound was emitted from it, the flying dagger began to rotate. Then, it gradually sped up and spun so fast that it couldn’t be followed with the eyes.

Swish!

The dagger’s qi, which had been spreading out in all directions, swirled around. The resulting wind pressure pushed the dust that filled the ground and soon began to pull it back, too.

The dirt and gravel shot up with a thud and got sucked into this whirlpool.

Kwaaaak!

A huge Dragon Wind Fist swept around the dagger. The huge whirlpool, dyed yellow, was more like a giant yellow dragon ascending than the wind.

“Kuak!”

“Get back!”

Those who had been watching the confrontation from a distance lowered their forms. This huge gravitational force seemed to suck them in too.

But that wasn’t the end.

The Dragon Wind Fist that was spinning fiercely and sucking everything in hadn’t yet increased its speed.

It was just spinning around and around in that spot, threatening Chung Myung.

A bright blue light radiated from Chung Myung’s eyes. Even as he watched the enormous vortex of qi, his mouth curled up. He even showed his teeth and smiled.

As if he was really enjoying this situation.

‘Here it comes.’

And then.

Kwaaaaak!

The sudden change happened.

The slow-moving dagger, which seemed to reach Chung Myung in a day, exploded with incredible speed.

At that moment, Chung Myung saw it.

The dagger, which seemed to be riding at a different time while the surroundings had slowed down, spun fiercely. The vortex that was created behind it was sucking everything around it.

Even Chung Myung’s body was being sucked into the dagger that was flying towards him.

At that moment, Chung Myung’s eyes widened.

Even Chun Myung, who had faced countless battles and overcome numerous martial arts challenges, couldn’t have anticipated this attack. Yet, there was another reason.

The myriad assassin weapons he had struck down, neutralized, and destroyed now lay scattered on the ground. Suddenly, as if responding to an unseen force, they rose into the air and began to move simultaneously.

‘What...?’

It appeared as if the ground itself was rising to engulf Chung Myung.

If the Thousand Flower Rain descended from the sky to blanket the world, this attack would devour everything from below.

An assault that could even shock Chung Myung.

In the face of flying daggers laced with poison and the Poison King’s force, as if intent on overwhelming the opponent, the assassins’ qi moved like a torrential downpour.

There was nowhere to escape. Not even above.

In that life-or-death moment, what Chung Myung saw was neither the dagger heading his way nor Tang Gunak’s pale face.

It was Baek Cheon and his group, shouting with their mouths agape.

‘Those....’

Seeing their desperate expressions, tinged with despair, Chung Myung’s face twisted.

‘Morons.’

Why would they panic?

Were they afraid he would be hurt? Or that the sword of Mount Hua wouldn’t prevail?

‘Look carefully now.’

Who could have imagined?

Who didn’t hope for this?

From the moment their ancestors settled in Mount Hua and took up their swords, everyone wished to see the tip of that sword.

That was why previous generations passed their will to the next.

That was why the next generations learned and followed the teachings of those before them.

Learning was the agreement of those with different names but the same intentions. Order was the will of those living in different times but sharing the same mind.

That was why it was called sword technique and martial arts.

‘It is here.’

The sword of Mount Hua held everything that those who had used the name of Mount Hua and their wishes from the past to the present.

And now, the sword, those who stood here, and even the will of Chung Myung would come together.

‘Bloom.’

A red plum blossom bloomed at the tip of Chung Myung’s sword.

Blooming represented the desire for conception and the nobility of birth.

What came after that?

‘Bloom out.’

If they established their will correctly, believed in it, and carried it out, it would spread throughout the world. Perhaps the fragrance of the plum blossoms would bloom across the vast nine provinces.

“Plum Blossoms... bloom in nine provinces....”

The blooming plum blossoms sway gently. In the blowing wind, there is the soft rustling of the grass and the passing clouds.

Not a single plum blossom was the same; each one was different.

Some lacked leaves, some had yet to fully bloom, and some were plain. Even in full bloom, some plum blossoms were pale and didn’t match.

However, as more plum blossoms bloomed, all the different flowers were contained in a single flow, forming waves and blending together.

The plum blossoms didn’t resist the blowing wind; instead, they carried it, slowly floating in the air.

Sometimes swiftly, and sometimes gently.

Riding the oncoming wind, swirling and rising, then quietly settling down, and then drifting far away again.

The plum blossom leaves, which spread out while swirling around Chung Myung’s body, blocked the dagger flying behind him.

Kakak!

Then, the daggers, which flew fiercely, pierced the petals without mercy.

However, before one petal could even crumble, a new petal blocked the path, and just as another petal was about to wilt, another flew in and embraced the cold metal.

Kakang!

The clear sound of the clash spread like music.

The sand and the needles, which shot out reflecting the sunlight, lost their force as they were entangled in the flower petals, which did not feel a single bit of pressure.

The expanse of the sky is vast and sparse, yet it misses nothing.

The flower petals carried by the wind seemed to flutter aimlessly, but they did not miss anything as they flew towards Chung Myung.

And at that moment, everyone witnessed it.

The assassin needles and the Soul Destroying Sand, which seemed poised to envelop Chung Myung and create chaos, were deflected by the plum blossoms.

“....!”

The plum blossoms gently repelled everything they encountered, stretching and scattering into the world.

Across the vast field, reaching far beyond.

The red-tinged plum blossoms spread out like giant plum blossoms, unfurling their leaves.

Kwaak! Kuwaak!

The final daggers, infused with all of Tang Gunak’s qi, advanced through the swirling plum blossoms. Forward and forward.

Though they trembled as if they might falter at any moment, they never ceased.

They surged fiercely, tearing through and pushing aside the petals. As if drawing on the last of their strength, they pressed forward relentlessly.

Swish.

Tear apart another petal.

Swish.

Crush through another sword qi.

However, the dagger, which had been advancing determinedly, soon began to tremble as if its strength had waned, slowing down and coming to a stop. Even the disciples of Mount Hua gasped in awe.

But at that very moment.

Kwang!

With a thunderous noise that shattered the silence enveloping Mount Hua, Tang Gunak’s body surged forward toward the halted dagger like a bolt of lightning.

The disciples of Mount Hua widened their eyes in astonishment at the unexpected movement.

Tang Gunak seized the dagger in an instant, breaking through the plum blossom sword qi as he rushed forward.

And Chung Myung also charged toward him as if it were the most natural thing.

“That!”

“E-enough!”

A scream-like voice erupted from the disciples’ mouths, who believed this confrontation would end with one of them perishing.

Kwaaang!

The dagger and the plum blossom sword collided head-on in the air.

Kwaaaak!

A strong wind blew explosively in all directions, and an incredible wave of qi surged upward.

Wooong.

The dagger aimed right in front of Chung Myung’s neck twitched. Tang Gunak’s sleeves bulged as if they would release the qi at any moment.

Kuaak!

The sword, which approached Tang Gunak’s face as if it would split it open at any moment, emitted a red sword qi. It seemed as if it would split Tang Gunak apart at any moment.

The world stopped as the blades met.

At that moment, no one could move forward, and no one could even speak.

There was only one thing moving.

A fluttering petal.

A fragile petal swept up in the confrontation between the two rose to the sky and slowly fell down along the calming flow of air.

Everyone’s eyes were fixed on that sight.

The petal, which slowly fell, then rose up again and slightly moved around, soon approached the weapons aiming at one another.

And then, as if it was coming to a quiet end, it fell on the dagger.

“....”

“....”

The two people’s eyes turned to the plum leaf that fell on the blue blade.

Not the plum blossom sword qi, but a real plum leaf.

The moment they saw the petal that didn’t match this powerful clash, the two people’s faces turned blank at the same time.

Their blades were naturally lowered.

A dejected smile soon appeared on their lips as they looked back and forth between the petals and each other’s faces.

Chung Myung’s hand lightly grabbed the dagger at his neck with his left hand. Tang Gunak also lightly grabbed the sword pointed at his face with his left hand.

The two people, who grabbed the weapons as if they were stealing them, looked at each other’s weapons in their hands without another word.

“... I never thought this sword’s blade would break.”

“That is what I should say. The weapon of Lord Tang should be the one...”

The numerous scars engraved on the sword’s body seemed to tell of each other’s past.

Tak!

Tang Gunak, who caught the blade thrown by Chung Myung, handed over the sword to Chung Myung.

The two people who had been staring at each other in silence for a while burst into laughter.

Chung Myung shrugged his shoulders and asked with a smile.

“Continue?”

“Hmm.”

Tang Gunak hesitated for a moment and then shook his head.

“Then there is no point anymore.”

“....”

A warm smile spread across his lips.

“Welcome back, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.”

“Wpoahhhh!”

Finally, a huge cheer erupted from all directions.

It wasn’t just a single word.

Mount Hua and Tang family. It was the moment when those two great names faced each other after three years.

Chapter 902: Welcome Back, Mount Hua's Sword Saint (2)

What was the most important thing when welcoming a guest?

The answer would probably vary depending on the person.

Some might say it was the heart, while others might say it was courtesy. Or they might say it was a cozy bed.

However, the thoughts of those who had become accustomed to living on this barren mountaintop seemed to be a little different from those of ordinary people.

“Drinkkkkkk!”

“Meat! Meatttt!”

“Kuaa! Not the rabbit meat! Pork! Pork meat!”

“Hahahahaha! Have another drink!”

A sight that was too much to bear was unfolding here, with Taoists in robes half-naked, devouring alcohol and meat.

The only good thing was that since they were on high mountains, there were no eyes to watch this tragic situation.

The disciples of Mount Hua, who had no need to be concerned about others, drank alcohol and tore into meat as if they were going to let go of all the regrets of the past three years in a single moment.

As soon as the food they had ordered in advance was delivered, it kept coming down, so the disciples had to take turns running down the mountain to get new food.

In the middle of the noisy drinking party, Tang Gunak, who was facing Chung Myung, quietly raised his glass and held it out. Chung Myung smiled and lifted the bottle of alcohol.

Tang Gunak’s expression was also comfortable.

He had confirmed the growth of Chung Myung to the point of overflowing. He had to use his secret technique in a not-real-life situation, so it wasn’t an exaggeration to say that the reward for running all the way from the Yangtze River to here was fulfilled.

“How is it?”

“What do you mean?”

“Have you confirmed that I have not been idle for the past three years?”

“... Hmm.”

Tang Gunak frowned as if he was struggling. It seemed as if he was having trouble deciding what to say.

“Definitely.”

“Yes.”

“I have experienced many swords, but this is the first time I have seen something like that.”

“Hmm.”

“I now completely understand how the sword of Mount Hua was able to rule the world in the past.”

“Hmm.”

“Even if we search the world now, there will not be many who can handle such a sword.”

“Hmm!”

“You have worked hard. No matter how talented you are, it would have been impossible without hard work. You deserve praise.”

Chung Myung’s chin rose higher and higher at Tang Gunak’s remark, which seemed to lift him even higher.

“...if you keep doing that, he will see the sky.”

“He is already seeing it, sasuk.”

“Moron.”

Baek Cheon and Jo Gul clicked their tongues, and Yu Yiseol criticized him, but Chung Myung’s chin, already raised in high spirits, showed no signs of coming down.

“And...”

Tang Gunak’s sharp gaze moved to the Five Swords sitting around Chung Myung.

“...it isn’t just you, Chung Myung, who has gotten strong.”

A satisfied smile formed on Tang Gunak’s lips.

“The public announcement is over.”

Chung Myung, who had lowered his chin, said while looking at Tang Gunak.

“I don’t know about others, but it doesn’t seem like something the family Lord should be saying.”

“Me? Why?”

“That attack.”

When those words came out of Chung Myung’s mouth, everyone stopped what they were doing and looked at Tang Gunak. Everyone was curious about the last technique.

“Did the lord make it?”

“...How did you know?”

“Well, simple reason. Hmm, it is entirely different from what we have seen before...”

Chung Myung, seeing Tang Gunak’s slightly suspicious gaze, snickered and continued.

“Something is a bit strange.”

“Ahem!”

Tang Gunak coughed loudly and sipped the alcohol in one gulp as if he were upset. No, actually, he looked more embarrassed than upset.

“Strange?”

“That one?”

Baek Cheon opened his eyes wide and looked at Chung Myung. Even Yu Yiseol, who rarely showed emotions, glared at Chung Myung with a thought, ‘Has that guy gone insane?’

It couldn’t be helped.

What Tang Gunak showed at the end was incredible even to them. And yet, how could his moves be awkward?

However, Tang Gunak didn’t show any sign of displeasure, but rather coughed repeatedly as if he was being stung.

“Did it show much?”

“A bit much...?”

“Ugh.”

A groan escaped his lips. Tang Soso couldn’t take her eyes off Tang Gunak, who she couldn’t see very well.

Chung Myung casually asked.

“It is unfinished, right?”

“Yes.”

“I knew it.”

Tang Gunak let out a deep sigh. To put it bluntly, that move wasn’t yet ready for a real fight.

“Why is it still not done? Its power is truly incredible.”

Baek Cheon couldn’t hold back their curiosity and joined the talks.

And Tang Gunak answered.

“It isn’t all about the power, right?”

“... Uh?”

“That technique consumes too much stamina. Once you use it, one will be too exhausted to even continue the fight anymore.”

“Isn’t there a saying that one blow is sure to kill? If one could definitely defeat the opponent with that one hit...”

“You cannot defeat everyone.”

“... ah, no. That is...”

Baek Cheon stuttered, unable to continue.

Right. Yes, that attack couldn’t knock the enemy down. But that was it...

‘No, what on earth was he saying this about?’

The kid who stopped it was the strange one, not the technique?

If it had been a little more precise, it would have been possible to cut off that guy’s neck?

Neither of the two were good answers.

“It isn’t precise enough, so it isn’t a one-hit attack; it isn’t just a one-hit, but it consumes a lot of internal qi. So this kind of...”

“An empty vessel makes a lot of noise?”

“....”

At Chung Myung’s words, Tang Gunak’s eyebrows twitched.

“Uh? No? Then maybe a bright one?”

Clang!

The alcohol glass in Tang Gunak’s hand shattered in an instant.

“Hmm... it seems that the alcohol glasses of Mount Hua are not of good quality. Let us tell the Tang family to send new ones.”

‘Even if it was made of iron, it would have broken...’

However, no one said anything out loud about it. It would be better to take poison if the Tang family gave it to you.

Tang Gunak, who had been trembling, let out a deep sigh.

“It is unfinished. I don’t know when it will be completed.”

“Ah. So you used that unfinished technique for the spar?”

Furrow.

Tang Gunak’s eyebrows flinched again.

“... It is unfinished, so it is used for the spar. If it was complete, would I have been able to do that? I wouldn’t want to kill a friend I worked so hard to get.”

“Even if it is complete, I don’t think I will die...”

“If you make a promise like that, you will lose your head.”

“...”

“...”

Tang Gunak and Chung Myung glared at each other with eyes that seemed to want to kill one another. The others, who were exhausted with this, turned to Tang Soso. It was telling her to do something about this. She sighed as well.

“Stop it now! You are not even kids!”

“Ahem.”

“Tsk.”

Only then did Tang Gunak and Chung Myung turn their heads away and gulp down the alcohol.

At that moment, Im So-Byeong, who was watching them, clicked his tongue.

“Anyway, men with swords....”

The place where the Tang family lord and a Taoist of Mount Hua were being criticized by the bandits.

“Surely.”

As if to refresh the atmosphere, Tang Gunak opened his mouth.

“It isn’t yet a full technique that can be used in a real battle or spar. I was too greedy. I am thinking about whether I should be more sophisticated even if it means giving up destructive power.”

“It isn’t that needed, right?”

“Uh?”

Chung Myung shrugged.

“The reason the internal qi consumption is excessive is not because the force requires so much, but because the method of pushing the internal qi is too aggressive.”

“Method?”

“Yes. It involves pulling it in by rotating it.”

“Right.”

“Instead of condensing it and exploding it out, you should rotate the qi inside the dagger. The consumption is greatest when the stagnant qi returns all at once.”

“Uh?”

Tang Gunak’s eyes shone, his expression revealing that he had found a clue.

“... the direction of the weapon and the direction of the qi are in sync... it is basic. The most basic of the basics. But...”

“Yes.”

Chung Myung nodded, anticipating what Tang Gunak was about to say.

“It is the most basic of basics, so when aiming for an increase, you forget the natural progression.”

“... I see. I made an obvious mistake.”

Of course, it wasn’t as simple as Chung Myung suggested. Making the qi circulate within a fixed structure was much more difficult than it seemed. But if one could manage it, they would drastically reduce the consumption of internal qi.

‘Perhaps its strength will increase even more.’

If that happened, the Twelve Daggers technique would no longer be limited to twelve.

‘Thirteen Daggers... no, let us just keep it as Twelve.’

What a peculiar name...

“Right. Yes, that could be possible.”

As Tang Gunak mumbled to himself and nodded in understanding, Chung Myung quietly began to speak again.

“That is not the only thing that is unfinished, right?”

“Hmm?”

Tang Gunak pretended not to know, and Chung Myung chuckled.

“Originally, the daggers that fell on the ground would fly up at the same time, right?”

“... were you in the Tang family in a past life?”

He wasn’t in the Tang family, but he did know someone similar. When he went to the Tang family, the lord ran out of the household barefoot to throw me out. Right. He wouldn’t cause trouble.

“Right. That is how it is completed. But... with my current skills, it is impossible to execute.”

“If you reduce the consumption of the internal qi, it will surely be possible.”

“... is that so?”

“Of course.”

A picture was drawn in Chung Myung’s mind.

A single flying dagger with incredible force. And the assassin weapons would also be flying around.

That would definitely be a visionary technique.

And on the day that the technique revealed itself to the world, another Dark Saint would appear.

“But...”

“Uh?”

“Why did you suddenly create a new technique?”

“Hmm.”

Tang Gunak smiled broadly at Chung Myung’s question.

“Of course, I haven’t mastered what I have yet, so it is a bit much to create a new technique.”

“...”

Chung Myung said nothing.

There were two reasons for this.

One was that Tang Gunak was right.

Even Tang Bo, who was a more outstanding warrior than Tang Gunak now, didn’t come up with new techniques for the existing ones. So, it certainly was unreasonable for Tang Gunak to make a new one.

But the second reason he didn’t say anything was the opposite.

The new technique that Tang Gunak created clearly surpassed the existing techniques he knew as part of the Twelve Techniques.

“However, if you ask why I created this, there is just one answer.”

“What is that?”

“Because it is needed.”

Tang Gunak shrugged and continued.

“The Tang family’s secret technique and arts are certainly excellent. But... it is lacking when handling the warriors that can be called ‘absolute’. Especially since the number of types of daggers you can use is limited; the more you throw them out, the more difficult it turns.”

“Right.”

“It would be nice if you could just retrieve the dagger, but if you are handling an absolute warrior, you won’t even have the chance to do that.”

Chung Myung slowly nodded.

This was clearly something Tang Bo had been contemplating. Why else would he have considered attaching a tough silver thread to the end of the dagger? Of course, he abandoned the idea because it would have disrupted the trajectory.

“While I was training and constantly worrying, I looked at the daggers around and thought, why should I just use those daggers in my hands? The daggers scattered across the sky aren’t mine, and the daggers in my hand aren’t mine either.”

“Ah...”

Tang Gunak coughed in embarrassment.

“Well, that is what I started with... but it isn’t going well. I feel embarrassed to show my shortcomings.”

“No.”

Chung Myung shook his head seriously.

“It was the best.”

“....”

“Really.”

A smile appeared on Tang Gunak’s lips as he stared at him.

“... Thank you.”

“You are kind.”

Chung Myung filled Tang Gunak’s cup. Tang Gunak then took the bottle and filled Chung Myung’s cup.

“Drink.”

“Yes.”

Chung Myung emptied his glass happily.

‘We are progressing...’

In the past three years, Mount Hua certainly gained a lot. However, Mount Hua was not the only one who worked hard.

His descendants had created something that even Tang Bo couldn’t create in the past. It was still lacking, but one day, this technique would become perfect like the other twelve techniques.

If Tang Gunak could not do it, someone who inherited his intention would, and someone else who followed after him would gradually complete it.

That was how learning developed.

‘Right?’

Chung Myung asked a person who came to mind lightly and smiled as he looked at Tang Gunak. Then, as if he suddenly remembered something, his eyes widened.

“Ah, but I have a question to ask.”

“Uh? What?”

Chung Myung asked playfully, giggling.

“It’s because you are running low on the daggers, but why do you only use the twelve daggers? Is there a reason for it?”

“... Uh?”

“Just because it’s called the Twelve Dagger Technique doesn’t mean you have to use only twelve, right? Since you’re carrying a lot of assassin weapons anyway, when you have around 24 daggers, you don’t have to worry about running out, right?”

“...”

“Even if your ultimate goal is to use twelve daggers at the same time, you don’t have to carry just twelve, right? It’s not bad to have a spare...?”

“...”

Unlike Chung Myung’s playful face, Tang Gunak’s face turned pale.

“...”

And Chung Myung, who saw that, couldn’t laugh anymore.

... Um?

Excuse me? Did he not...

Think about it? For real!?

-Woah, I didn’t think about it either.

After all... it seemed that what was inherited wasn’t just the will.

Chapter 903: Welcome Back, Mount Hua's Sword Saint (3)

Pour.

The glass was filled with alcohol.

Chung Myung, who had been silently pouring, glanced at the person sitting across from him. The person was meticulously wiping a dagger with a pure white hemp cloth.

“...Put the dagger away. The alcohol is losing its flavor.”

“Oh my, in this war, who even cares about alcohol? If it is edible, then it is fine enough.”

Tang Bo wiped off all the oil from his dagger and furrowed his eyebrows. He looked as if he was taking perfect care of the weapon as if no one would call him a member of the Tang family.

“Does that mean the dagger is sharp?”

“If you don’t know about it, don’t talk. A human-like Taoist hyung can pick up a fallen sword or something to cut, but a person like me needs a weapon that perfectly fits the hand. This is a masterpiece, you know.”

“What nonsense.”

Regardless, Tang Bo meticulously maintained all twelve daggers. After cleaning them neatly, grinding off the scratched parts, and re-oiling them, he finally placed the dagger in front of Chung Myung.

Then he picked up the bottle and filled Chung Myung’s empty cup.

“Why is the Taoist hyung in such a bad mood today?”

“....”

“No, no, it is always bad. Hmm, right, it is like the law of nature now.”

“This bastard!”

“Come on, have a drink? A drink?”

When Tang Bo snickered and lifted his cup, Chung Myung snickered and lifted his glass in return. The two glasses clinked in the air.

Chung Myung slowly emptied his glass and looked at Tang Bo, who was sitting across from him and asked.

“How many people died yesterday?”

“Twenty.”

“...Not killed but dead.”

“Five. I told you to stop acting like those bastards....”

Tang Bo’s face twisted as if he was annoyed. It seemed that there were quite a few casualties among the Tang family people he led.

That was how the war was. No matter how careful they were and how hard one tried, there were bound to be deaths.

“Those bastards have been going insane lately. If they had moved, their balls would have gone flying off.”

Tang Bo mimed, slitting his throat with the dagger in his hand.

“Insane bastard.”

Chung Myung chuckled.

It was just a joke, but no one here made jokes like that.

In a place where the same spot they sat and ate yesterday was empty today, a joke about death was too heavy to be taken lightly, yet too light as a feather to be discussed seriously.

“I almost got killed, you know? I ran into the high priest bastard.”

“Hmm.”

Chung Myung’s eyes narrowed slightly at the mention of the high priest.

Several high priests had already been beheaded by his sword, but the title of high priest still posed a threat to Chung Myung.

“It wasn’t done properly, so I managed to get out...”

Tang Bo pressed his chin hard.

“If it had been attached right, my neck would have been in danger. Damn it, why was I born into the Tang family?”

“You are weak; why are you using the Tang family as an excuse?”

“Oh my. Our Taoist Hyung seems to be stuck in the mountains and doesn’t know a lot of things. It isn’t that I am weak; it is just the nature of the dagger. I can handle it up to a moderate level, but if it goes beyond that, there is a limit to a dagger.”

“You are weak then.”

“No, what I am saying is...”

“You are weak.”

“....”

Tang Bo smiled brightly with a red face.

“Be careful when you head home. You might get stabbed in the back by a blind dagger for all you know.”

“Then it won’t be a blind dagger, but a blind man throwing it.”

“Ah, damn it.”

Tang Bo grumbled and fiddled with the daggers in front of him. No matter how he looked at it, it seemed like he was considering whether he could just stick it in Chung Myung’s head or not.

“Well, so... I am thinking about developing something useful like a technique.”

“A technique?”

“Yes. A technique that can handle those damn high priests or those damn bastards under the Heavenly Demon. Those bastards who wear black clothes, those bastards who walk around in black robes. Ah, it would be better if they held a sword.”

Chung Myung looked down at his own clothes in silence.

Hmm. They were black. Very black, too.

Chung Myung snickered.

“Stop fooling around and do what you were doing. When are you going to use the technique you made now in real life? You will try it out in the afterlife.”

“Well, it isn’t like I will use it right away.”

“Uh?”

Tang Bo smiled.

“If I just have the rough idea, I can make it slowly after the war ends.”

“Are you confident that you will be alive by then?”

“Ah, I am Tang Bo. If the Grim Reaper comes looking for me, I will just put a dagger through his head.”

“Crazy man.”

Tang Bo laughed.

Tang Bo stared at the empty glass in his hand with an odd look, then grabbed the bottle and filled it.

“Even if I don’t make it...”

Chung Myung raised his head at the low voice of Tang Bo.

Tang Bo, who had been slowly turning the glass in his hand, poured the alcohol into his mouth in one gulp.

“Someone will continue it. Someone who inherits the Tang family’s blood and learns the twelve techniques.”

“....”

“Then, even if someone ends up in a similar situation as me, they won’t have to worry like I did, right?”

“Hmm...”

Chung Myung was about to say something like ‘You think too much,’ when Tang Bo’s slightly irritated voice rang in his ear.

“Then at least my descendants won’t be beaten by some black-hearted Taoists who come down from rocky mountains with nothing but grass to eat. Just slap a dagger into their backs...”

Srrng.

“Ah! T-that! Why are you pulling the sword now when we are drinking! You really don’t get jokes! Where did you come from to train in Taoism!”

“You crazy bastard...”

Chung Myung snickered, pushed the sword back, and picked up the alcohol. The drink flowing down his throat was unusually bitter.

“What is the point of it all?”

“Uh?”

“Descendants and everything...”

Chung Myung’s eyes, which had sunken gloomily, turned to the window. The dark clouds covering the sun seemed like they could burst into rain at any moment.

“What do the survivors have to care about? I might not even be alive tomorrow.”

“Hmm. Well, that is true.”

Chung Myung’s gaze shifted to Tang Bo.

“You’re a bastard who got thrown out of the Tang family, and yet you still think of the descendants.”

“Who threw me out? They just couldn’t handle me!”

“...you are unbearable.”

“Was that an insult?”

“It is a compliment.”

“That is an insult!”

Tang Bo gritted his teeth. Seeing him like that, Chung Myung finally laughed.

Rather than a loud laugh, because it was funny, he kept trying to laugh whenever there was something to laugh about.

That was probably why he kept looking at Tang Bo instead of his sahyung whenever he had a tough time on the battlefield.

‘You don’t know.’

It was amazing that people could yell, get angry, joke, and laugh like that on a battlefield where people were losing their reason to exist as humans.

“But it is just how it is.”

“Uh?”

Tang Bo said, stroking the dagger blade in his hand with his fingertips.

“Someone...”

The eyes looking at his face, reflected in the dagger, were calm. The expression on his dark face was clearly washed out.

“The next person should not repeat the mistakes of the fools. At least they should be better than us, right?”

“...”

But as if that would never happen, Tang Bo quickly erased the doubt that had been hanging over his face and smiled brightly.

“There might not be a boring Taoist next to those who can wield the sword like a ghost.”

“Who cares? It’s bloodlines. I used to hate the fools around me, but as I got older, I began to worry.”

Tang Bo laughed at that. Chung Myung, too, laughed without another thought and looked up at the ceiling.

‘Someone to continue, huh...’

What nonsense.

Continue what? He could not even see their faces.

How could he know what happens after his death? He couldn’t even save the lives of those he had been living with face to face. As time went by, he was only losing more of them.

“If you have time to think about all that, kill one more at least.”

“Uh?”

“As long as you don’t make a mistake, it is fine. If you cut off the cheek of that young Heavenly Demon brat, at least your descendants won’t have to fight him.”

“Well... that is also true.”

“So don’t worry.”

“What are you even saying?”

Chung Myung’s lips curled up.

“I will cut that bastard’s neck.”

“I told you he is mine; why do you keep saying that?”

Tang Bo clicked his tongue.

“Nice. If Taoist hyung cuts off the Heavenly Demon’s head, then I will give you a gift.”

“A gift?”

“Hmm! I will make you taste the new technique that Tang Bo makes! The day is the day that the dagger gets stuck in your back, and from that day on, I will be the best warrior in the world... ah, no! I told you not to draw the sword when I am joking!”

“Just die, you moron!”

“Eikk! A Taoist is going around killing people!”

Tang Bo, who had been making a fuss and joking at the same time, suddenly dispelled the sword qi, which Chung Myung used and laughed.

“When that day comes, there will be no more wishes.”

“... just drink.”

“Take it. I will fill your glass.”

The alcohol which flowed from the tip of the bottle slowly filled the white glass.

The two of them poured alcohol as if competing with one another. As if they wanted to moisten something which had dried up long ago.

Until the rain that came that night had washed away the smell of blood, the two of them just drank.

Chung Myung opened his eyes and looked at the ceiling with a blank face.

His head slowly turned to the side. Sunlight was streaming in through the window.

‘Is it morning already?’

When the day came, he would wake up and go to practice... When he returned to Mount Hua after so long, the tension that had been controlling my body for years seemed to have been released.

Was that why he dreamed of it?

“... and so it happened.”

Right. He had forgotten.

Because he didn’t think the conversations he had without much thought were important enough to remember. No, he didn’t even know they were important.

“First, technique...”

Chung Myung snickered. It was the face of the past Plum Blossom Sword Saint.

“Moron.”

Right. He kept his promise to cut off the Heavenly Demon’s head. He cut off that head in the most pathetic means possible.

And Tang Bo also kept his promise. In fact, in the most foolish way possible.

So...

Chung Myung raised his gaze again. It was a different ceiling than the one he had seen at the drinking party.

“... I told you I won’t let you see it.”

That was why he tried so hard, but if that was going to happen again... what on earth did the death of all those people mean?

Just what...

Kwang!

At that moment, the door opened violently, with familiar faces rushing in. No, they fell in.

“Euk! What the fuck!”

Even Chung Myung, the best in the world, couldn’t help but be terrified at that.

“Ack! I told you not to push!”

“No! It is because you didn’t get out of the way!”

“I told you to let him rest! The spar wasn’t easy to do!”

“Why are you waking up a sleeping person?”

Chung Myung’s face was blank as he watched the Five Swords struggling while lying face down. He soon covered his face with both hands.

‘For this...’

Tang Bo. Didn’t this seem more like a dog’s death now? Uh?

“... what?”

Chung Myung asked weakly. Baek Cheon, who was on the bottom, covered his mouth with his fist and coughed. Even so, he looked like he had been beaten by savages, so he didn’t have any dignity even if he did that.

“Well, that... I had something to ask about the spar yesterday.”

“....”

“No matter how much I think about it, I don’t understand the technique.”

“Explain it.”

“....”

Chung Myung, who was speechless for a moment, looked at them blankly and asked.

“You had to ask it right away in the morning?”

“We have to do it right away! We need to know as fast as we can.”

“You never know when something like that will come for us! Such things need to be known right away.”

Chung Myung chuckled as if he was losing it.

Since he was letting them off... no, as he watched those guys babbling, he felt like an idiot to even feel depressed for a second there.

“How does it work?”

“Yes.”

“If I tell you, can you understand?”

“....”

“You can see as much as you know. How can I explain something you cannot even see when you are so weak? It is like teaching the alphabet to a monkey.”

“Bastard...”

“Look at this. I told you we wouldn’t hear anything good!”

“Kuak! Spit!”

Chung Myung shook his head and got out of bed at the loud reaction.

“If you have time to speak nonsense, do some training now. Don’t get lost in emotions just because you are back in Mount Hua.”

“Everyone else did it except you, though?”

“Uh?”

“You are the one lost in emotions the most! You moron!”

Baek Cheon blocked Chung Myung’s path as he shook his head and tried to leave. Chung Myung looked up with a sullen look.

“What?”

“I am sure that my head will not understand it.”

“Uh?”

“But...”

Baek Cheon twisted his lips into a smile.

“I think I will understand it with my body.”

“...”

“How is it? Let us loosen up a bit?”

Chung Myung opened his mouth as if he had something to say, then shook his head again.

“Is the place you got hit last time okay?”

“It is already healed, you moron.”

Baek Cheon let out a proud laugh. Chung Myung laughed as if he wanted to pull the air out.

“Fine, let us meet in the training ground. I will break it down where it broke again.”

“Okay!”

The Five Swords headed out without another word. Jo Gul, who was at the back, grabbed Baek Cheon’s shoulder and ran ahead.

“Me first!”

“No, you idiot! Yah, bastard! Do you not know the hierarchy?”

“When does Mount Hua even have it!”

“Move.”

“Samae! Yah! Yu Yiseol!”

Chung Myung stood there and watched it happen. As he watched the Five Swords running, chattering among themselves, it seemed like he heard voices in a dream.

-Whoever takes over should not repeat the mistakes of the fools before. At least, shouldn’t they be better than us?

Chung Myung looked at the window through which the sunlight came in. Unlike that day, the weather was clear.

“Right. Better than us, for sure.”

Better than fools like the two of them.

Chapter 904: Welcome Back, Mount Hua's Sword Saint (4)

Many people gathered at the residence of the Mount Hua Sect leader.

Not only the people of Mount Hua but also Tang Gunak and Im So-Byeong, who had arrived the day before, sat across from the sect leader.

“Firstly....”

Hyun Jong coughed softly as he continued.

“I would like to offer my deepest apologies to the two of you who must have worked hard while Mount Hua was away. And as the leader of the Heavenly Friends Alliance, I also feel the need to apologize to the two of you for not being able to fulfill my role.”

“You don’t have to say all that, alliance leader.”

Tang Gunak shook his head.

“Who doesn’t know that Mount Hua was away and that we didn’t have much time? We just had different things to do.”

“Lord...”

Amidst all of the warm talk came this crooked voice.

“Well, of course, it was a bit difficult.”

“....”

Im So-Byeong looked at Hyun Jong with a bright smile and no trace of guilt.

“We trembled at the thought of stray dogs that might come from the other side at any moment, and we had to watch out for the Nine Great Sects, who would come to the Yangtze River once in a while and glare at us for no reason. We had to please the merchants who kept talking about how expensive the toll was, and we even had to give the money we worked so hard for to the common people. We lived a hard life...!”

“....”

“Of course, Mount Hua, training behind the scenes, must have had a hard time too. Yes! What could be harder? Hahahaha.”

“No, this guy!”

Chung Myung glared and tried to get up, but Baek Cheon pressed his shoulders down hard.

“Hold on, Chung Myung. It is just the start, and if you keep doing it, we won’t be able to get anything done today.”

Then, Chung Myung, who was groaning, turned his head sharply and asked Hyun Young.

“The ledger is fine, right? Is there anything missing in it? Is there anything that was taken advantage of? Could there be something? There has to be something, right?”

It was a statement that momentarily caused confusion as to whether embezzlement was a good thing or not.

Unfortunately, Hyun Young’s answer was different from what Chung Myung wanted.

“It is clean.”

“Clean?”

“Yes, it is clean.”

Hyun Young nodded and emphasized.

“Not a single coin was missing.”

“Really?”

“I checked everything. This is the first time in my life I have seen such perfect ledgers.”

When Hyun Young looked back with an admiring expression, Im So-Byeong spread out his fan and smiled with a proud look.

“Hahaha. I am glad I could satisfy the elder.”

But then.

“I knew it! That bandit bastard!”

Chung Myung suddenly rushed at Im So-Byeong and grabbed him by the collar.

“Kuak!”

Im So-Byeong, who was suddenly grabbed by the collar, opened his eyes wide like he was struck by lightning.

“N-No! How long have you been deaf? It is clean and perfect! Perfect! Don’t you know perfect?”

“Yeah, I know, perfect! That is why you are acting like this!”

“Uh?”

Im So-Byeong looked at Chung Myung with a face that said, ‘What are you even talking about?’ and shouted.

“A bandit bastard wrote an account book, so how can you say there is nothing wrong with it? Who do you think you are fooling?”

“...”

Im So-Byeong’s mouth fell open.

“Spit it out! You moron! How much did you gulp down? You must have scraped it all clean and taken it away! Spit it out! Aren’t you going to spit it all out now!?”

Chung Myung shook Im So-Byeong’s hair. The neatly combed hair of Im So-Byeong had turned disheveled.

“C-Chung Myung. That guy...”

“I know! You are a thief, bastard! Don’t worry. Sect leader! I will take every single coin that this bastard has hidden, even in his underwear, and get it all back!”

“N-No, the Green Forest King...”

“This brat, will you not confess? Are you wanting to die? Uh?”

Tears welled up in Im So-Byeong’s eyes.

It was obvious that the thief would be suspected of doing business, so many people stayed up all night to ensure no information was overlooked, even by mistake. But what was all the effort for?

“Catch him!”

“Yes!”

As soon as Hyun Jong’s order was given, the Five Swords, who had been waiting, rushed in and pulled Chung Myung away from Im So-Byeong. Chung Myung kicked the air as he was dragged away.

“No! Let go! I’m telling you, everyone is being fooled right now!”

“The ledger is accurate!”

“How dare this bandit’s bitch speak to the sect leader!”

“....”

Ah, right.

Then let us talk.

Baek Cheon, who almost let go of Chung Myung’s hand, flinched at the last moment and regained his senses. He then grabbed Chung Myung tightly and dragged him back.

“... I am sorry, Green Forest King.”

“...”

“Are you alright?”

“... I am alright.”

His eyes looked wet, but neither Hyun Jong nor Tang Gunak bothered to point that out.

“Anyway...”

Chak!

Im So-Byeong opened his fan. It was a shame that the fan, usually a symbol of confidence, was now being used to hide his face.

“As you can see from the ledger, Plum Blossom Island is making a lot of money. Since the merchants who confirmed the Island is safe are trying to cross the Yangtze River through the Island, there is a shortage of ships.”

“Oh my! These people are stealing money to buy ships... eupp! Eup!”

“...”

Im So-Byeong looked back at Chung Myung with empty eyes. At that time, Hyun Jong said.

“Green Forest King.”

“Yes, Sect Leader....”

“Let us just not worry about it.”

“... Yes.”

Im So-Byeong coughed and continued speaking.

“As Mount Hua’s Sword Saint mentioned, increasing the number of shops could address the issue. However, since the benefits ultimately arise from controlling the Yangtze River, the situation at Plum Blossom Island could change drastically at any time, depending on the Yangtze River’s conditions.”

“Hmm. Right. Honestly, I hadn’t considered that the path would block the Yangtze River until now.”

“Yes, Sect Leader. That’s why we might incur losses if we recklessly increase the number of ships. For now, it seems wiser to maintain the current situation.”

“You have really worked hard.”

“Don’t mention it.”

Im So-Byeong smiled brightly.

“Shouldn’t we, the Green Forest Bandits, fulfill our role as proud members of the Heavenly Friends Alliance? Hahaha.”

“But where is that Evil Sect guy sticking his nose in! I think I’ll see the end before dirt gets in my eyes... eupp! Eupp!”

This time, even Tang Gunak stepped forward and covered Chung Myung’s mouth.

“Be quiet! Please be quiet!”

“Shut your mouth.”

“If you open your mouth one more time, I’ll stab you in the forehead, sahyung!”

Hyun Jong covered his face and apologized to Im So-Byeong once again.

“... I am sorry.”

“They say the life of a bandit is worse than that of a dog.”

“Isn’t that for a slave?”

“... slave or bandit, it’s the same.”

They were different. Very different.

However, Hyun Jong comforted Im So-Byeong, who was groaning under the shackles of social status.

If the Green Forest had truly lived honestly for the past three years, as Im So-Byeong claimed, the world’s perception of them would have changed significantly. Even if that perception hadn’t changed much, they would have acknowledged that Mount Hua had suppressed Green Forest.

So, as Im So-Byeong suggested, it might be necessary to clarify the position of Green Forest, which was unofficially allied with the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

“Before the problem with the Four Precept Alliance really comes to a head.”

Hyun Jong nodded quietly and then turned to Tang Gunak.

“Lord, you have had such a hard time too.”

“It wasn’t hard at all.”

Tang Gunak shook his head.

“There really wasn’t a big problem during the riots in the Yangtze River. If there was a problem, it would be that the Tang family, which already has a small number of people, had to coordinate the large area.”

Chung Myung took Baek Cheon’s hand off his mouth and grumbled.

“Then why didn’t you just use those bandits who are just messing around?”

“... it wasn’t that easy in reality. Who would welcome bandits coming down to capture the Evil Sect people?”

“Well, I told you, those bandits are of no help.”

While Im So-Byeong wondered why he had come all this way from the Yangtze River, Tang Gunak continued.

“But thanks to that, the public sentiment in the Yangtze River has clearly turned towards the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

Hyun Jong nodded at that.

He also knew how scary public sentiment could be. Wasn’t it because a few people were willing to support Mount Hua that Mount Hua was able to endure the difficult situation before Chung Myung appeared?

“But now we have reached our limit, alliance leader.”

“Hmmm.”

“Right now, the Four Precepts Alliance is quiet, so we can manage, but if the Four Precepts Alliance does rise up, the problem will definitely get worse again. We need support from the entire Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

Although it was called the Heavenly Friends Alliance, the only Heavenly Friends Alliance in the Central Plains was Mount Hua and the Tang family.

This was no different from Tang Gunak officially requesting support from Mount Hua.

“Certainly... if you stayed in the Yangtze River for the past three years, the main family in Sichuan would be in trouble.”

“It is embarrassing, but that is the truth.”

“Understood, my lord. We, Mount Hua, will—”

“What?”

“Uh?”

Everyone turned to Chung Myung.

Baek Cheon, who had been holding onto Chung Myung, slowly released his hand. He could now predict that this guy, who had been speaking nonsense before, would start talking properly.

“Should we just leave him alone?”

Baek Cheon’s hand slowly grabbed Chung Myung’s shoulder again.

Did he subdue him?

“...did we move?”

“Yes.”

“To Yangtze?”

“Yes.”

Tang Gunak frowned as he watched Chung Myung speak calmly.

“I know you’re not the type to speak thoughtlessly, but I don’t understand what you’re saying in the least.”

Baek Cheon’s eyes widened in shock.

This guy, to the lord...? He seems to have misunderstood something. There’s no one in the entire Central Plains who spoke more thoughtlessly than this guy.

“The Tang family has protected the common people all this time, of course, because it was a natural duty, but it was also to create favorable public opinion towards the Heavenly Friends Alliance. But if you back out now, wouldn’t it all be useless?”

“No, if you curse me for taking a break after doing everything you have done for me, can you be called a human? Isn’t that an animal?”

“...”

“When someone helps, you need to be grateful! You cannot ask me to hand over the money after saving me from drowning!”

“That... that is not wrong, but isn’t that how things in the world work?”

“That is right, but why would we be guarding that place? It is supposed to be guarded by the Wudang and Namgung.”

“They closed the gate...”

“Is closing the gate an official position? If you close the gate, you do nothing?”

“...that isn’t something Mount Hua should say.”

Baek Cheon nodded quickly.

Right. That wasn’t something they should be saying, right? Uh?

But Chung Myung snorted bitterly.

“Then continue.”

“Uh?”

“Are you asking for support from Mount Hua now?”

“Right...?”

Tang Gunak’s face was filled with anxiety.

And as expected... Chung Myung was being Chung Myung.

“Even if a knife is put to my throat, I won’t go to the Yangtze River to fight instead of those Wudang bastards, so either get out of there or just keep doing it!”

“....”

“Why are we doing what those rotten Nine Great Sects bastards are supposed to do!”

“Even so, even though the Nine Great Sects and the Heavenly Friends Alliance aren’t official, they are kind of like an alliance...”

“Allianceee?”

Finally, Chung Myung had lost it.

At those dazzling eyes, Tang Gunak unconsciously shut his eyes.

“I would rather stick with the Evil Sect than ally with the Nine Great Sects! Shaolin and Wudang can just eat some shit!”

“Hahaha. Then, while we are on the topic, Green Forest and...”

“You should spit out any money you ate!”

“No! I ate nothing!”

Chung Myung was throwing a tantrum, Im So-Byeong was protesting, and Tang Gunak was in agony.

In front of the scene where chaos was raging like hell, Hyun Jong smiled happily.

‘Yes. That is how it felt.’

Ah... he just wanted to go back to the deep mountains.

Please...

Chapter 905: Welcome Back, Mount Hua's Sword Saint (5)

“Please...”

Chung Myung and Im So-Byeong were fighting while swearing, and the Five Swords pretended to stop them while subtly blowing air.

The chaotic scene brought more than just confusion to Tang Gunak, who was accustomed to the strict rules of the Sichuan Tang family.

‘Why is Mount Hua so...’

“Uh? Soso?”

Why was she there?

Seeing Tang Soso sticking close behind Chung Myung’s shoulder and cursing at Im So-Byeong, something felt like it was crawling up from deep inside his stomach.

This damned Mount Hua...

Thud!

“Anyway, that is what happened.”

Chung Myung hit the ground hard.

“You have to pull your feet out.”

“Well, that is true.”

“... No, why is the Green Forest King suddenly agreeing to this?”

Im So-Byeong shrugged.

“That is not wrong. We cannot keep continuing like this.”

“... why?”

“Isn’t the reason obvious?”

Whack!

Im So-Byeong, who had spread out the fan, said with a calm face.

“There is no way Jang Ilso can just drool over the north of the river forever.”

“....”

“We will move soon, one way or another.”

“Isn’t that something we all know?”

“Right. So, of course, we have to get out.”

Chung Myung, who had been listening, intervened with Im So-Byeong.

“I don’t know what that insane guy is trying to do, but there is one certain thing. He has to cross the Yangtze River to get to the north.”

“Right.”

“Then what do you think will happen? The Tang family that was guarding the Yangtze River will be the first shield?”

“... Hmm.”

Tang Gunak nodded quietly.

He was no idiot. He knew that if the Four Precepts Alliance crossed the Yangtze River, the main force of the Sichuan Tang family would be the first to greet them.

But...

“A shield...”

Tang Gunak looked at Chung Myung quietly.

“If it were to be the shield of the Nine Great Sects, then, of course, I would refuse it flat out.”

“Yes.”

“But if it was to shield the common people?”

“....”

Tang Gunak slowly shook his head.

“The reason the Tang family has guarded the Yangtze River until now was not to help the Nine Great Sects. It was simply for the sake of the common people.”

To be honest, that wasn’t his intention at first.

He simply thought it was a chance to make the name of the Heavenly Friends Alliance widely known and build up a reputation, while the Nine Great Sects’ reputation was failing.

But while living with them and traveling the Yangtze River on his own, Tang Gunak had felt so many things. He couldn’t let the land and the people he had protected until then fall into the hands of the Four Precepts Alliance and suffer.

“Since we do not know what the Four Precepts Alliance will do to them, can we step down for that reason?”

Chung Myung frowned slightly.

“Wasn’t the interest of the family the priority in the Sichuan Tang family?”

“That is true. But...”

Tang Gunak’s gaze was fixed straight on Chung Myung.

“That is a story from our time in Kangho. The Sichuan Tang family doesn’t fight over commerce and profits. At least as long as I am the head of the Tang family.”

His tone was more resolute than ever.

Chung Myung’s lips curled up slightly.

It wasn’t the answer he wanted. But maybe it was an answer he liked even more.

‘I don’t know what happened in the past three years...’

It seemed that Tang Gunak had also felt a lot since leaving Sichuan.

“Well, right, it is what the lord said. But...”

“But?”

“The problem is that while trying to help the commoners, you end up being a shield for the Nine Sects.”

“Isn’t that something we have to endure?”

Chung Myung’s lips curled further.

“Even if the Nine Great Sects don’t come to help until the entire Tang family is destroyed?”

“....”

At those words, Tang Gunak fell silent.

Tang Gunak, who seemed to be thinking of many things, asked Chung Myung.

“You think that is possible?”

“Yes.”

“... if we cannot stop the Four Precepts Alliance, it will not just be the Heavenly Friends Alliance that perishes. Do you think the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families will sit by and watch the Sichuan Tang family fall to the Four Sects Alliance?”

“Yes.”

Tang Gunak was momentarily speechless at Chung Myung’s calm and simple answer.

This indicated a difference in perspective between Tang Gunak and Chung Myung regarding the Nine Great Sects.

Chung Myung opened his mouth slightly.

“I think there is something you misunderstand.”

“... What do you mean?”

“The Sichuan Tang family is no longer a comrade of the Nine Great Sects. You are their enemy.”

“Enemy?”

“Yes.”

Chung Myung nodded and added.

“From the perspective of the Nine Great Sects, there is no difference between fighting alongside the Sichuan Tang family or fighting after the Sichuan Tang family is used as a shield to exhaust the Four Precepts Alliance. Who would fight together across the border when there is another enemy on the way? They would just watch until one side is destroyed.”

“...”

Tang Gunak’s expression changed several times in a short period. He was silent for a moment and then shook his head.

“But that analogy doesn’t fit. There are people of the nation outside the border. Aren’t there civilians living north of the Yangtze who need protection?”

It was said that the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families would move for the sake of the people, not the Tang family. But upon hearing it, Chung Myung smiled wryly.

As if he had been waiting for Tang Gunak to say it.

“You’ve heard the story of Shaanxi, right?”

“....”

“Do you think those bastards didn’t have the strength to stop the Evil Sect people and just sat by?”

Tang Gunak couldn’t find the words to speak. After hesitating for a long time and moving his lips, he finally let out a long sigh.

“... I guess not.”

If Mount Hua hadn’t opened their Closed Gate and arrived in time... no, if Mount Hua had been even a little late in receiving the news, chaos would have spread.

‘No.’

Tang Gunak bit his lip tightly.

Xi’an wasn’t burning, but chaos had already begun to spread. Hadn’t two villages already been ravaged by those Evil Sect people? The ones who stood by and watched were clearly Shaolin. To be precise, the Nine Great Sects.

They had already pushed the common people into the clutches of the Evil Sect once. Why wouldn’t they do the same thing again?

Chung Myung nodded as if he could read Tang Gunak’s thoughts and drove the point home.

“If the Four Precepts Alliance moves north, how do you think the Nine Great Sects will react? They will probably wait for support, fight for a better position, and just waste time with pointless discussions?”

“Until the Sichuan Tang family completely falls to the Four Sects Alliance?”

“No.”

A bitter smile appeared on Chung Myung’s lips.

“Unable to stand by and watch the Tang family fight alone and fall apart, the entire Heavenly Friends Alliance, including Mount Hua, rushes to the Yangtze River only to get caught up in the war.”

“...”

“Then how much easier would it be for the Nine Sects? Mount Hua, the Tang family, and Green Forest, who were thorns on their side, would join hands and help the Four Precepts Alliance in a mutual destruction of both sides. They would leisurely wait for the Four Precepts Alliance to win over the Heavenly Friends Alliance and then try to clean up the Four Precepts Alliance, which had lost all its strength. Talking about noble sacrifices and all.”

A mocking smile formed on Chung Myung’s lips.

How did he know?

‘Because I have already been through it once.’

It wasn’t like Chung Myung or Chung Mun didn’t know and suffered as ignorants. Even if they knew it, they had no choice but to risk it all. If they had backed down, everything would have crumbled.

Right. He didn’t regret the past.

But...

“I will not suffer the same thing twice. You fucking bastards.”

Chung Myung gritted his teeth and spoke.

“The abbot isn’t a bad guy.”

When the words “abbot” and “bad” came out simultaneously, Hae Yeon, who was in the corner, flinched and looked at Chung Myung. However, he was in a situation where he could only keep his mouth shut.

“Even Venerable Heo Do isn’t a bad man. He just has one thing in his head.”

“... What is it?”

“The most important thing is the benefit of his sect.”

At Chung Myung’s words, Tang Gunak closed his eyes.

That wasn’t much different from what Tang Gunak had been thinking until a while ago. That iswas why he could understand clearly that there was a high chance Chung Myung’s words could come true.

Chung Myung was filled with a sense of pride.

Times have changed, but their thinking hasn’t. When Mount Hua climbed the Ten Thousand Mountains in the past when the Demonic Sect attacked Mount Hua, and when Mount Hua miserably fell.

Even the actions of Venerable Heo Do and the Shaolin that they saw during the Yangtze River incident were no different. All of them were just rational decisions that prioritized the interests and safety of their sects.

“I don’t think of it as wrong. Everyone is the same, you know.”

“...”

“But...”

Chung Myung shrugged.

“If that is what you are saying, we cannot help but think about our interests, right?”

“... Right.”

Tang Gunak nodded.

“No matter how right that is, if we just back out like this, the people of the Yangtze River will turn away from the Heavenly Friends Alliance. Aren’t people innately cold-hearted? I am worried that the hard work we have put in over the past three years will be in vain.”

At that moment, Chung Myung’s mouth curled up into a wicked smile.

“Vain?”

“Yes, vain.”

Chung Myung quietly looked at Tang Gunak and opened his mouth.

“Do you know what I hate the most?”

Tang Gunak spoke with an extremely serious face.

“Southern Edge.”

“....”

At that moment, Chung Myung’s brain froze. His mouth, which had been spitting words like a cannon, suddenly stopped and hung open.

“Is it not?”

“That... well...”

Chung Myung’s face was conflicted. Of course, he should have answered ‘no!’ Only then could he continue with what he wanted to say.

But...

‘How on earth can I say with my mouth that I don’t hate Southern Edge?’

Uh? It wasn’t that he didn’t hate them, but that he hated them less.

That’s it, those bastards!

The only thing he hated more than the Southern Edge in the world was the Demonic Cult!

Chung Myung closed his mouth as if he was speechless. His eyes began to dart left and right.

Baek Cheon, who couldn’t stand it any longer, reached out a helping hand to Chung Myung.

“Lord, right now, this guy is not asking who you hate the most, but what situation you hate the most.”

“R-right!”

Chung Myung quickly agreed and nodded.

Ah, right, even Dong-Ryeong could be of help when you feed him right!

“The situation?”

“Yes! The situation!”

“... losing to Southern Edge?”

“....”

At that moment, Tang Gunak felt as if he saw Chung Myung’s face crack like dried wood.

It wasn’t just Chung Myung who was shocked; even the Five Swords, who were silently listening from the side, shuddered.

“Woah, this pisses me off too.”

“I don’t even want to imagine that.”

“I would rather bite my tongue and die.”

Baek Cheon, who added those last words, seemed ready to draw his sword and run to attack Southern Edge at any moment.

Even the elders and Sect leader nodded as if agreeing with the words.

“Ugh, uh... that is definitely the most frustrating thing...”

Chung Myung shook his head.

“Anyway!”

“... why yell so suddenly!”

“I hate it when someone touches my rice bowl when someone else eats all the work I put in! I absolutely cannot stand it!”

“If it is as you say, shouldn’t we be protecting the Yangtze River even more now?”

“But there is just one thing I hate more than that.”

“...What is that?”

Chung Myung stared at Tang Gunak silently and slowly opened his mouth.

“Being obsessed with the illusions of profit and justification and losing those who we shouldn’t lose to.”

“....”

“I don’t care if I lose anything to avoid that.”

Tang Gunak closed his mouth.

There wasn’t a trace of a smile on Chung Myung’s face. He could tell that what he had said earlier was the utmost sincerity.

‘Those who shouldn’t be lost to...’

Even after hearing those words, he couldn’t be stubborn.

“...I understand.”

Their gazes met in the air. Tang Gunak nodded as if he had made up his mind.

“I don’t know if this decision is right or wrong. But...”

His true feelings, unadorned, flowed out calmly.

“I just trust you.”

A soft smile appeared on Tang Gunak’s lips.

Chapter 906: Let A Bite Of This Now! (1)

When it seemed like the decision had been made, Im So-Byeong finally spoke.

“There is no need to take it so seriously.”

“Uh?”

When Chung Myung asked again, Im So-Byeong smiled slyly.

“People are naturally grateful if you help them once, but if you help them ten times, they take it for granted. And they confidently expect you to help them the eleventh time.”

“....”

“And if you don’t give them that eleventh help, they curse and criticize you.”

“.... What do you take people as?”

“It is natural, it is natural. Isn’t that how everyone is?”

Im So-Byeong looked around as if asking for agreement. But all he met with were critical and cold eyes.

“You cannot fool your origins, after all.”

“I wonder if anyone would say he is not a bandit.”

“... Evil Sect is Evil Sect.”

Im So-Byeong’s eyes trembled.

“A-am I the only one? Just me? Am I the only one who is trash here?”

“Yes.”

“Yep.”

“Seems like it.”

Im So-Byeong’s head dropped.

He could faintly hear the mumbling, ‘These damned Taoists, I cannot get along with them. They act more like bandits, but at such times they...’ but the disciples of Mount Hua ignored him as if they couldn’t hear him.

“So what?”

“Ahem.”

Im So-Byeong cleared his throat and continued.

“The important thing is the method, the method! You don’t just step down; you make a declaration and step down.”

“... declaration?”

“Yes. What happens if you don’t help them the eleventh time?”

“You said we would get cursed.”

“Right! If you don’t help, you get cursed. But what if we change the technique? If we boldly declare that we won’t help them from now on and step down, what happens?”

“... wouldn’t we still be cursed at?”

“Hahaha. You are so narrow-minded. You are wrong!”

“Then?”

Im So-Byeong answered with a big smile.

“They will curse you even harder. It is like wanting to kill...”

“Ya, you moron!”

Bam!

Chung Myung flew through the air and kicked Im So-Byeong in the chest. When Im So-Byeong screamed and fell, the Five Swords and Tang Gunak were shocked and quickly subdued Chung Myung, dragging him away.

Chung Myung, whose eyes were wide open, growled like a dog that had found a thief.

“No, you are just being a bit too much! Why are you provoking people to make them angry? What kind of plan is that? Let us see how strong the heads of the bandits are!”

“Calm down, Chung Myung!”

“He is the Green Forest King! You cannot kick him!”

“Come on now, Chung Myung. He is a bastard, but he still has a position. We need to treat him well.”

But the Green Forest King was the Green Forest King. Im So-Byeong, who was kicked, bounced back up like a dragon and stood up.

“Oh?”

At that sight, the disciples of Mount Hua nodded their heads as if to say, ‘He is indeed the Green Forest King.’

“Wait a minute... what is... cough! Cough! Cough... why is this cough... cough!”

Im So-Byeong, who was coughing loudly while his body shook, suddenly spat red blood from his mouth.

“Mom!”

“Eik!”

After everyone’s shocked scream, silence fell over.

Im So-Byeong quietly looked down at the blood soaking the ground. Then he weakly turned to Chung Myung, making Chung Myung grumble.

“What kind of man is he? Coughing up blood because he got kicked once?”

‘Is he even human?’

‘Devil.’

‘Why did something like that step into this world... I would rather consider Jang Ilso as kind, Jang Ilso!’

“... are you alright, Green Forest King?”

Im So-Byeong nodded feebly at Tang Gunak’s question.

“... Yes, don’t worry. Don’t wor... cough cough!”

“....”

“More than that... till the end of the... cough....”

Chung Myung nodded absentmindedly.

“Right. Well, try it.”

“How far... did I say? That... cough. Well, they will hate you. But cough! Just let them know we are not the ones they should hate.”

“... They would turn the arrow towards the Nine Great Sects?”

“Huh. You speak such odd things. Why are... cough! Cough! Ahem! Turn the arrow. That is expected.”

Im So-Byeong snickered.

The sight of him laughing while bleeding was not victorious but rather unsettling, yet no one pointed it out.

“It is better. Since Mount Hua lifted the Closed Gate in Shaanxi and defeated the Xi’an raiders, we now have a justification.”

“Justification?”

“Yes. What better justification is there than returning to protect your land? Since the Evil Sect left Hubei and Hunan and is extending its reach to Shaanxi and Sichuan, you can just say that you are returning to your sect to protect the place you are meant to.”

“....”

Chung Myung looked at Im So-Byeong with an ‘oh?’ face.

“Then the direction of the resentment will change. You are leaving not because it is difficult or annoying but to protect what you have to. Then who will the remaining people look at?”

“Who?”

“Tsk tsk. Instead of people going a long way to protect other civilians, wouldn’t they see those Taoist bastards who are playing the role of a recluse by staying in their sects? While everyone outside is having a hard time, the Taoist bastards who are behind the gate cannot even show their noses... no, put their fist down. Put that fist down! Cough! Cough! N-nose blood!”

When Im So-Byeong quickly pushed into the blood-soaked front, Chung Myung reluctantly put down his slightly clenched fist. However, his eyes were still filled with a grim expression.

Swish!

Im So-Byeong spread out his blood-soaked fan.

“You just need to remind the people that originally, Kangho was guarded by another sect, Sichuan was for the Tang family, Shaanxi for Mount Hua, and Hubei was under Wudang. Then, naturally, they will remember once again that the Tang family left the place they had to look after and helped others.”

Hyun Young nodded as if he liked what Im So-Byeong was saying.

“Indeed, there is such a way. It would be much better than just leaving.”

However, Baek Cheon seemed to have a slightly different idea.

“But it isn’t like the real Evil Sect people were in Sichuan, so aren’t we just pretending?”

“Hehe. What do you mean, Taoist Baek Cheon? Sichuan already has the Evil Sect within it.”

“Uh?”

Baek Cheon was shocked, and even more surprised was Tang Gunak, who asked Im So-Byeong.

“Is that true?”

“Yes. I don’t know exactly who they are. Still, they are definitely members of the Evil Sect hiding in various parts of the Sichuan mountains, threatening the lives of those who travel around.”

If the Evil Sect people were hiding in various parts of the mountains and threatening those who come and go...

Uh... then what did you usually call those people...

“... bandits?”

“Ehh!”

Im So-Byeong waved his fan.

“An unknown Evil Sect. An unknown one! Anyway, those Evil Sect guys will cause trouble soon. They will not kill anyone, coincidentally. Very coincidentally!”

Baek Cheon’s mouth dropped open.

So, in order to give the Tang family an excuse to leave, The Green Forest would disguise itself as an Evil Sect and cause an accident in Sichuan?

“No, would they be fooled by that? Of course, there is a different...”

“Nope.”

“Nope.”

“... Yes. It won’t work. Right.”

If the Evil Sect people were in the mountains, they were bandits; if they were in the river, they were called pirates, and others were thugs.

They didn’t even have the word ‘bandits’ written on their faces, so how would one know which side the guy belonged to if the clothes were changed?

“Surely...”

Tang Gunak nodded as if he had a point.

If the rumor that an Evil Sect caused a disturbance in Sichuan, big or small, spread correctly, no one would begrudge the Tang family to return to face the Evil Sect.

After all, wasn’t it the Sichuan Tang family? The Sichuan Tang family was going to protect Sichuan, so who could ask them to leave Sichuan alone and continue to protect the rest of the people?

Everyone understood and dazedly accepted, so Im So-Byeong opened his mouth in triumph.

“Hahahah. It seems like you understand. Do you know what this is called?”

“Conspiracy.”

“Despicable task.”

“Dirty.”

“A s-scam! A scam! It is a scam. It isn’t dirty, you Taoist bastards!”

The lonely bandits’ bloody cries, as always, were not heard by the Taoist bastards.

Only Chung Myung looked at Im So-Byeong as if he was impressed.

“I saved a dying man, but I cursed him a lot, saying he was of no use. Now he has many uses.”

“Useless! I almost broke my back making money on Plum Blossom Island!”

“You would have broken your wrist trying to steal.”

“... If I had known this would happen, I really should have just stolen it.”

“If you had, it would have been your neck and not the wrist.”

“...”

Chung Myung also thought about spreading rumors through the Beggars Union or Green Forest to ease the responsibility. Still, he couldn’t think of making Green Forest disguise as the Evil Sect people and cause trouble in Sichuan.

“Evil Sect is definitely Evil Sect. The level of plotting is different. Is it because of what he used to do?”

“... I said it was a clever plan.”

“Tsk tsk. The same water becomes milk when a cow drinks it and poison when a snake drinks it. The same plan is clever when it comes from the Justice faction’s head and conspiracy when it comes from the Evil Sect’s head.”

“N-no, that is...”

“If you feel wronged, just become one of the Justice faction. Who would want to be a bandit?”

“....”

Im So-Byeong felt his eyes getting blurry and closed his mouth.

Baek Cheon whispered quietly to Chung Myung.

“But... isn’t that a bit too much?”

“What is too much? Even if you freeze and die, what money do you even have? You should at least be grateful for this.”

“...”

When he heard that, it felt painfully true...

At that moment, Tang Gunak, who thought that if he left the situation as it was, it would end with Chung Myung verbally beating Im So-Byeong, quietly intervened.

“If that happens, we will have a reason to get the Tang family out of the Yangtze River. But I am still concerned for the civilians who suffer there.”

To that, Chung Myung responded.

“Do not worry. We have a way.”

“Uh? There is a way?”

“Even if it is said that we cannot help it, some of the people living on Yangtze River island will move to Sichuan if you give them some support.”

Tang Gunak looked at Chung Myung, a bit shocked.

“To Sichuan?”

“Yes.”

Tang Gunak gave a light nod.

“You have to give them a chance. If they move to Sichuan, not just the Tang family but also Mount Hua will be able to support them. The money we have accumulated over the past three years is overflowing.”

This time, Im So-Byeong clapped his hands as if he was impressed.

“There is definitely an overflow of money. And even if you give them a chance, there probably won’t be many people who actually want to move, so it won’t cost much. Hehe. The idea of anticipating all this and making a fuss about it. The devil will even cry....”

“But, this bastard, the more he speaks!”

“Wait, Chung Myung!”

“If you hit him again, he will die!”

After a brief pause, Chung Myung finally calmed down and opened his mouth.

“That way, I will feel better.”

“Hmmm.”

Tang Gunak looked at Hyun Jong as if asking for permission. Then Hyun Jong smiled brightly and nodded.

“If the family lord cares for common people like that, how could someone who walks the path of Taoism be obsessed with such wealth?”

“... he is obsessed.”

“He should be obsessed.”

“We were beggars, sect leader.”

“B-be quiet, you people! Don’t we have guests here?”

Hyun Jong, who was furious with a red face, coughed loudly and spoke again politely.

“So do as you wish.”

“... thank you, alliance lord.”

When Tang Gunak nodded, Im So-Byeong smiled brightly.

“Then we will withdraw from Plum Blossom Island in step with the Tang family.”

“Uh? Plum Blossom Island?”

“Plum Blossom Island is in front of the Tang family. If war breaks out, that would be the first to fall.”

“N-no, that is okay, but...”

Chung Myung licked his lips.

“It is a shame to lose all the money I haven’t earned yet... should we stay a bit longer?”

“... people’s lives are most important now.”

“Right. But...”

Chung Myung blew on the finger he used to dig into his ear.

“Are bandits treated like humans these days? It wasn’t like that in the past.”

Im So-Byeong was speechless for a moment and looked at Chung Myung with a face that said, ‘Is this man really a human?’ but Chung Myung was still looking sullen.

“He won’t be much help to the world even if he lives, so why don’t we just earn money through him?”

“Give up now!”

“Don’t make hasty decisions!”

“Money is money, but shouldn’t we stay alive first?”

“Anyway, these days, people don’t understand how precious money is! Tsk tsk, it wasn’t like that back then.”

Were there only devils in the world back then?

“Ahem. I think that is right, Green Forest King.”

“Thank you, lord.”

Up until then, Im So-Byeong had thought that Chung Myung was the real leader of the Heavenly Friends Alliance. But, at this moment, he was grateful to heaven that Hyun Jong, not Chung Myung, was the alliance leader.
