If that devil had been sitting in the position of Alliance leader, the world would... no, the world may not know, but the Green Forest might have been burned down.

When things were roughly sorted, Chung Myung began to snicker.

“I have laid the groundwork so that bastard can start moving.”

“... laid the groundwork?”

“No, well. It is nothing. Hehehehe.”

Everyone trembled with nervousness as they watched him laugh with the most excited look without knowing any details.

‘So you are saying that you dared to screw us over? You bastard abbot!’

Chung Myung’s mouth began to twist.

“I have been away for three years, so I guess he has forgotten who I am.”

“...”

“Take a bite of this now. Hehehehe! Hehehehe!”

“...”

Finally, looking at Chung Myung, who was starting to fall into insanity, Jo Gul asked Baek Cheon quietly.

“... why is that guy at it again?”

“What are you going to say? It seems like he is plotting something inside.”

What if it was the same water? If a cow drank it, it was milk, and if a snake drank it, it was poison?

‘Then that guy is a snake. And a vicious one at that.’

The evil eyes of the giant evil dragon turned south.

Far, far away, to the Yangtze River, where chaos that would shake the world would turn to ground.

Chapter 907: Let A Bite Of This Now! (2)

There was a strange aspect to perception.

Surprisingly, people did not feel peace as peace and did not feel chaos as chaos.

In situations where peace continued, they did not perceive the tranquility as special. In situations where chaos persisted, they did not find the urgency unusual.

It had been three years since the Yangtze River War.

At first, those who were groaning from the great war that started from the Yangtze River began to accept such situations as a normal part of daily life.

Even in chaos, there was stability.

In the midst of the chaos caused by the conflict between the Evil Sects and the Nine Great Sects and the existence of many sects, people were somehow building stability in their lives.

That was until a rumor suddenly spread like a footnote across the Yangtze River basin.

“The Tang family is withdrawing from Hubei?”

The man who heard that suddenly got up with wide eyes.

“What the heck is that supposed to mean? The Tang family is stepping back?”

“It is exactly that. They are heading back to Sichuan.”

“Then what should we do?”

The faces of those gathered in groups of three or five turned pale.

Those who cultivated the land north of the Yangtze River could not help but know how much work the Sichuan Tang had done over the past few years. If they had not guarded the place, some of those there would certainly not be alive.

But when such a party suddenly withdrew from the Yangtze River, he couldn’t help but be shocked.

“W-why so suddenly?”

“It seems that the Evil Sect appeared in Sichuan.”

“What? In Sichuan?”

“Right. Didn’t the Evil Sect run rampant in Sichuan a while ago, burning down houses and plundering property?”

“Now, even in Sichuan...”

“And not long ago, the Evil Sect bastards stirred up Shaanxi again. Now Shaanxi and Sichuan are no longer safe places.”

“Ah...”

The faces of those who spoke showed a regret that could not be expressed in words.

As the name suggests, the Sichuan Tang Family were the stewards of Sichuan. If the Evil Sect was heading to Sichuan, then the Tang family should naturally return to protect Sichuan.

“... I am worried about what will happen if the Tang family leaves....”

“That is why I am saying....”

“Is the Tang family really that powerless? Even if Sichuan is in trouble, it isn’t enough reason to completely withdraw them....”

“What is this guy talking about!”

One of the people listening turned red with anger.

“It is the Sichuan Tang family! The Sichuan Tang family! They have no ties to Hubei but haven’t they protected us for the past few years without receiving any compensation for it?”

“W-well, that is true....”

“Then what? Their house is on fire, and you want them to abandon it and protect us? Is that something a person who has been wearing soft clothes is supposed to even say?”

“I was just saying that I was so sad, just that. Why are you so upset....”

The one who spoke became dispirited and shrugged his shoulders.

“Right! Isn’t there a separate place that should be responsible for those damned Evil Sect guys?”

At those words, the name of a sect came to everyone’s mind. The sect had been their pride and joy until the Yangtze River war happened.

“Even the Tang family would travel a thousand kilometers to get back when there was a problem in their own town, but when the people are suffering in Hubei like this, where are the goddamned Wudang, and what are they up to?”

“... who doesn’t know that? Isn’t it because Wudang is Wudang that they don’t show their faces?”

“Then we should head to the Wudang and protest! Honestly, what did the Wudang do when they decided to sit silently and do nothing? Moreover, I heard that even Mount Hua broke down their Sealed Gate to rescue Shaanxi from the Evil Sects.”

“Mount Hua did?”

“Yes!”

The faces of those who heard the name Mount Hua changed.

For the people of Hubei, the name of Mount Hua brought extreme confusion.

It was a forgotten Taoist sect, not comparable to the Wudang sect that once represented Hubei. However, after the Yangtze River incident, it was the only sect that fought against the Evil Sect.

Now, three years later, Mount Hua’s position was somewhere in the middle.

“Mount Hua broke their Closed Gate and came out...”

“Isn’t that what I’m saying? To be honest, Mount Hua and Wudang went under Closed Gate at the same time, right?”

“It’s different. Definitely different.”

Mount Hua closed their Gate to gain power for the sect, but Wudang couldn’t handle the criticism and ran away.

“If Mount Hua had been active in the murim world for the past three years, it would have received all kinds of praise. Wasn’t Mount Hua the only sect that protected its pride during the damned Yangtze River incident? Yet, the sect that refused to do that and closed the Gate, claiming it lacked strength, is our sect! Mount Hua!”

“... right. That is a good sect.”

“Even that sect is opening the Closed Gate and coming out to protect its people in trouble, but the damn Wudang doesn’t care if the people in Hubei live or die. Ugh, damn!”

The fear that the Tang family was leaving began to turn into criticism of Wudang.

Actually, anyone with sense would naturally say it.

“Where is Wudang? What on earth are the small and medium sects doing in Hubei now? They used to brag about being a sub-clan under Wudang! If there is no Wudang, do the sub-clans have to sit and suck their thumbs? They say there were sub-clans of Mount Hua in Shaanxi who fought against the Evil Sect before Mount Hua arrived!”

“Even the sub-clans of Southern Edge went there and fought with their lives on the line.”

“The Wudang is...”

The thing that stimulated people the most was anxiety.

The anxiety over the absence of the Tang family potentially worsening the chaos was causing unrest in Hubei.

“I heard that the people of Gyun County have turned to Wudang. Let us wait a bit longer. If they have any shame, they will surely open the gates and come out.”

“... if they had any shame, would they have closed the gates on us?”

“Ugh, damn it! This is so frustrating!”

The news of the withdrawal of the Sichuan Tang family was enough to turn the Yangtze River upside down. But there was another place facing its own crisis.

“What? What did you say?”

“... Plum Blossom Island is being abandoned.”

The color drained from the face of Sima Gong, the lead merchant of the Great Blessings Merchant Union.

“Plum Blossom Island?”

“... Yes, lord, Plum Blossom Island.”

“N-No! Why is Plum Blossom Island, which was doing so well, being abandoned? Did I hear something wrong?”

“No, the rumor spreading right now is the same. They say they will receive shipments until tomorrow and no more after that.”

“T-They are doing final maintenance? Is that all?”

“... It looks like they are going to withdraw entirely.”

“No! Why are they withdrawing, then? They have been raking in money! And what about the facilities built there? What about them?”

“It looks like they are going to throw them all away.”

“... What the hell....”

Sima Gong mumbled in a bewildered tone.

How much effort did the Heavenly Friends Alliance put into Plum Blossom Island and the adjacent dock? What used to be nothing more than a vast plain has now become a huge city that rivals any place in Hubei.

And yet, they were going to completely withdraw from the place?

‘Are they in their right mind?’

With common sense, it was something he couldn’t understand.

He was a merchant, so he couldn’t understand the thoughts of others, but no matter what, it was like...

“No, if that is the case, you shouldn’t have started a business here! They could make more money, but they decide to close it down? Have they lost it?”

Sima Gong couldn’t stand it anymore and jumped up from his place.

“L-lord? What are you going to do...”

“I have to see it with my own eyes! I cannot believe it!”

Sima Gong ran out.

Fortunately, the residence where he was staying was not too far from Plum Blossom Island. Not only the Great Blessing Merchant Union but also many merchants had established branches near Plum Blossom Island to ensure smooth shipping and had merchants preside over them.

When he arrived at the dock, gasping for air, he saw many people who had already gathered after hearing the same news.

“Island Lord! What is happening here?”

“Is Mount Hua really withdrawing from Plum Blossom Island?”

“Then what are we going to do? If this happens, we will all starve to death!”

Surrounded by the crowd, Im So-Byeong let out a deep sigh.

‘Damn it.’

He had run all the way to Shaanxi and was chased away without even resting, and today was the day he returned to Plum Blossom Island. But as soon as he returned, he was surrounded by people and tormented, so he couldn’t help but cry.

No, not this.

Maybe he was a little happy. After being tormented by those damned people who only considered him a bandit, he was still with those who treated him like a fugitive.

“Everyone, please calm down.”

Im So-Byeong opened his mouth with a hollow cough.

“Is it really true? About Plum Blossom Island?”

“It is true. It is unfortunate, but Plum Blossom Island will be closing down its business starting tomorrow.”

“Then what are we supposed to do?”

“Without Plum Blossom Island, we cannot transport the goods to the South! Those thugs and bandits will run wild!”

“Mount Hua! What is Mount Hua saying? Is this the intention of Mount Hua?”

“Now, now, calm down, please.”

Im So-Byeong waved his hand and stopped the commotion.

“I also want to help out with the business, but... unfortunately, the movements in the South are rather odd. Don’t you all know the situation by now?”

Those who had been opposing him all that time went silent.

They also knew that the Four Precepts Alliance had finished their internal reorganization. They were merchants. No matter how vicious the Four Precepts Alliance was, they wouldn’t try to kill the merchants.

But Plum Blossom Island was Mount Hua’s project. It was a place they would definitely target when they crossed the Yangtze River.

“Then really...”

Im So-Byeong nodded.

“But you don’t have to worry too much. Isn’t this Plum Blossom Island? Even if it isn’t Mount Hua, someone will surely try to continue the plan.”

“Ughh. That is right, but...”

“Then doesn’t the Heavenly Friends Alliance know anything about that?”

“Well.”

Im So-Byeong smiled wryly.

“It is pointless to talk about it when they are leaving. But....”

“But?”

“Everything flows naturally, right? The actual owner has to return to their place.”

“If you say the original owner....”

“Well. I think I have said everything I wanted to say. Even if it is night, we won’t rest and will make sure the goods are delivered before the sun sets tomorrow, so if you have any unloaded goods, bring them in quickly!”

The merchants’ eyes shook as they heard those words.

“M-Move fast!”

“Damn it! The warehouse is still full of goods!”

“Now wait a minute, even if we move the goods, how are we going to bring back the exchanged goods?”

“We will think about it later!”

In an instant, they were caught up in confusion and couldn’t even think of asking Im So-Byeong anything else.

In the midst of that chaos, Im So-Byeong just snickered.

‘The Evil Sect’s conspiracy?’

Don’t even say it as a joke.

In terms of plotting, he was nowhere near the toes of that devil.

Im So-Byeong took in the large city spread out before him with both eyes. It was a commercial city where the wealthiest merchants in the world would gather, a city that anyone in the world would covet.

‘Is there a greater bait in the world than this?’

Im So-Byeong wasn’t sure whether Chung Myung had really planned this three years ago or if things had just happened this way. He had his suspicions, but no evidence.

In any case, regardless of the intentions, this land was sure to become the epicenter of a tornado that would sweep up countless people.

“... such a shame though.”

Im So-Byeong licked his lips and gestured to his subordinates.

“Ship the money in advance. We have to leave tomorrow.”

“Yes! Green Forest... no, Island Lord!”

“Tsk!”

Im So-Byeong opened his fan and waved it in front of his face.

“It is a shame that the name’ Island Lord’ will vanish, but I guess it is for the best.”

His gaze turned to the other side of the river.

“I think it is going to be more fun from now on.”

A soft laugh drifted through the confused shouts of the people.

Chapter 908: Let A Bite Of This Now! (3)

“Kuaak., Ga-Myung, honey... honey water, bring it...”

“... Alliance lord.”

A deep sigh escaped Ho Ga-Myung’s mouth.

This man was so skilled in martial arts, so why didn’t he use his internal qi to dispel the toxins of alcohol? Why was he acting so foolishly?

“... Honey water.”

“Yes.”

Instead of reprimanding Jang Ilso, Ho Ga-Myung instructed a maid to bring honey water. He received the honey water from the maid and even infused a bit of ice qi into it so that Jang Ilso could have a cold drink.

“Here it is. Drink it.”

“Kuaak.”

Jang Ilso took the bowl that Ho Ga-Myung offered with a contorted face and gulped it down.

Then he shouted in a displeased tone.

“It is cold!”

“....”

The words, “I have to hit him, even if he is the lord,” came to the tip of his tongue, but Ho Ga-Myung swallowed them with patience.

In truth, he didn’t want to criticize Jang Ilso anymore.

Because he had seen with his own eyes what Jang Ilso had done... no, what he had accomplished.

‘As if.’

No one could have imagined that Jang Ilso would reach the heights he had now. He was someone that even the world-class Ho Ga-Myung would have never envisioned in his dreams.

‘So different.’

This was exactly what they meant when they said that imagination could not match reality.

This was the difference between a monarch and a leader.

If one focused on accurately and meticulously planned tasks, Ho Ga-Myung was clearly superior to Jang Ilso. However, the one who ascended to the throne drew the big picture that a general could never dare to imagine.

Jang Ilso was the one who casually committed to dreamlike stories and brought them into reality.

Ho Ga-Myung knew his lord was that good... but he was still dissatisfied.

“... I understand why you are like that, but no matter how much I think about it, there is no need for the alliance lord to deal with such bastards one by one.”

“Tsk tsk. Even if you say that every day.”

Jang Ilso clicked his tongue at Ho Ga-Myung’s words.

Then he quickly threw away the blanket he was covering himself with, got down from the bed, and started walking slowly.

The servants quickly approached him, took off his bedding, and began to dress him up. They wet Jang Ilso’s body with a wet towel and wiped the moisture away with a dry towel. They put on his innerwear made of high-quality silk over the exposed upper body.

“Haven’t I told you this several times that the moment I neglect the little things, I end up as a pig? Do you want me to become a fat pig?”

Jang Ilso, who was used to being served by the maids, glanced at Ho Ga-Myung with eyes as sharp as blades.

That sword-like gaze was directed at Ho Ga-Myung.

Ho Ga-Myung flinched without realizing it. It was because he did not know the meaning of that gaze.

A clear reprimand.

It was a warning that Ho Ga-Myung’s remarks had exceeded the level that Jang Ilso could tolerate.

“This subordinate is foolish, Alliance Lord!”

Ho Ga-Myung bowed down right there.

Jang Ilso’s eyes, which had been silently glaring at Ho Ga-Myung, softened once again.

“Tsk tsk, Ga-Myung. Ga-Myung.”

“....”

“Why don’t you understand why I say it? Even so, this Alliance Lord is not a lowly man, so you probably don’t like the sight of me sharing a drink with lowly people and giggling and laughing.”

“... it is true that I had such feelings.”

“You are not wrong.”

The maid’s hand caressed Jang Ilso’s clothes.

“People need class. The level required for a warrior, the level required for a branch leader, and the level required for an Alliance Lord are all different.”

“I think so, too.”

“But that is just the attitude of someone who is satisfied with their position. If you are happy with your position, all that remains is a fall.”

Jang Ilso’s face contorted.

“If I had been content as an Alliance Lord, I would not have mixed drinks with those people. But if I want something bigger if I am still hungry for more!”

He covered his face with his hand as if grabbing it.

“Even if it is a cup mixed with their spit, you should know how to drink it down with a smile. Authority doesn’t come from attitude but from strength.”

“....”

“I...”

He paused for a moment and took his hand away from his face. A gentle smile appeared as if nothing had happened.

“You are getting stronger even at this moment, Ga-Myung. Should we stop a warrior from getting strong?”

“The Alliance Lord’s words are a hundred, no, a thousand times right!”

Ho Ga-Myung, who had been lost in thought, hit his head on the ground.

Jang Ilso frowned.

“Why are you hitting your head like that? It will hurt.”

“....”

“Get up. Get up now. I told you not to do that, but you really don’t listen. Tsk tsk.”

Ho Ga-Myung carefully got up.

Every time this happened, he was reminded of the fact that Jang Ilso was the lord.

Jang Ilso was not someone he could deal with easily. It was the same no matter what he did. It wasn’t Jang Ilso’s strength that showed that he was strong or that he was capable.

It was just because he was such a kind of person.

The maid slowly helped with Jang Ilso’s face. After lightly applying red powder to his white face and drawing on his eyebrows, the maid stepped away, and Jang Ilso began to apply the lip tint on his own.

“Anyway, so...”

Kwang!

“Alliance Lord!”

At that moment, the door suddenly burst open, and Jang Ilso’s finger, which was slowly moving over his lips, wavered at the shout.

“... tsk.”

A serious look of displeasure crossed his face.

Ho Ga-Myung, who noticed that killing intent, quickly shouted.

“Where do you think you are running into? You moron!”

“I am sorry! I apologize! This is urgent!”

“Shut you! You mor...!”

“... enough.”

Jang Ilso wiped the mark with a towel the maid handed him and turned his head to the person who rushed in. The killing intent in his eyes had already vanished.

“It must be something. What is happening?”

“Plum Blossom Island... no, the Green Forest and the pirates are retreating from the island on the river.”

“Where?”

“The island. The one the Mount Hua bastards captured...”

“... Plum Blossom Island.”

Jang Ilso lifted his flowing bangs with his fingers. Then a maid approached him, combed his hair back, and placed a crown on his head.

“They emptied Plum Blossom Island and retreated?”

“Yes! That is right. And the Sichuan Tang family also said they had let go of the Yangtze River and would return to Sichuan.”

Ho Ga-Myung asked with a shocked look.

“This suddenly?”

“... Judging from the fact that the news has reached us, I think they might have already withdrawn and left.”

“N-no, this is...”

Ho Ga-Myung closed his mouth. The information he had suddenly heard wasn’t being organized in his head.

‘What are they thinking now?’

He understood the situation.

If they crossed the Yangtze River, Plum Blossom Island and the Sichuan Tang family guarding the Yangtze River would be the most dangerous places, so it wouldn’t be strange to raid them now that the situation was resolved.

But what Ho Ga-Myung couldn’t understand was their opponent’s urgency.

‘We didn’t even move, though.’

That might be the case elsewhere. Those who were scared tended to rush into anything.

But the problem was that Mount Hua wasn’t the type to be scared.

Wasn’t Mount Hua the place where no one in the sect had any fear, where Mount Hua’s Sword Saint made one wonder what he ate to have lost his mind, alongside the other disciples?

Even in a situation where Wudang became afraid and surrendered, Mount Hua was the one who stood stubbornly and rushed at Jang Ilso and the Ten Thousand People clan.

That Mount Hua, leader of the Heavenly Friends Alliance, became scared and abandoned the Yangtze River? A place where huge profits were guaranteed just by being there?

‘It cannot be that.’

If it were Mount Hua, if it were the Heavenly Friends Alliance that he knew, they wouldn’t give up profits without a reason.

Then what was pushing them to do...

“Anyway...”

At that moment, Jang Ilso swept up a strand of hair that had fallen and clicked his tongue.

“That reckless brat, as soon as he got out of his cage, he stabbed someone in the side with a knife. Tsk tsk tsk.”

Ho Ga-Myung flinched at the sight of his face.

A strange expression appeared on his face—an expression that seemed extremely irritated, yet with an odd joy.

“Are you saying you won’t just play along? Right, that should happen. Then.”

After hearing Jang Ilso’s words, Ho Ga-Myung couldn’t hold back and asked.

“Who are you talking about?”

“Who could it be?”

“... Are you talking about Mount Hua’s Sword Saint this time?”

Jang Ilso didn’t answer. Ho Ga-Myung wasn’t the one to know that silence was affirmation.

But Ho Ga-Myung couldn’t simply accept the situation.

It was only natural that Jang Ilso existed in a realm Ho Ga-Myung didn’t understand. But how could someone like Mount Hua’s Sword Saint be on the same level as Jang Ilso?

“Alliance Lord. I want to speak about something.”

“Go ahead.”

“... I think the Alliance Lord is overestimating Mount Hua’s Sword Saint. He isn’t someone who can exchange thoughts with the Alliance Lord. Wasn’t that grandiose name of his, Sword Saint, given to him by us?”

“Why do you think so?”

“That little brat is...”

“Tsk tsk. Ga-Myung.”

“... Uh?”

“Do you know how old I was when I first appeared in murim?”

When Ho Ga-Myung didn’t answer, Jang Ilso said in amusement.

“Compared to that time, I am a little older now, a little more cautious. And incomparably stronger.”

“Yes, Alliance Lord.”

“But I have not become smarter.”

“...”

“That is how people are. Growth is ultimately about how much you can bring out of what you have. A tiger is a tiger even when it is a cub, and a dog is a dog even when it is fully grown. And it goes without saying for a dragon.”

Ho Ga-Myung bit his lip.

Jang Ilso’s words couldn’t be wrong. However, Ho Ga-Myung didn’t want to admit it.

Was it because of simple jealousy?

No.

The reason he was angry was not because Mount Hua’s Sword Saint was superior to him, but because he dared to encroach upon Jang Ilso’s existence. To him, that was no different from blasphemy against the lord.

“And even if what you said was true, it would be no different. What is important is not whether he is worthy of sharing the same level with me, but that the level he has reached is currently strangling me.”

“Strangling you...”

“Map!”

“Yes.”

The servants rushed over and brought out a large map. When they spread it out on the table, Jang Ilso’s long and slender fingers touched the middle of the Yangtze River.

“How is it?”

“... Uh?”

“This Plum Blossom Island too.”

At Jang Ilso’s words, Ho Ga-Myung, who had been looking at the plum blossoms once again, tilted his head.

“I don’t know. For a soldier, it would be a strategic point, but for a warrior, would it be that important? Moreover, we have taken complete control over the Yangtze River through the waters.”

“Tsk tsk. You are still thinking about fights alone.”

“Then...”

“Do you know how much material is flowing in and out of this place?”

Jang Ilso laughed.

“For the past three damn years, the amount of material flowing into the south through the Plum Blossom has skyrocketed. If this place gets blocked now, it means we are the ones who will be suffocated.”

“Ah...”

“You damn bastard.”

Jang Ilso gritted his teeth. But even then, the corners of his mouth curled up as if he was happy.

“It really hurts. It really.. really hurts.”

His blazing eyes turned to a distant place.

To a mountain in the far north that boasted rugged terrain.

Chapter 909: Let A Bite Of This Now! (4)

The Justice Faction led its disciples with harmony and values.

However, the Evil Sect despised empty platitudes. The only thing that could move them was profit.

Of course, Jang Ilso was currently in a situation where he dominated the Evil Sect with the strength he had, but there was clearly a limit. When food ran out, wasn’t it human nature to rebel against the emperor?

“... They are trying to cut off the food supply?”

Ho Ga-Myung, who understood the situation, bit his lip.

When he first considered giving the island, now called Plum Blossom Island, to Mount Hua, he hadn’t expected this kind of situation. At best, he thought it would be used as an element to divide the ties between the Nine Sects and the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

That crazy Mount Hua Sword Saint had made things worse by endlessly expanding the matter.

“But Alliance Lord, isn’t it strange?”

“Strange?”

“Yes. Plum Blossom Island is a place where the Yangtze River must be blocked by any means. If the pirates lift the block on the Yangtze River, isn’t that pointless? This couldn’t be a plan.”

“If you think logically, then yes.”

Jang Ilso snickered.

“If that pig of the Black Dragon King also has the thought of lifting the blockage on the Yangtze River, then.”

“... Uh?”

Ho Ga-Myung tilted his head, unable to understand.

“Aren’t the pirates suffering from financial difficulties because of the existence of Plum Blossom Island? That is why he had requested me to attack Plum Blossom Island several times.”

“That was when Plum Blossom Island belonged to someone else.”

Jang Ilso’s face twisted.

“That swordsman and the crazy man harassed that island and concentrated the logistics of the entire river in one place. Just by carrying and moving a moderate amount of goods, they made a huge amount of money, making the money the pirates made from plundering seem like small change.”

“That... is right.”

It was initially established by the Four Precepts Alliance. Still, it was their opponents’ capability that allowed it to exceed their expectations.

“What would you do if you were the Black Dragon King?”

Jang Ilso’s lips curled into a smirk.

“Would you try to eliminate that Plum Blossom Island and revert to the previous state? Or would you sustain the current situation and attempt to claim Plum Blossom Island for yourself?”

Ho Ga-Myung’s eyes flickered with uncertainty.

“N-No, of course, the one who takes Plum Blossom Island would gain more. But wasn’t the reason Plum Blossom Island was favored because it was managed by Mount Hua? If the pirates took over, who would trust them enough to leave their goods there? It would be like leaving fish with a cat.”

“Who actually managed Plum Blossom Island?”

Ho Ga-Myung fell silent.

It was a question he was reluctant to answer, but why not respond to Jang Ilso’s inquiry?

“Green... Forest.”

“They really did entrust their goods to bandits.”

Jang Ilso chuckled.

“Ordinary civilians might not know, but to merchants, bandits, and pirates are not so different. And what kind of people are merchants? If only their safety is guaranteed, they would trade with ghosts as well as pirates. If the pirates were prevented from plundering within Plum Blossom Island, everyone would soon start using it again.”

“....”

“Or, they could just send a proxy. I am sure there is suspicion, but isn’t there another way?”

“Would the Black Dragon King think that far?”

“Of course, that pig is lazy and greedy. But he is no fool.”

“....”

“He calculates profit quicker than anyone else.”

Jang Ilso clicked his tongue.

“We might manage to deal with that, but the issue is that the Black Dragon King isn’t the only one eyeing Plum Blossom Island.”

“....”

What Jang Ilso intended to convey, Ho Ga-Myung understood immediately.

“This is a matter of great significance. There’s no way the Lower District Sect and Black Ghost Fort won’t take action.”

“Surely...”

Ho Ga-Myung nodded more vigorously.

The more he thought about it, the more painful it felt.

Jang Ilso had been striving to completely control the Four Precepts Alliance for the past three years. However, the parts Jang Ilso could actually hold were those excluding the three sects.

The power of the Four Precepts Alliance, led by the Ten Thousand People Clan, had become incomparably stronger than before. Yet, even for Jang Ilso, it was not easy to instantly destroy or absorb the power of the Shenzhou Five Sects, which had been building a solid system for decades.

So, this was like a closed box that could explode at any time.

“What if the pigs who have been cowering while waiting for a small chance start to move?”

“... it might light a fire at the fuse.”

“Tsk. That is why I have been cautiously moving up until now.”

Jang Ilso shook his head as if he didn’t like it at all.

If those bastards had slowly withdrawn from Plum Blossom Island, they might have had time to prepare. However, Mount Hua withdrew from the Yangtze River too suddenly.

Where the balance was broken, a fierce battle to create a new balance occurred. He accurately pointed out that the enormous profits would create problems within the Four Precepts Alliance.

“... But.”

At that time, Ho Ga-Myung asked with a stern look.

“If you think about it, it could be a chance, Alliance Lord.”

“A chance?”

“Yes. A chance.”

He nodded heavily and spoke in a clear voice.

“I had to sort them out at some point. It is true that leading those who harbor motives and fighting a war was burdensome. When I think about it, it is also an appropriate time.”

“Tsk tsk. Ga-Myung. Ga-Myung.”

But Jang Ilso shook his head immediately.

“You strangely narrow your vision whenever you hear stories related to Mount Hua. It has been like this for a long time.”

“... what are you talking about?”

“Why don’t you understand? Don’t you know what Plum Blossom Island means?”

Jang Ilso pointed his finger at Plum Blossom Island.

“The cargo moves from the mainland to the island and then across the river.”

As Jang Ilso’s fingertip slowly traced from Plum Blossom Island to the riverside on the opposite side, Ho Ga-Myung grasped the implication.

“... Ah!”

“Occupying Plum Blossom Island inevitably means controlling the dock across the river as well. And this place....”

“... North.”

Ho Ga-Myung’s expression turned serious.

“What is on this land?”

“... a huge city. A city that has grown to an unbelievable size in just three years.”

“Exactly. Do you think those opportunists will seize Plum Blossom Island and not covet the city?”

It was impossible.

If it were just an island, would those who controlled the island and the docks leave the city connected to the docks untouched? That was like a warrior obsessed with strength, ignoring the chance to learn from a peerless master.

“Seizing Plum Blossom Island means advancing northward. Whether you like it or not, there will be conflict. Conflict that I cannot control!”

“....”

“But... he engineered a situation where he couldn’t retreat or look away, and the Heavenly Friends Alliance withdrew completely. It was as if he was forcing us to confront the Nine Great Sects and our own people.”

Cold sweat trickled down Ho Ga-Myung’s forehead.

‘How could such a simple move lead to this?’

Initially, he thought it was a foolish decision to forgo a significant profit. But it wasn’t Chung Myung who was foolish; it was Ho Ga-Myung.

It was not about what you gave up that mattered but how much you gained in return.

By giving up the huge profit brought in by the island, Mount Hua created an internal conflict within the Four Sects Alliance, and this internal strife in their alliance would lead to a clash with the Nine Great Sect.

What was even worse was that he had known it all along but couldn’t back out.

The worst situation that lay before them was one where they had no choice but to walk into a place where they knew they would die.

‘What the hell is...’

He completely understood this horrible plan in his head. Still, on the other hand, he was confused, as if he didn’t understand anything. It was understandable...

How could this simple move lead to so many consequences?

A shiver ran down his spine.

“This is fun. Really fun. Hahahahah! For the first time in three years, I have felt this! It is so much fun! Hahahahah!”

Jang Ilso laughed loudly, bending at the waist. Then, as if he couldn’t hold back his laughter, he covered his face and groaned.

But at that moment, Ho Ga-Myung saw Jang Ilso’s eyes, visible between his long, white fingers. Those creepy eyes.

“Ga-Myung.”

“Yes, Alliance lord.”

“I regret the whole thing. Should I have killed him back then, even if it meant going too far?”

Jang Ilso licked his red lips.

“It is falling apart. The plan I made with so much effort has fallen apart in an instant. It is so frustrating, so frustrating.”

What Jang Ilso disliked the most was being dragged into a situation he didn’t plan for.

This wasn’t Jang Ilso’s taste. He wasn’t a person who dragged others into his own situation but rather danced in a situation that others created.

“... Do you really think that Mount Hua’s Sword Saint predicted all of this and moved ahead three years back?”

“As if.”

Jang Ilso said calmly.

“It cannot be like that for a human; it must have just happened by chance.”

Ho Ga-Myung nodded. His thoughts were the same as Jang Ilso’s.

“But...”

But what he said next was somewhat different from what Ho Ga-Myung expected.

“Even if they hadn’t used the means called Plum Blossom Island, the results would have been the same.”

“Uh? What does that mean?”

Jang Ilso chuckled.

“Those who like to talk think that a clever plan that catches the eye is the result of some genius. They say it is an amazing idea created by broad thought and quick thinking, allowing them to see things that no one else has thought out.”

“... isn’t that true, though?”

“No, no, not at all.”

Jang Ilso waved his hand. The trinkets hanging from his wrists clanked against each other, making a noise.

“In the first place, if he were that brilliant, he wouldn’t have created a situation where he would have to come up with such a risky and odd plan.”

“....”

“A plan is not the means of the strong. It is the means of the weak. A plan is something that someone who cannot win using conventional methods thinks and thinks and squeezes out.”

A wicked smile appeared on Jang Ilso’s lips.

“It is sticky. Such thick malice. It is a foul smell that somehow wants to pour shit on my face. If it were a different situation, he would have figured out a way to drag me to hell.”

“....”

“Hehe. The damned bastard.”

Jang Ilso’s hand, which was holding his face, clenched.

“Then...”

Ho Ga-Myung squeezed out the words with a pale and contorted face.

Those words that a person with rank would never want to say out loud.

“... what do you plan to do?”

Jang Ilso sat down on the chair and slumped against its back after hearing those words.

With a look that seemed languid and indifferent at the same time. Like a family who had its favorite eldest son taken away, or like someone who had been assigned a bothersome task.

“I have to play with him.”

He nodded slowly.

“When you are stuck in a swamp if you try to get out of it, you will end up getting dragged deeper into the swamp. The first thing to do is let him go.”

Jang Ilso’s expression instantly turned relaxed.

“If it has reached our ears, the Black Dragon King must have moved too, and the Lower District Sect must be faster than us, so we should assume he is moving too. The only one left is the Ten Thousand People Clan.”

Jang Ilso waved his hand as if he had finished clearing his mind.

“It is a headache. Everyone, go out and see.”

“Yes.”

As the maids retreated, Ho Ga-Myung also got up. He knew that Jang Ilso didn’t need him then.

After he stepped aside so as not to disturb the thoughts, Jang Ilso, who was finally left alone, lightly knocked on the table.

“Mount Hua’s Sword Saint....”

Jang Ilso’s whole body, which showed his fangs, had a fearful presence.

“It seems a bit too much for a greeting that has only been said after three years... this time, I got hit. I am worried about what I should give you in return...”

Jang Ilso, who had been knocking on the table with a knocking sound, quickly grabbed the corner.

“But...”

And slowly, very slowly, his face relaxed.

And a strange expression formed. As if teasing, or perhaps worried, or simply bemused.

“You don’t know. You don’t know yet. How scary a person’s malice could be. Hahahah! Hahahahah! Hahahahahaha!”

The shadow of the table rippled along with Jang Ilso’s laughter as if being sucked into a deeper and endless darkness.

Chapter 910: Let A Bite Of This Now! (5)

“Leave it?”

“That was what was said.”

“Is that so? All of it?”

The first elder opened his mouth.

No, of course, it was understood. One could not just take things apart and move them, and they could not destroy it all when the island was left behind.

But...

“Why are you leaving all the ships behind, the ships!? Do you know how much one ship cost?”

“Tsk tsk! A bandit is being greedy for a ship! You have lived by the water for too long now, so you’ve developed the habit of fishing?”

“That is not the story! If you just take it and sell it...!”

“You bastard. You have to have a place to sell it. Selling a ship cannot be done overnight.”

Im So-Byeong spread out his fan.

“And what is important isn’t money. It is the intention. If you are always after money, you will end up missing out on something really important.”

The first elder couldn’t say anything and just kept pounding his chest.

The Green Forest King had always had a strange side, but it felt even worse after meeting that Taoist from Mount Hua. He couldn’t understand what this man was doing.

“So, what about Cheol-su?”

“... everything is done. The collected treasures have been sent to the Eunha Merchant with an escort, and the guys from Plum Blossom Island have all moved to the mainland. The rest of them have packed up and left, so now it is just us to go.”

“Right. So.”

Im So-Byeong waved his fan gently. A small breeze tickled his cheek. He looked at Plum Blossom Island with slightly regretful eyes.

“It was a good place.”

“It didn’t suit my temperament. It is like Commander Zhongzi living off the pine needles! What kind of work would it be for the proud heroes of Green Forest to carry other people’s luggage and make money?”

“But aren’t you wearing really expensive clothes? Didn’t you buy new ones?”

“A-ahem. That is...”

The first elder’s face went red.

Since Plum Blossom Island earned so much money, even if they took only the money the Green Forest bandits received, they still made a huge profit. Consequently, the elders’ pockets were quite full of silver coins.

The first elder twisted his body, but how could the expensive silk clothes he wore cover his entire body?

“If the first elder insists on going into the mountains and becoming a bandit, I don’t plan to stop them either.”

“W-what are you saying? That is...”

“Tsk tsk. A bandit wearing silk clothes and doing business—if the ancestors knew, they would ignore everything else.”

“Ughh.”

As soon as the word “ancestors” came out, a groan escaped from the first elder’s mouth. If the ancestor of the Green Forest king had seen this, they would have tried to tear the first elder apart, not just his silk clothes.

Im So-Byeong clicked his tongue as he looked across the river.

‘Three years.’

In the past three years, Im So-Byeong and the Green Forest have truly gained a lot. For Im So-Byeong personally, it was a time when he could stabilize the Green Forest, which had been in chaos. Money was not a means to solve everything in the world, but it was a means to solve most things.

Those who opposed Im So-Byeong becoming the Green Forest King kept their mouths shut when they saw the wealth falling into his hands.

However, compared to what the Green Forest really gained, the wealth was nothing.

The biggest achievement was that the common people no longer feared the Green Forest.

Changing their business to not harm those traveling to and from the mountains, collecting tolls, and safely escorting them had a clear effect. Demonstrating hard work through Plum Blossom Island also had an impact.

But what had an even greater impact was the Four Precepts Alliance.

The Four Precepts Alliance declared unification and absorbed all the Evil Sects under their command. At the same time, they absorbed all the hatred of the people in the world.

In the meantime, Green Forest rebelled against the Four Precepts Alliance and supported the Heavenly Friends Alliance. Hence, those who hated the Four Precepts Alliance began to look at Green Forest with a rather warm gaze.

“Mount Hua’s halo also played a part in it. Anyway, I told you he was a very useful Taoist.”

“Uh?”

“No, nothing.”

Im So-Byeong smiled softly.

‘This is how you gamble.’

When he, the leader of Green Forest, said he would join hands with Mount Hua, there must have been more than one or two people who thought he was insane.

He must have been unable to say it out loud because he had gathered power by sweeping away the Great Star Bandits and other places. Still, there must have been no one who truly agreed with the idea.

But the gamble was a complete success.

“No, no, not yet.”

However, Im So-Byeong’s eyes sank coldly once again.

‘At least I need to sweep away the Four Precepts Alliance and stab Jang Ilso in the neck before I can talk about success.’

He briefly pictured that scene in his head and chuckled.

“Well, that isn’t my job.”

“What on earth have you been talking about all this time? Like you are insane.”

“....”

Im So-Byeong’s forehead turned red at the sudden harsh words.

‘We should change into a justice faction at this chance; we can...’

He didn’t even want to go as far as the family lord, who would pretend to die at the word of the family. But even that crazy Taoist from Mount Hua didn’t spit at the Sect leader’s words, so why on earth was the Green Forest going around like this?

His insides were seething.

But before he could say anything, the first elder asked with a serious face.

“More than that, are you really going to back out like this?”

“Why are you bringing up a story that is already done?”

“I am saying this because I feel bad because I feel sorry. Plum Blossom Island is...”

The First Elder’s gaze turned to the back. He could see the large pavilions stretching from the dock.

How much effort had he put into creating the city?

Leaving all of this behind felt like trying to sever a limb. However, Im So-Byeong waved his hand, dismissing the elder’s regret.

“Forget it, get ready now. The guests will be arriving soon.”

“Guests?”

“Yes, guests. Very dark... hmm, it seems like they are already coming.”

“Uh?”

The First Elder turned his head towards where Im So-Byeong was looking.

“Uh...?”

Indeed, the black ship was revealing itself in the distance. At first, it looked small, but as it got closer, its majestic presence was truly amazing.

“T-that is?”

A ship that looked at least three times larger than the other ships escorting it. The sails were painted with the image of a black dragon sailing across the Yangtze River.

And the First Elder was one of those who knew the identity of the ship better than anyone else.

“I-is that the Black Dragon ship?”

When the First Elder asked in surprise, Im So-Byeong calmly fanned himself.

“Hmm, I didn’t expect him to show up in person. It seems like the Black Dragon King is quite pleased with how things are going.”

The ship and the Black Dragon flag hanging from it meant that the Black Dragon King had come here in person to take this small island into his own hands.

Im So-Byeong chuckled.

“No... the Black Dragon King himself...?”

The First Elder opened his mouth wide in shock.

Im So-Byeong was the Green Forest King, but he was not like the other kings, so it was not surprising that he moved around lightly. However, those who were generally called ‘kings’ did not move easily.

But the Black Dragon King himself appeared?

“He must have come to get his hands on Plum Blossom Island while we are away. Before the others do anything.”

The First Elder, who did not immediately understand the situation, opened his eyes wide and looked at the approaching Black Dragon ship.

However, at that moment, another thought crossed Im So-Byeong’s mind.

‘Just one day, right?’

A strange smile appeared on his lips.

Just before leaving Mount Hua, Chung Myung called him and asked him to confirm one thing: Who would come first after they retreated from Plum Blossom Island, and how long would it take for them to show up?

‘Since the pirates came to occupy this place before the bandits even left it alone, it means that he didn’t ask for permission from the Four Precepts Alliance.’

If they showed up within two days, it would be considered a success, but this guy showed up in just a day?

That means a clear rift had begun to form between them. It was undeniable evidence that the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s plan worked more effectively than expected.

“Hehehehe. This is why I like the Taoist.”

Im So-Byeong laughed.

For the past three years, the one who has taken the lead in martial arts was, no matter what anyone says, Jang Ilso of the Four Precepts Alliance. Shaolin, which once held supremacy in martial arts, was unable to handle the Four Precepts Alliance’s speed and simply stayed in hiding, alert.

There was no major conflict, but no one could deny that martial arts was being used around as Jang Ilso intended.

But, as soon as Mount Hua returned, Jang Ilso’s hegemony began to falter. Without lifting his sword even once, without shedding a single drop of blood, he struck a blow more fierce than any sword attack.

Im So-Byeong, who had been lost in thought for a moment, shook his head.

“Let’s go. If you stay here for no reason, you will get struck by lightning.”

“N-No, Green Forest King. If the pirates eat this place...”

“There will not be any trouble.”

Despite the voice of the first elder’s concern, Im So-Byeong spoke with confidence.

“The one who would be most upset at the sight of us abandoning the mountain and mingling with the commoners and comfortably amassing wealth would naturally be the Black Dragon King.”

“....”

“Furthermore, we know how to bow our heads to Mount Hua, but that proud man doesn’t know how to bow his head to Jang Ilso. So, he will try to gather wealth here and strengthen the power of the pirates somehow.”

“That sounds plausible, but...”

“That is what is going to happen.”

Im So-Byeong covered his mouth with the fan and smiled meaningfully.

“Who do you think felt the greatest fear at the sight of Jang Ilso gathering his strength?”

“What do you mean?”

“Let’s go.”

Im So-Byeong turned around, folding his fan, and started walking alone.

“Green Forest King! Come, let us move together!”

The first elder quickly followed. He asked some more questions, but Im So-Byeong just smiled meaningfully and hurried his steps.

‘Now, how will the Shaolin act?’

As expected, there was nothing more fun than watching a fight.

“We have arrived! Black Dragon King!”

“Hmmm!”

The Black Dragon King, who was standing ahead, stared intently at the small island in front of him.

“That insignificant thing can be so annoying.”

If Jang Ilso hadn’t blocked it, he would have wiped them out long ago. Because of that one island, the pirates’ plan to control the Yangtze River was entirely destroyed.

But...

‘This is what they mean by recovering the land.’

The past was no good. He had seen with his own eyes that collecting the proper tolls was a lot more profitable than sloppily robbing people.

Furthermore, he was the leader of the pirates. Unlike them, he was in a position to firmly control those who traveled across the Yangtze River. If he put his mind to it, it wasn’t difficult to earn several times the money that Mount Hua earned on Plum Blossom Island.

“If that happens... I can build a force that surpasses the money of that Ghost. Then Jang Ilso will be nothing.”

The Black Dragon King gritted his teeth.

The status of the Four Precepts Alliance has risen day by day, but the Black Dragon King’s power wasn’t as great as it used to be. People even considered him to be Jang Ilso’s subordinate.

The leader of the Four Precepts Alliance was Jang Ilso, and he was just a sub-leader, so it wasn’t wrong. But...

“This isn’t even fun.”

Now, he would show who the true leader of the Four Precepts Alliance was.

“I will join you!”

The Black Dragon King nodded heavily.

“Get off and occupy!”

“Yes!”

The calm Yangtze River began to boil again.

Chapter 911: There Is No Such Thing As A Fight Between Two Evil Spirits (1)

“It-it is the pirates!”

“It is the pirates!”

The massive ship couldn’t help but draw attention.

The faces of those who saw the enormous black ship near Plum Blossom Island turned pale instantly.

Of course, the pirates were not ones to carelessly kill or harm those who seemed to be of some status.

Just as bandits didn’t always descend the mountain to attack civilians, the pirates didn’t bother setting foot on land. That was the iron rule they had kept to survive.

But no matter what, pirates were pirates!

The sight of pirates taking over Plum Blossom Island, which had been left alone until now, was enough to frighten everyone.

“Yah, is this really okay?”

“Why is Mount Hua aiding this? If we back off like this, the island will really fall into their hands!”

“...can we do business then?”

There was only one reason why people did not flee immediately after seeing that sight.

Most of the people here were merchants. Those who used Plum Blossom Island and filled the nearby cities were merchants and their families.

The rest were those who came to do business with the merchants, meaning that if they ran away, they would have to find a new way to make a living.

It wasn’t as easy as it sounds to abandon one’s home, so they had no choice but to remain.

“T-that seems to be coming this way?”

“What?”

Everyone’s eyes widened at someone’s shout.

The ship that had approached Plum Blossom Island slowly turned its bow and headed towards the dock.

“What should we do? Should we run away now?”

“If we run away now, what else can we do? If we cannot do business anyway, aren’t we all just going to starve to death? In that case, it would be better to hold on and die.”

“Would a living person have their mouth shut? We have to eat and survive.”

“I cannot leave this place. You go.”

Those who had some foresight had already taken out the items from the warehouse, yet they still couldn’t bring themselves to move.

The merchants who could survive in the North wouldn’t have to worry about anything. Still, for those who made a living by trading in the South, it wouldn’t be an exaggeration to say that everything depended on Plum Blossom Island.

“L-let’s listen to what they have to say. Isn’t it the methods of the Evil Sect before all this?”

“But that is....”

“The principle of the Evil Sect is that if you put it on your ear, it’s an earring, and if you put it on your nose, it’s a nose ring. How can something that changes at will be a principle?”

“Then what are we going to do?”

While the people hesitated and struggled, the huge black ship drew closer to the dock.

Soon, the ship slowed down and began to dock at the port.

Gulp.

Silence fell for a moment. Someone gulped, the sound echoing like thunder among them.

A group of people wearing blue clothes jumped from the ship so high it seemed doubtful they could even reach the bridge. They ran and took over the area around the dock in one swift motion.

“Back off!”

“Get back right now if you don’t want to get your throat cut!”

The merchants hurriedly retreated in fear at the threat of the men.

“N-No, wait a minute. I have a question...”

“You moron!”

In the midst of all this, one merchant refused to back down and tried to speak stubbornly. Suddenly, one of the pirates raised his weapon.

But right before, the merchant’s neck was cut off.

“BASTARDDDD!”

A thunderous roar erupted. The men holding the weapon froze in place like stone.

“I-I...”

A man slowly appeared on the deck of the tall ship.

A long black beard and a large physique.

Just by looking at the black robe with a golden dragon engraved on it, one could easily guess the identity of the man.

“B-Black Dragon King.”

A light poured out from the Black Dragon King’s two sharp eyes.

He had a large physique and an appearance that was enough to frighten people. When such a man radiated anger, some of those watching the scene lost their strength in their legs and collapsed on the spot.

“I told you not to touch people.”

“B-Black Dragon King! I just...”

“This filthy bastard!”

With a thud, the Black Dragon King jumped out of the ship and slowly approached the dock. Then he grabbed the guy by the collar.

“S-save me...”

“Cool your head and come back.”

The Black Dragon King grabbed the man with one hand and threw him.

The guy, who flew through the air in one swift motion, screamed and fell into the middle of the river. After the splash, silence fell.

‘A-a person...’

‘Is that a human’s strength?’

The merchants stared at the Black Dragon King with mouths wide open, unable to believe what they had just seen.

“Hmmm!”

The Black Dragon King glared at the surroundings. Fortunately, the ones he was glaring at were not merchants but pirates.

“I will say it again. Anyone who hurts someone here will pay the price. Anyone who hits someone will have their arm cut off; anyone who cuts someone will have their neck cut off. And anyone who kills someone will have their body turned into something that can neither live nor die, and they will be used to fill the stomachs of the Yangtze River fishes!”

“Yes! We will keep that in mind!”

The Black Dragon King finally nodded, seemingly satisfied.

“From now on, this place will be managed by our pirates. Everything else will go back to the way it was before.”

One of the merchants who had been oppressed by the momentum mustered up the courage and raised his hand hesitantly. His entire face was covered in cold sweat.

“What?”

“Uh, B-Black Dragon King. What you mean is... we can use Plum Blossom Island like before?”

“Yes!”

The Black Dragon King shouted loudly enough for everyone to hear.

“In the name of the Black Dragon King, I declare that there will be no plundering on this Plum Blossom Island! We will protect you!”

“T-then the usage fee is...”

“Hmm.”

The Black Dragon King frowned.

Unlike Im So-Byeong, who would rush in with his eyes lit up when money was mentioned, the Black Dragon King was still uncomfortable talking about money.

One of the pirates quickly guessed his intentions and spoke up.

“The Black Dragon King said to double the previous usage fees. Since the people have changed, the fee should naturally change as well.”

The merchants nodded, swallowing their anxiety.

Double would be an acceptable amount. Of course, some weren’t happy, but dissatisfaction had to be expressed carefully. No one dared to bargain in front of the Black Dragon King.

If they tried to bargain without solid reasons to save a couple of coins, they might have to bargain with their lives.

“Go.”

The Black Dragon King gestured forward with his chin.

“From now on, this city is also part of the territory of the Yangtze River 18 River Families. Anyone who wants to stay or do business here will have to pay taxes to the pirates!”

“Yes!”

The pirates rushed ahead to occupy the city.

Some looked at the scene and felt relieved that the pirates had guaranteed their safety. Others lamented the fact that the fee for using Plum Blossom Island had doubled, and some doubted whether they could really trust the pirates’ words.

But, surprisingly, few understood that all of this meant the Four Precepts Alliance had advanced into the North.

“The pirates have occupied the river mouth.”

The slowly spinning prayer beads suddenly stopped in the hands.

The abbot’s eyes narrowed.

“... You said they occupied it?”

“Yes, abbot. To be exact, it isn’t the mouth of the river, but a new city next to it. The name hasn’t been decided yet....”

“People used to call it Plum Blossom Island.”

“Yes, right, abbot.”

The abbot tapped his lips lightly as Beop Gye quickly nodded.

“I heard just a day ago that Green Forest withdrew from Plum Blossom Island. And in just one day, the pirates took over Plum Blossom Island?”

“... Yes.”

“Mount Hua... no, didn’t the Green Forest give a warning to the Four Precepts Alliance? If not...”

“I don’t know much about that. But considering the nature of Mount Hua...”

“Right...”

The abbot closed his eyes.

They said that when the spirit of suspicion arose, everything became suspicious. Still, even that seemed to lose its reason for a moment. Even though everyone in the world dealt with the Four Precepts Alliance, he knew that Mount Hua alone wouldn’t do so...

“... How far have the pirates advanced by now?”

“They have crossed the river...”

Beop Gye trailed off, and the abbot’s face turned cold.

“So they crossed the river to the North.”

“Seems like it, abbot.”

“... They didn’t just cross over temporarily; they occupied it and declared it as their land.”

The abbot’s face darkened for a moment.

“Abbot, isn’t the Four Precepts Alliance declaring its advance to the North?”

“No.”

“... Uh?”

The abbot shook his head and answered.

“It isn’t the Four Precepts Alliance. It must have been the pirates’ sole action.”

“How...”

Beop Gye, about to ask the abbot why he thought that way, soon shut his mouth. Since the abbot, of all people, spoke with such confidence, there must be a reason for it.

“But wouldn’t it be different for others?”

The abbot didn’t answer the question either. It was because he also knew that the abbot was right.

Since the pirate was affiliated with the Four Precepts Alliance, this action would be perceived as the Four Precepts Alliance advancing into the northern region.

And since the land was in the North, it had nothing to do with the Non-aggression treaty that had been suppressing the Nine Sects.

“How stupid...”

The abbot softly gritted his teeth.

Why didn’t he know?

Up until now, the Nine Great Sects had been blamed for causing the Yangtze River war. Now, those who had been watching the Nine Great Sects would demand an answer regarding the incident.

They would demand to know if the reason why the Nine Great Sects refused to cross the Yangtze River was because of the treaty or because they were afraid of the Evil Sect.

And the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families had no choice but to respond to that demand.

‘This is something we cannot back down from.’

It didn’t matter what their will was. In order for them to be proud of being a sect that protected the Central Plains against the Evil Sects, they must punish those who invaded the North.

‘But will that punishment really end as punishment?’

If an all-out war broke out with the pirates, the Four Precepts Alliance would eventually take action. If that happened, the flames would spread in an instant.

“What an idiot... calling us out of petty greed. This fake is better than war.”

After much agony, a name came out of the abbot’s mouth.

“... Chung Myung.”

“A-abbot?”

“Hahah, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint... Mount Hua!”

Crush.

The prayer beads in his hand cracked apart with a crackling sound.

The abbot knew.

All of this movement had originated from Mount Hua.

But, because of their merits in guarding Plum Blossom Island and stabilizing Hubei for the past three years, Shaolin and the Nine Great Sects couldn’t point to the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

’Abbot...“

“Send a message to the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families. Gather all of them at Shaolin right away.”

“The sects are in closed gates...”

“All sects.”

“But, Abbot...”

“Didn’t I say all the sects!”

As the abbot’s voice grew louder, Beop Gye nodded.

“I will do so.”

“Hurry up now.”

“Yes!”

After Beop Gye quickly left, the abbot glanced down at his hand. He noticed the bead cracked in his palm.

“Hoho.”

A hollow laugh escaped his lips.

“There is no such thing as a fight between two evil spirits. Hehehe. Hehehehe.”

His laughter echoed coldly across his darkened face.

Chapter 912: There Is No Such Thing As A Fight Between 2 Evil Spirits (2)

“Hehehehehe.”

“....”

“Hehehehehehehehe.”

“....”

Jo Gul and Yoon Jong exchanged anxious glances before turning to Baek Cheon.

“S-sasuk.”

“... What?”

“Uh, t-that one, why is he acting like that?”

“...”

Baek Cheon glanced at ‘that one’ with a look that suggested the world had lost its meaning.

The figure he hesitated to call a person was clutching a bottle of alcohol in one hand and a letter in the other, giggling like a devil. It had been going on for quite some time.

“Gul, Jong.”

“... Yes, sasuk.”

“What are you all talking about...”

“N-No. I understand that the kid has been out of his mind for more than a day or two.”

“But today, he is especially bad. Sasuk, look at that.”

Baek Cheon flinched slightly at Yoon Jong’s gesture.

Baek Ah, who rarely left Chung Myung’s side, was slowly moving away, keeping a wary eye on him.

‘It’s really cute to see the white one trembling.’

... T-this isn’t it.

That spirit could even blow a tiger’s ear off, so one shouldn’t be fooled by its appearance. If it had a good nature, it wouldn’t say anything...

Baek Cheon turned his gaze back to Chung Myung.

It certainly seemed that his condition was seriously bad. He could almost see the illusion of Asura’s image rising up behind him.

Wasn’t it amazing that a person could exude such an ominous aura while enjoying themselves so thoroughly?

“Kuehehehe....”

Chung Myung mumbled to himself, looking like a conman who had just struck it rich.

“Are these people trying to screw me over? It’s been such a long time, these little brats!”

Baek Cheon shook his head at those words. He probably wouldn’t listen, but he wanted to tell Chung Myung this.

‘Chung Myung...’

If he was dealing with someone who was already not the type to listen to little brats... how upset would that little brat be when he heard that?

“Heheheheh. Uhahahaha! Heheheheh! Hahahahgk!”

“... he has gone insane.”

“Yes. Totally mad.”

“He was never ordinary to start with.”

Chung Myung happily put the bottle of alcohol in his mouth. Watching the liquid move down his throat made everyone feel strangely refreshed.

“... why on earth is he doing that?”

Baek Cheon said with a soft and warm smile.

“You still don’t know him well.”

“... Uh?”

“There are only two cases where that Evil guy really likes things.”

“W-what is that?”

“When he makes a lot of money, or when he really screws someone over.”

“...”

“Looking closely at his smiling face, it seems like it’s the latter this time...”

Jo Gul and Yoon Jong’s eyes began to tremble.

Baek Cheon, who was considered one of the best in understanding Chung Myung, said that it had to be true.

Then...

“Nine Great Sects?”

“Five Great Families?”

“Jang Ilso? Four Precepts Alliance?”

“N-No, maybe it was the Southern Edge?”

As they recited the names that came to mind, they realized it.

There were too many people in the world that Chung Myung wanted to mess with.

‘No, at this point, isn’t everyone in the world except for the Heavenly Friends Alliance that guy’s enemy?’

At this point, shouldn’t we seriously consider who is the cause of all this?

“Hehehe! The weather is so damn nice!”

Rumble!

... it was going to rain, that crazy bastard!

But regardless, Chung Myung laughed, shaking his shoulders cheerfully.

“Hehehehe. Die, all of you! All! Uhahahah!”

Words that should never come out of a Taoist’s mouth were coming out as naturally as breathing.

If that was a Taoist, then even an earthworm was considered a dragon.

“What makes you feel so good?”

In the end, Baek Cheon approached him quietly and asked, and Chung Myung turned his head.

“Ah, sasuk! Want a glass?”

“Are there any other guys in Mount Hua who drink in broad daylight besides you?”

“No, but starting today, sasuk can also have it.”

“... I will decline it.”

Baek Cheon shook his head and asked again.

“But what is happening?”

“Ah, it is nothing. It is just...”

“Yes.”

“It looks like the Nine Great Sect, the Five Great Families, and the Four Precepts Alliance are going to have a showdown.”

“Ah, right. Then it... w-what now?”

Baek Cheon’s eyes widened, and he grabbed Chung Myung by his collar.

“W-who are they fighting with?”

“Nine Great Sect and Four Precepts Alliance.”

“War?”

“Yes, a war.”

Baek Cheon’s face turned pale in an instant.

“Yah, you bastard! If that isn’t a big deal, then even Mount Hua collapsing wouldn’t be a big deal!”

“Ehh. I mean, it is really not a big deal. It is none of our business.”

“How does that have anything to do with us, you insane bastard!”

“It is fine. It is fine.”

Chung Myung reached out and patted Baek Cheon on the back.

“Our Dong-Ryong is being timid. We should just watch a ritual performance and eat rice cakes. Heheheheh!”

Baek Cheon let go of Chung Myung’s collar and covered his face.

‘Oh Heavenly Lord!’

He didn’t understand. Why did He have to give them a child of such chaos... why...

Baek Cheon, who seemed to be agonizing over something, slammed his palm with his fist.

“Ah, first, I should send some letters to Yunnan and the North Sea.”

“T-To the Beast Palace and the Ice Palace?”

“Yes.”

Baek Cheon, who had been suffering, quickly raised his head and smiled brightly.

‘Yeah, even though this kid speaks harshly, he cannot just easily accept this situation. He definitely is requesting reinforcements in advance...’

“I cannot keep this good news to myself! Happiness is doubled when it is shared with others. We should laugh together! Hehehe! Because we are friends!”

“...”

The dark clouds, which had been creeping in, were getting thicker. Baek Cheon, looking up at the clouds with a sad face, was patted on the shoulder by Yoon Jong, who had approached without Baek Cheon realizing it.

“Don’t cry, sasuk.”

“Don’t cry...”

Baek Cheon sniffled and pushed Yoon Jong’s hand away. Then he asked.

“Shouldn’t we also run to the Yangtze River right away?”

“Why?”

“If it is war, we should help too. After all, we are dealing with the Four Sects Alliance.”

Chung Myung nodded with a determined look.

“Of course! Of course, we should fight too!”

“R-right?”

“That is true, but...”

“Uh?”

Chung Myung’s firm face relaxed. With a face like a fully puffed-up cat, Chung Myung leaned back against the tree and slumped.

“It is a bit bothersome right now, so let us go slowly, slowly... Very slowly.”

“....”

“They fight each other enough, and after, their noses bleed enough.”

“....”

“If we are going to do it, it is better to plan a double cross and lose both of them.”

Baek Cheon looked at Chung Myung with a dumbfounded expression.

However, unlike Baek Cheon, who still had some hope for Chung Myung, Jo Gul had given up all hope. That is why he didn’t care what Chung Myung said and just figured out the situation.

“But Chung Myung.”

“Uh?”

“If we do this wrong, won’t it cause problems for us?”

“What will?”

“Think about it. We can fight the Four Precepts Alliance, but we cannot fight the Nine Sects or the Five Great Families, right?”

“Why can’t we fight? We can just beat them up clean, that is it.”

“Ah, no, no. We cannot really have a war. It is hard to just kill each other and bleed.”

“... Uh?”

Chung Myung tilted his head with wide eyes when he heard it.

And everyone who saw that scene instantly knew what was going on in his head.

‘He is thinking about doing it.’

‘He doesn’t get it.’

‘Oh, mother. I am scared of him.’

What on earth was the difference between the Evil Sect and the Justice Sect in this guy’s head?

“So what now?”

“Th-that is right. Uh... but if war breaks out like this and the Nine Sects or Five Great Families wipe out the Four Precepts Alliance, wouldn’t we just be giving the initiative to the Nine Sects and Five Great Families without doing anything? Wouldn’t it be like a dog chasing a chicken that’s on the roof?”

“Uh?”

“... Hm?”

When Jo Gul finished speaking, everyone looked at him in surprise.

Clap, clap, clap, clap.

Chung Myung clapped his hands as if he were impressed. Jo Gul’s shoulders slowly tensed.

“Right? I am right!”

“No, not at all.”

This brat?

“... Then why did you clap your hands?”

“I was impressed because I realized that now my sahyung thinks of the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families as equivalent to the Four Precepts Alliance.”

“That ruined brat.”

“Evil Sect-like bastard.”

“....”

Jo Gul tried to protest because he felt wronged, but it was of no use.

Except for one person.

“No, doesn’t that make sense though?”

“Sasuk!”

Tears of emotion welled up in Jo Gul’s eyes. As expected, Baek Cheon was Baek Cheon. He was worthy of being the next sect leader of this era...

“Usually, what he says is just nonsense...”

No, the next sect leader of the sect had to be Yoon Jong.

“Anyway, isn’t it true that the later we get involved, the stronger the positions of the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families become? No matter how much I think about the situation, I don’t think the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families would lose to the Four Precepts Alliance.”

Even though the Four Precepts Alliance did show absurd growth over the past three years, the Nine Great Sects were the Nine Great Sects, and the Five Great Families were the Five Great Families.

If they gave it their all, no matter how strong the Four Precepts Alliance was, there was no way they would win. That was what Baek Cheon’s common sense was telling him.

Chung Myung, who had been listening, chuckled.

“Some things are wrong, and some are right, but in fact, there is no need to worry about that. The situation sasuk thinks of will not happen in the first place.”

“Uh? Why?”

“Because the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families will not mobilize their forces.”

Baek Cheon’s face hardened a bit.

“... What is that supposed to mean?”

“It is exactly as I said. No matter how much Shaolin talks, no one will gather around.”

“E-even when the Kangho is in crisis?”

“Right, it is a crisis. But you see.”

Chung Myung smiled wryly.

“People don’t know how to act in a hurry until they have been lit on fire. And as sasuk said, if the Nine Great Sects give it their all, they can beat the Four Precepts Alliance.”

“... Right.”

“But would they go ahead just because you want to do it? Then their losses will be greater, and like Jo Gul sahyung said, if we win the war, our forces get weakened, and we will be pushed back by the other sects.”

“D-don’t we have things like merit to gain from it?”

“M-merit? That useless, subtle thing of giving speeches around?”

“....”

“Life is practical, practical. You bastard! This world is a place where the cheaters who earn money through usury live more affluently than soldiers who have served on the battlefield and returned!”

“T-that does hold truth, but...”

“Even if all the Kangho fought together, they would have won, and even when the Demonic Sect invaded, they were the ones who slowly backed out for their own gain. What? Four Precepts Alliance?”

“....”

“Oh my. You really fought so hard.”

Cold sweat broke out on Baek Cheon’s forehead at that.

“So does that mean the Four Precepts Alliance will win?”

“What difference do you think that would make? If Jang Ilso beat the drums and horns, the other leaders of the Shenzhou Five Sects would have applauded from behind. They would not have quickly stepped forward, fought, and then suddenly died.”

“... No.”

Baek Cheon was speechless.

The fight between the Nine Great Sects and the Four Precepts Alliance that he imagined was not like this. He thought it would be a fierce battle between Justice and Evil...

Wasn’t this a battle of wits?

“What kind of war is....”

“No.”

“Uh?”

Chung Myung snorted.

“What kind of war is this? War is always like this. They leave a few lines in history to make it seem like things went down fine. If they had recorded everything that happened, all the warriors around would have died young from anxiety.”

Baek Cheon thought that Chung Myung wasn’t normal. That was because all his nonsense sounded plausible.

“Sasuk, that is why we...”

Chung Myung, who had taken a quick sip of alcohol, chuckled again.

“I said we can just watch those guys’ heads explode while drinking, hehehe. Thinking about the blood on the bald head of the Great King, it feels like everything I did three years ago will end up getting digested all at once! Hehehehe!”

Chung Myung watched from afar and grabbed Baek Ah, who was about to run away. Then, looking at its small face, he burst into laughter.

“... insane bastard.”

Baek Cheon let out a deep sigh, feeling fed up. He realized once again that he had returned to Mount Hua.

He would rather die than suffer.

He would rather die and suffer....

Chapter 913: There Is No Such Thing As A Fight Between 2 Evil Spirits (3)

The news that the pirates had taken over Plum Blossom Island and finally set foot onto the North spread worldwide in an instant.

The word that was emphasized and spread wasn’t “Plum Blossom Island” but the term “North.”

For those living along the Yangtze River and the merchants of the Central Plains, the name Plum Blossom Island was extremely important. However, for the people of the North, who had no reason to leave their place of residence for their entire lives, Plum Blossom Island was still an unfamiliar concept.

“You’re saying that the pirates have set foot into the Northern Lands?”

“Right! They have taken over a city on the Yangtze River and are now acting like they own it!”

“H-How could something like this happen? Didn’t they agree not to cross the river for three years? There is still some time left before the three years end, right?”

“Tsk, tsk, tsk. You’re speaking such nonsense. Wasn’t that mutual non-aggression? It is the Southern Lands’ non-aggression! The Justice Sects of this place cannot cross the Yangtze, but the Four Precepts Alliance can cross over there without any problem.”

“Uh...”

And that situation was enough to remind the world of how one-sided the treaty between the Wudang and the Four Precepts Alliance in the past was.

“So? They will just watch it?”

“How can that be? The treaty was about not invading the Southern Lands, not about touching those who crossed to the North.”

“Right! Of course, it should be!”

“Even though the Nine Great Sects suffered great humiliation, they are still the Nine Great Sects! What they are saying is true. If they had not fought in the Yangtze River and had fled in the first place, would they have lost to the Evil Sect?”

“Yes! And they didn’t lose! They just negotiated!”

Some people were even thinking that the process or content of that negotiation was no different from defeat. However, those words remained unspoken, caught in their throats.

This was because the atmosphere was not conducive to pointing out the facts.

“Anyway, this is the Northern Lands. No matter how much the Nine Great Sects are treated as a toothless tiger these days, isn’t a tiger still a tiger? Would they just leave a wolf cub that entered their lands alone? They would surely kill it.”

“Right! Of course, it should be so! How dare the Four Precepts Alliance bastards set foot in here?”

Even those who had criticized the Nine Great Sects in unison for abandoning the pride of the Justice Faction in the Yangtze River now came out to defend the Nine Great Sects at this moment.

The advance of the pirates, or the Four Precepts Alliance, into the Northern Lands region was equally frightening news to them. No matter how dishonorable the actions of the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families were, they couldn’t possibly be compared to the Evil Sect.

“Even beasts are scarier when they get injured. Haven’t those Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families been waiting for a chance to take revenge on the Four Precepts Alliance for the past three years?”

“Ah, right. The Southern Lands Non-aggression treaty has kept us tied up all this time. If it wasn’t for that, we would have already invaded the Southern Lands and cut off Jang Ilso’s head!”

“Tsk tsk. I don’t understand. What is so important about a treaty? If we had just ignored it and attacked the Four Precepts Alliance, no one would have said anything.”

“Ugh, this bastard! If you cannot keep your own words, how can you call that a justice faction? How will they be any different from those unruly Evil Sect people?”

“That is what they say, isn’t it?”

“In any case, the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families will never stay still. Never!”

Everyone believed it. The Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families, who had been humiliated by the Four Precepts Alliance and had their pride damaged, would naturally seek revenge in time.

Although they had been disappointing, the expectations for the prestigious sects, who were once everyone’s pride, remained alive.

And if those famous families couldn’t stop the Four Precepts Alliance, the people’s lives would turn into hell, a reaction driven by fear.

That was why everyone north of the Yangtze River hoped that the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families would once again come to the forefront and restore order to this chaos....

However, things didn’t always go as planned.

A cold chill appeared on the abbot’s face.

“... What did you say?”

Beop Gye couldn’t bear to look him in the eye and lowered his head. He hadn’t done anything wrong, but he felt guilty and embarrassed for no reason.

Beop Gye forced his mouth open and spoke again in a weak voice.

“We got a reply... there aren’t many sects that want to come to Shaolin.”

“Not many?”

“... Yes.”

The abbot was silent for a moment. The only sound he could hear was the regular movement of prayer beads rolling.

“Which sect refused?”

“T-that is...”

A cold sweat ran down Beop Gye’s forehead.

“First of all, the Beggars Union has expressed their intention to participate.”

“....”

“And... the Kongtong Sect is also said to be coming to Shaolin now. Kunlun Sect also said they would come...”

“Kunlun is so far that they probably wouldn’t be able to arrive on time.”

“I was thinking the same.”

Kunlun Mountain, where the Kunlun Sect was located, was on the border of Qinghai and Xinjiang. As a result, the distance from Hunan was as much as 350 km. This was even farther than some sects beyond the land’s borders.
