Therefore, though Kunlun had been a member of the Nine Great Sects since long ago, it had also been subtly excluded from them.

“As you know, the Southern Island...”

“I know. I guess they will not be able to come.”

Beop Gye nodded.

Since the Four Precepts Alliance took control of the Southern Lands, the sect in the most dangerous place was the Southern Island Sect. They had established on the Southern Island, located in the South Sea, made it their base, and were now unable to set foot a single step inland.

“So...”

The abbot, who had been listening to Beop Gye’s mumbling, eventually contorted his face.

“Where are the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families? Do not drag your words and answer me right!”

Beop Gye was startled by his tone and quickly nodded.

“Yes, abbot! Emei, Qingcheng Sect, and Diancang Sect have informed us that it would be difficult for them to come through the path of the Southern Lands.”

“... reason?”

“That... if the Four Precepts Alliance advances into the Northern Lands, Sichuan will become deserted then...”

Crack.

The prayer beads, which had been making a regular tapping sound, were clenched, letting out a harsh noise.

It wasn’t an unreasonable statement.

However, what the abbot had originally requested was not for those sects to move north. It was a conversation with the elders of the sects. What kind of law is this to refuse to talk based on something that hasn’t even been discussed?“

“And?”

“... and the Closed Gate sects haven’t responded yet.”

“Even Wudang?”

“... Yes.”

The abbot looked up at the sky without another word. After staring at the blue sky for a long time, he sighed and mumbled in a self-deprecating voice.

“The Nine Great Sects are supposed to discuss things as a whole.”

“....”

“There are only three sects willing to discuss this critical situation. Only...”

“Abbot...”

Beop Gye unconsciously clenched his fists as he watched, unable to hide his disappointment.

Southern Edge, he could understand.

They entered the Closed Gate before the incident occurred and have maintained their position. To put it bluntly, they were free from responsibility for the entire situation.

The Southern Island Sect, too, could be understood. They also had no choice due to their position.

But the other sects, not so much.

Not only Wudang behind their Closed Gate, but also the three sects in Sichuan were responsible for this. In particular, Wudang, who participated in the war between the Yangtze River and the Qingcheng sect, could not ignore this.

All that was left were the Kongtong sect, the Beggars Union, and the Kunlun Sect, which was too far away to be of much use.

“Who in the Five Great Families?”

“... The Hubei Peng family sent word that they would come. But the Namgung family hasn’t answered yet...”

“...”

“The Zhuge family gave a positive answer that they would come to Shaolin depending on the situation... but the Moyong family still hasn’t responded.”

The abbot shook his head.

With the Tang family missing from the Five Great Families, there were just four left. Of those, only the Hubei Peng family has expressed the intention to participate. The Zhuge family’s words that they will participate depending on the situation couldn’t be taken at face value. They were the most cautious sect in the world.

“Beop Gye.”

“... Yes, abbot.”

“I won’t say I am not at fault.”

“A-abbot.”

The abbot opened his mouth with a stiff face.

“As abbot of Shaolin, I certainly made a big mistake. But... what is the situation now? Do you really not know what kind of situation the pirates stepping into the Northern lands could lead to?”

That was impossible.

If one didn’t anticipate that much, one wouldn’t dare become the leader of any prestigious sect. Absolutely not.

“But in the midst of all this, they are all looking out for their own interests! In the midst of all this!”

This was exactly what made the abbot so angry.

The sects that had announced their participation were either directly related to the Northern land or too far away to affect the outcome anyway.

The places that were barely a step away from the fire that would unfold in the Northern Lands were all keeping their distance while keeping an eye on things.

“How can you even talk about this? How can! This is why...”

The abbot, who had been angry, suddenly closed his mouth as if he was trying to say something but couldn’t bring himself to say it.

For a long time, he bit his lips as if he couldn’t control his emotions. And then he said,

“Beop Gye.”

“... Yes, abbot.”

“Tell me, do you think their actions can be forgiven?”

“... Abbot.”

“The opponent is not somewhere else, but the Evil Sect? How can you say that you can take advantage of this situation?”

Beop Gye thought for a moment and lowered his head.

The answer he had given was too embarrassing and sinful to say on his own.

“Abbot...”

“Say it.”

“Weren’t we the first to break something that shouldn’t have been broken?”

“....”

Beop Gye couldn’t bear to look at the abbot and closed his eyes tightly.

“I... did it. I was there... I should have been prepared to die, and yet...”

Beop Gye closed his mouth.

He realized that he had been overcome with emotions and said things he shouldn’t have said.

“I also thought about the safety of the disciples and Shaolin before my rightful task. How can I blame others?”

“That is...!”

The abbot tried to refute it but then shook his head.

It was because he knew that no words would comfort Beop Gye, and no words could save them.

“Even so, isn’t this too narrow-minded? After the Northern Lands burn down, what is next? It is obvious who will be next. Amitabha. Amitabha...”

But the abbot’s words, which had been pouring out, soon became slurred.

It was because he knew that the words he had spoken were all losing their power.

Beop Gye glanced at him and opened his mouth.

“Abbot, if only the incident at the Yangtze River had been handled more delicately, more sects might have been willing to confront the Evil Sects. But...”

“... What does that mean?”

“It is unfortunate... but Mount Hua.”

The abbot’s face went stiff at the mention of the word ‘Mount Hua.’

A self-mocking voice continued from Beop Gye’s mouth.

“No one wants to take the lead. The price of risking one’s life for the central plains... now everyone knows it.”

The abbot’s face felt blank at that.

“Huhu... hu.”

In the end, what came out was nothing but a self-mocking laugh. He laughed for a moment before speaking softly.

“... the original sin.”

Those words had a heavy resonance. Beop Gye looked at the abbot with a worried face.

“... Abbot.”

“Yes, you are right. Who are we to blame others...”

They were not words to be spoken.

Words they shouldn’t say.

That incident left deep scars not only on Mount Hua but also on the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families. And if it continued to this day, everyone would shudder.

Neither Wudang nor Shaolin could escape that perception.

Because they saw with their own eyes that the result of discussing the agreement and fighting ahead of everyone was destruction and exclusion.

Perhaps the real cause of the Yangtze River war was that.

“... send a letter to the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

“A-Abbot?”

“We need to wait, even if I am shameless and guilty.”

Beop Gye nodded without another word. He knew that adding anything more would push the abbot into more trouble.

“I will do so.”

As Beop Gye stepped back, the abbot’s face displayed deep regret.

‘How do I endure...’

A small grumble escaped from his mouth as he closed his eyes.

‘H-How did you endure that painful time...’

The name Mount Hua stung like a needle engraved on the chest.

Chapter 914: There Is No Such Thing As A Fight Between 2 Evil Spirits (4)

“What...”

“....”

“Is it here?”

The head slowly turned to the side.

Hyun Jong unconsciously shut his eyes at the sight. He knew all too well what that guy was thinking when his head snapped to the side.

“Ahem. So...”

“Yes.”

“We have a letter from Shaolin.”

“Ahh, Shaolin.”

“Y-yes. Shaolin.”

“Is it the Shaolin I know? The place where all those shiny bald-headed people live together on a mountain called Mount Song?”

Hyun Jong’s gaze scanned the room anxiously.

He was hoping there might be another shiny bald (?) person there, but fortunately, everyone there had thick hair....

Thick... hair....

“What is it?”

Hyun Jong’s gaze lingered on Hyun Young for a moment, and then Hyun Young tilted his head.

“That...”

“Uh?”

“N-Nothing.”

Hyun Jong gulped the words he couldn’t bring himself to say and slightly averted his gaze. His eyes turned wet.

That poor guy suffered all his life...

“Ahem.”

Hyun Jong coughed loudly and turned his gaze back to Chung Myung.

“Yes. That is that Shaolin.”

“Huhhh?”

Chung Myung said with a face of sudden surprise.

“The Shaolin is sending us a message? Oh my! I thought all we had to share were warm fists and cool swords, but a letter, too! The civilized attitude of trying to solve problems through dialogue is between us and those...”

“N-No, Chung Myung. Even so, it isn’t like they cannot send us letters....”

“Ehhh?”

Chung Myung asked with his eyes wide open.

“No, Sect Leader! A letter?”

“The letter....”

“A letter is something written in words, right?”

“Uh? Yes. Right. But why bring it up...”

“Sending something written in words means you understand what is being said. Those bald Shaolin bastards cannot possibly understand what is being said, so how can they send letters?”

“...”

“If they could understand what is being said, they wouldn’t be acting like this! How can those hairless bastards even read letters? They are all useless!”

Chung Myung unleashed a torrent of harsh words that would have made the abbot foam at the mouth and collapse if he had heard them.

Meanwhile, Hyun Jong was very thankful that there were no outsiders present.

“... Yes, oddly enough, they sent a letter. So, I would like to discuss the contents first.”

“Why?”

“... please now.”

When Hyun Jong spoke earnestly, Chung Myung nodded, crossing his arms.

“Well, I will listen.”

“Right. Right. Think carefully....”

“You can even listen to a dog bark, so how can you not listen to a monk barking?”

“...”

Hyun Jong quickly opened his mouth, reminding himself that the best way to talk to Chung Myung was to simply state the business.

“This is a letter from the abbot of Shaolin. It is addressed to the Heavenly Friends Alliance, not Mount Hua.”

“What is the content?”

“Hmm.”

Hyun Jong relayed the content with a slightly worried expression.

“The movements of the Four Precepts Alliance in the Southern lands are unusual, so they want to discuss it, and the Heavenly Friends Alliance leader is being asked to attend. And... your name is also mentioned separately. He is asking you to make time to come to Shaolin.”

Hyun Jong quickly checked Chung Myung’s expression after finishing his speech. He expected Chung Myung to lose his composure and throw a fit.

But, strangely, Chung Myung didn’t show any particular reaction and just nodded his head with his arms still crossed.

Hyun Jong, slightly puzzled, asked cautiously.

“... Are you alright?”

“Uh? What?”

“No, it’s just...”

To say this with my own mouth...

“Well, it seems like a good thought.”

“G-Good thought?”

“Yes. In conclusion, they are telling the sect leader and me to come to Shaolin, right?”

“R-right.”

“O-of course, it is a good idea.”

Chung Myung smiled brightly.

Hyun Jong, seeing that flawless smile, was about to nod in relief.

“I am glad you think so...”

“He told me to come because he wanted me to break his head, so why would I not go! That bald bastard! Come on, I am coming along too!”

“Where are you going now, you brat!”

As Chung Myung tried to fly out, the Five Swords, who had been waiting, flew in like rays of light, grabbing his limbs and hanging on. His quick response, reminiscent of a perfect footwork technique, was so impressive that he couldn’t help but admire him.

“Let go? Will you not let go? Okay!”

“W-where are you going, you bastard!”

“Where am I going! Where am I going? I am going to Shaolin!”

“Why Shaolin!”

“We need to go! The bald one told me to come! What is wrong with going because he told me to come!”

Chung Myung rolled his eyes.

“That guy has been quiet for three years and lost his senses! Ah, nice then! I will show him why I trained for three years! If I tattoo a plum blossom on his shiny head, he will become a true Buddha!”

“S-Shaolin, you say!”

“That is not what I should say! Why do you think I even trained for three years!”

Uh? Wasn’t it because of the Evil Sect?

Hyun Young spoke quietly as Hyun Jong sighed, holding his head in his hands.

“Sect leader.”

“... what is it?”

“I think there is a need for us to think.”

“Think?”

“Yes. Sect leader, what do you plan to do, sect leader?”

“Hmm.”

Hyun Jong thought for a moment and then said,

“Well, no matter how strong we are, we aren’t on the same level as Shaolin. Since the abbot personally sent a letter to us regarding the Four Precepts Alliance issue, wouldn’t it be polite to go and see him?”

“I have different thoughts, though.”

Hyun Jong looked a little surprised at Hyun Young’s words, asking for an explanation.

“As we saw during the recent breakout of the Evil Sect, Shaolin doesn’t think much of us.”

“....”

“But the fact that he insists on inviting us means that he has another ulterior motive, doesn’t he?”

Most people had noticed that Shaolin, the leader of the Nine Great Sects, despised the Heavenly Friends Alliance. He just didn’t speak out about it.

At that moment, a blunt voice suddenly interrupted them.

“What is the ulterior motive? It is just a slipping foot for him.”

“Uh? Slipping foot?”

Hyun Young looked back at Chung Myung. Chung Myung licked his lips as if he was displeased and shook off his four sahyungs, who were holding onto his limbs.

“If the Nine Great Sects or the Five Great Families had properly responded to the call, would the Shaolin have turned to us? Because those bastards didn’t listen to him, the abbot sent us a letter.”

“....”

“I bet they are holding onto a statue of Buddha and crying out now. Hehehehe!”

“Chung Myung. If you rejoice at the misfortune of others, you won’t be able to attain enlightenment...”

“Ehh, then all those Shaolin bastards would end up in hell! When they go to hell, won’t the Buddha say, ‘Hey! You bastards! I came down here specially to wait for you!’ and hit them with Buddhist scripture!”

“....”

Let us not talk anymore.

Hyun Jong was speechless at the disciple’s personality, which was endlessly gloomy. At the same time, Baek Cheon tilted his head.

“But Chung Myung.”

“Uh?”

“If what you said is true, then it is what you wanted, right?”

“That is also right.”

“Then why are you so angry?”

“No!”

Chung Myung’s eyes, which had calmed down, began to shine again.

“That arrogant bastard told our sect leader to come there!”

“....”

“Do they still think they are that great off? Even if he came running barefoot and begged, I wouldn’t have gone out once, but what? He wants us to attend there? Just like that!”

“....”

While Baek Cheon was speechless, someone nodded.

‘Samae?’

No, why was she siding with...

In the midst of all this, Hyun Jong, who hadn’t forgotten his duties as a sect leader, calmed down the chaotic situation.

“Please keep your mouth shut, everyone...”

“Yes, sect leader.”

“If you say that, it is unfair, sect leader. Only one person opened his mouth, so why are you... ack!”

Jo Gul, who had been beaten by Yoon Jong for interfering and was thrown into the corner, expressed his frustration with eyes like a puppy drenched in rain, but no one looked back at him.

“... It is a letter sent to the Heavenly Friends Alliance, so we should send a reply. What do you think we should do?”

“Let us chew it down.”

“Burn it down, too.”

“Oh. Why are you all like this? Let us politely decline it.”

“Can we just not ask him to come to us?”

In the end, the eyes of Hyun Jong turned wet.

These kids were so spoiled. They were not at a stage of being disciplined anymore.

“Sect leader.”

In the meantime, Baek Cheon opened his mouth seriously. However, Hyun Jong, who still had a glimmer of hope for Baek Cheon, felt happy.

“Yes, Baek Cheon. Speak.”

“The Shaolin has indeed requested us, but there is no reason for us to necessarily accept their request.”

“Y-You think so?”

“Yes, sect leader. If we look at this objectively, Shaolin has always ignored our requests or set conditions for us. So, is there any reason for us to do as they say?”

Hyun Jong nodded at the right words. But he still couldn’t shake off the worry in his eyes.

“But I am worried. Looking at the current situation, it seems like war might break out soon...”

“War doesn’t break out so easily.”

The one who said those words was Chung Myung.

“Even if you growl and fight like that, war isn’t easy to happen. Especially for those who have something to lose. Whether it is Shaolin or someone else, they all don’t want to get hurt.”

“...”

“That might be one of the reasons why the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families aren’t listening to Shaolin now.”

“Are you saying that they are afraid that if they gather for no reason, a real war might actually break out?”

“Yes.”

Jo Gul raised his hand with a puzzled look and asked.

“No, but it isn’t like war won’t break out just because they don’t come out. Jang Ilso won’t be satisfied with just the Southern Lands forever, right?”

“Jang Ilso hasn’t moved yet. People tend to postpone things even if they are inevitable.”

It certainly made sense. Jo Gul nodded.

“Well, they know they will get caught anyway, but they want to hide it for a day longer... ackk! Why are you hitting me!”

“You stole alcohol!”

“That is...”

Baek Cheon couldn’t finish his words.

In the past, he might have said yes. But now, his answer was already set.

“People are all the same. Even when Shaolin hears the name of the leader of the Nine Great Sects being called, it is because of a crisis. Isn’t it normal for everyone to try to defeat Shaolin at least once?”

“... right.”

“Those people who are always so emotional are unanimously listening to Shaolin in an ambiguous situation like this? Hehe. That is ridiculous. There must have been hundreds more of those like the abbot right now.”

After finishing his words, Chung Myung laughed with a bitter tone, reflecting on the past.

‘Those insane bastards.’

Even with the Demonic Sect right in front of them, those people had said, ‘We are leaving, we are stepping back.’ They were just barely alive. If those bastards had just moved properly, the sacrifices would have been halved.

When the most basic things, life, and profit, were at stake, Chung Myung knew how petty people could be, and he could clearly see what would happen in the future.

“Then what about the Yangtze River?”

“They will probably give them some time and step down.”

“... if the abbot does that, the reputation of the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families will really fall to the ground!”

“Not many people risk their lives for the sake of reputation, you know. Honor is something you can recover, but losing your life is the end of it all. No matter how much you give them, they will probably make excuses like they can recover everything if they just destroy the Evil Sect in the end.”

“Ughh.”

“That is why war is something that cannot be resolved through a meeting. Usually, it is one insane person...”

Chung Myung, who had been rambling on and on, suddenly closed his mouth.

Then, his eyes gradually widened, and he smiled brightly.

“W-why are you doing that?”

Chung Myung looked back with a blank expression as Baek Cheon asked in confusion.

“Sasuk.”

“Uh? What?”

“... war could break out, right?”

“What?”

Chung Myung mumbled, tilting his head to the side.

“If we just wait a moment like this...”

His expression became strange as he remembered the existence of the card he had forgotten about.

“Maybe it could really set Shaolin on fire?”

“What are you even talking about, you brat!”

“W-we need to check out a bit, though! Where is that beggar man! The beggar!”

When Chung Myung kicked the door open and left without a proper explanation, the others could only stare blankly at the spot where he had been.

“What is he thinking now?”

“How could I know...”

“I am hungry.”

Everyone let out a sigh.

Chapter 915: There Is No Such Thing As A Fight Between 2 Evil Spirits (5)

Shaolin.

The abbot quietly observed those seated before him.

Neung Sam of the Gentle Crow Beggars was attending in place of the Beggars Union leader. The Kongtong Sect’s leader, Demon Hunter Hermit, Jong Ri-Hyung, was present. Also in attendance was Peng Yeop, the head of the Hubei Peng family, known as the Lightning Sword Blade.

All of them were esteemed figures.

However, considering that the abbot of the great Shaolin had personally invited them, a gathering of only three seemed rather insufficient, no matter how formidable each individual was.

Even with the anticipated arrival of Kunlun, delayed by distance, the total would be just four. Including the Zhuge family, whose intentions remained unclear, the number would only rise to five.

If Shaolin’s reputation still loomed as large as the Big Dipper in the martial world, at least ten or more sect leaders would have assembled here.

The abbot’s anger simmered, his heart ablaze, yet he did not reveal his true feelings to those present.

To avoid showing weakness?

No, that wasn’t the reason.

He didn’t need to express his true feelings because they were already apparent to those gathered there.

“Is no one else coming?”

At that moment, Neung Sam asked unexpectedly. The abbot replied with a broad smile.

“The sect leader of Kunlun is on his way. The Zhuge family head has also confirmed his attendance.”

“Zhuge and Kunlun... so the others are not coming?”

“We haven’t received a definite response from them yet.”

The abbot shot a sharp glance at Neung Sam as he gave his brief reply.

It was the Beggars Union, not another sect. Those who could fill this place with their intelligence network must have known these were all who would gather. Yet, Neung Sam insisted on highlighting this fact to provoke the abbot.

“The abbot mentioned the need for discussion, so we came here... but can we have a meaningful discussion with just us?”

The abbot endeavored to maintain a gentle smile.

“Those who were involved in the discussion will accept the decision here, so it will not be a big problem.”

“That would be great, but... how do I tell our leader about this?”

Instead of answering, the abbot quietly rolled the prayer beads in his hand. It was one of his habits when he was angry.

Though in truth, he was more frustrated than angry at the moment.

How many years ago was it that Shaolin held the Worldly Murim Tournament? At that time, no one doubted that Shaolin was the best in the world.

Back then, not just the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families, but the leaders of all strong sects from around the world visited Shaolin and wanted to make friends with them. But in just a few years, the situation had changed so much.

“Those who didn’t come, that is fine. There is no use looking for those who aren’t here! The more people there are, the more they talk.”

Peng Yeop spoke in a cold voice.

The abbot looked at him in a new light. Judging people by their appearance was a bad habit, but whenever he saw Peng Yeop, he was reminded of his dislike of the Hubei Peng family.

Those with the Peng family name were famous for their large bodies. That large body was an advantage in training in martial arts, which was why the Hubei Peng family was able to establish itself as a martial sect.

But Peng Yeop’s body wasn’t large; it was smaller than the abbot’s.

This was why Peng Yeop felt sharp rather than fierce, like a swordsman with a swift nature.

“Rather than that.”

Peng Yeop stared at the abbot with his sharp eyes and asked.

“What are you going to do, abbot? You don’t plan on leaving them like that, do you?”

He did inherit the Peng family’s impatient temperament because he asked the main point right away.

“We gathered here to discuss that thing...”

“If you delay it, it will be too late. Even at this moment, the Four Precepts Alliance might be rushing into the northern river lands. If we give them time, the situation will become more difficult.”

However, the content of his words was correct. The abbot thought so, too.

It seemed there was someone else with a different opinion.

“You shouldn’t consider it so hastily.”

Jong Ri-Hyung, the sect leader of the Kongtong sect, quietly added.

“The enemy is the Four Precepts Alliance. They are not people to be underestimated.”

“I know that too, but...”

“What worries me the most is that there is no guarantee the other Nine Sects will provide timely support. If the news spreads, we will have no choice but to join the fight, but the damage could be severe before we can fully assemble our forces.”

“But we cannot just leave the issue alone, right?”

“We might end up interfering with the natural course of events. At best, aren’t they just stationed at the mouth of the Yangtze River?”

Upon hearing that, Peng Yeop coldly responded.

“The mouth of the Yangtze River, sect leader? That is the Northern Lands.”

“Is there a law that says the Four Precepts Alliance cannot set foot in the Northern Lands?”

“What do you...”

Jong Ri-Hyung shook his head with a cold expression.

“Because of the Non-Aggression Treaty, we feel like the land is divided between us and the Four Precepts Alliance, but to begin with, we didn’t prevent the Evil Sect from entering the Northern Lands. So why are we bringing up this topic now?”

Neung Sam of the Beggars Union, who had been listening quietly, snickered.

“The sect leader’s words are indeed true, but the problem is that the people do not think that way.”

“The people you say...”

“But we cannot go to the Southern lands, and we cannot declare that it is okay for those Evil Sect people to set foot into the Northern Lands, right?”

“Hm. That is...”

This time, even Jong Ri-Hyung couldn’t really refute.

“The justification is true, but the problem is the reality, abbot.”

Neung Sam’s gaze turned to the abbot this time.

“You have to be honest. I will have risked my life there with 100,000 people.”

“... Yes.”

“If we take the lead, will the Nine Great Sects or Five Great Families really come to support us in time?”

The abbot couldn’t answer easily. It was embarrassing to tell the truth, and he couldn’t tell a lie as a Buddhist.

“As I thought.”

Neung Sam clicked his tongue. It wasn’t something he should do in front of Shaolin’s abbot, but now, no one was going to bring it up.

“Then, I will ask you another question.”

“What is that?”

“Does Shaolin really intend to take the lead in this matter?”

“... Take the lead?”

“Even if the other sects don’t support you, do you intend to lead the sects there and fight against the Four Precepts Alliance by staying at the forefront?”

“Of course...”

The abbot’s words trailed off, though. His tongue didn’t move as he wanted.

Because once he said those words, there was no turning back.

To be the vanguard.

Now he realized how much weight those seemingly insignificant words, those words he had casually used, held.

Neung Sam, who was watching, clicked his tongue inside.

‘I know.’

It wasn’t that there was no way out yet. If Shaolin fought ferociously against the Four Precepts Alliance before anyone else, the other sects who were watching would have no choice but to move.

They would be afraid of the criticism of the world, and if Shaolin won the battle against the Four Precepts Alliance while preserving their power, there would be no way to stop the dominance of Shaolin.

Still, the abbot couldn’t bring himself to draw the sword.

‘What are you so afraid of?’

If it were the abbot who he saw at the Worldly Murim Tournament, he would have nodded his head, saying it was expected. However, the abbot stopped nodding. It meant that something was weighing on his mind.

‘Leading doesn’t mean pointing a finger from behind, but standing in front and running first.’

If Shaolin had forgotten that fact, there was no other way.

“Then I think we have concluded.”

Neung Sam shrugged.

“Just give up the Yangtze River and hold it back so it doesn’t go up any further.”

“Gentle Crow Beggar. That is impossible.”

Peng Yeop immediately refused. However, Neung Sam stubbornly shook his head.

“Lord. I am an elder of the Beggars Union. This means that I am not the sect leader.”

“... I did not mean to ignore you.”

“That is not what I meant. I mean, I am not the sect leader who is responsible for everyone, so I can say what I want to say. I do not need to save face here.”

“...”

“If we touch the river now, there will definitely be a war.”

“Why? Wouldn’t they retreat?”

“The Black Dragon King would do that. But would Jang Ilso do that?”

When the name Jang Ilso came up, Peng Yeop shut his mouth.

The reputation of the Hegemony Lord, Jang Ilso, was deeply hurtful even for the sects that didn’t participate in the Yangtze River war.

Furthermore, Jang Ilso was a person who possessed both the perfect plan to trap his opponents in a conspiracy and a terrifying determination to the point of frightening others. Just hearing his name made everyone feel uneasy.

“If Jang Ilso knew that just a few sects were coming to handle the river, he would definitely try to take advantage of that gap. Do we really need to expose our weakness?”

Weakness.

A large crack had formed in the Nine Great Sects. This meant that they could not reveal that crack to Jang Ilso. If they just step back and pretend to watch, it would be difficult for them to understand the situation fully.

Even though he completely understood what was being said, Peng Yeop exploded with anger.

“What on earth are the other sects thinking? Isn’t this a matter of dealing with the Four Precepts Alliance? It wouldn’t be enough even if everyone here were to fight, taking the lead!”

A deep look of concern appeared on the abbot’s face.

“What is the point of saying anything now?”

He took a deep breath and looked at Neung Sam.

“Elder.”

“Yes, abbot.”

“I want to ask you something.”

“Yes.”

After a brief pause, the abbot spoke.

“If the Heavenly Friends Alliance were to participate in the war, would the Beggars Union be willing to fight alongside Shaolin?”

“...Heavenly Friends Alliance, you say?”

“Yes.”

Neung Sam frowned.

“Since the Ice Palace and Beast Palace wouldn’t be able to reach the Central Plains, you are talking about Mount Hua and the Tang family, right?”

“Yes.”

The abbot nodded.

“I haven’t received their answer yet, but I have contacted the leader of the Heavenly Friends Alliance. It isn’t impossible.”

“It is possible then...”

Neung Sam frowned and thought for a moment.

‘Surely...’

The thing he had the hardest time predicting was the movements of the Heavenly Friends Alliance, especially Mount Hua. That strange sect was a place where one couldn’t guess where they were heading.

Common sense told you that an eclectic group like the Heavenly Friends Alliance, which wasn’t connected to Hubei or Hunan, would have no reason to join in.

But...

‘Isn’t it Mount Hua?’

Since they had a deep grudge against the Evil Sect, they might take this chance to sweep away the Four Precepts Alliance. Since it was an unpredictable sect, they could not consider the possibility as impossible.

“If the Heavenly Friends Alliance steps ahead, the situation will definitely change. Then there is a reason just to watch the enemies. The Tang family will surely join.”

“Hmm.”

The abbot nodded quietly.

“Then I guess we will have to wait for the answer from the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

“If the abbot wants to confront them... you have to either change the minds of the other Nine Great Sects who have withdrawn or bring the Heavenly Friends Alliance in, or you might have to do both.”

“... Amitabha.”

The abbot softly prayed.

‘In the end, it is the Heavenly Friends Alliance.’

It was almost impossible to change the minds of the sects that hadn’t come. In the end, what Neung Sam said just now was the same as saying that they would somehow get the Heavenly Friends Alliance in.

He sighed at the thought that he might have to appeal to the Heavenly Friends and Mount Hua again. But on the other hand, he also felt a sense of relief.

At least this meant that he didn’t have to decide the fate of Shaolin right then. For him, waiting for the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s answer and even the time he wasted on going to appeal to them was fortunate.

But...

Unfortunately, things in this world don’t always go as planned.

Kwang!

At that moment, the door opened violently with one person rushing in.

The face of the abbot twisted.

“This is where the sect leaders are. How dare you act this rashly!”

“W-we are in trouble! Abbot!”

However, the face of Beop Gye turned gravely shocked. The moment the abbot saw that expression, he could instinctively feel something had happened.

“What is happening?”

“T-that is...”

The words of Beop Gye continued.

The abbot’s face turned pale in an instant.

“Why, why now! WHY!”

A scream burst out of his mouth.

Chapter 916: There He Was, That Crazy Guy! (1)

“Ah! What happened!”

“....”

Hong Dae-Kwang stared blankly at Chung Myung with a completely absent-minded expression.

‘I am insane.’

Why did he miss this crazy person? This devil who destroyed all peace with his mere presence? Did he really?

“Um... Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon... no, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.”

“What?”

“Information is like that... uh? It isn’t like it is right here when you want to know... uh? It isn’t?”

“What are you even talking about! How can the Beggars Union have no information!?”

“That isn’t what I am saying... there is information, but it isn’t right here when you want it...”

“NOOO!”

Hong Dae-Kwang tightly shut his eyes.

“Anyway, these beggars aren’t helpful, they aren’t helpful! If you cannot even beg properly and you cannot even give proper information, what are you going to do to make a living!”

“... I appreciate your concern, but...”

He, of all people, didn’t need to worry about that, right? Uh?

However, Chung Myung kept pestering and nagging him, whether he knew Hong Dae-Kwang’s feelings or not.

“Ah, so what happened?”

Hong Dae-Kwang looked up at the ceiling with a relieved face.

There was much he wanted to say. If it wasn’t Chung Myung but someone else throwing this tantrum in front of him, Hong Dae-Kwang would have smiled broadly and said this:

‘Listen, little one. The information I requested from the main sect will take some time to arrive here via messenger. I cannot fly like a bird and get the information by myself, and nagging will not shorten the time, so just wait patiently; it won’t take long.’

Right. He could say that.

And if he said that, those who know the nature of the Beggars Union would nod or feel guilty for daring to nudge them.

That was if it was a person with common sense.

But unfortunately, the person in front of him right then was Chung Myung. This person didn’t even treat the Beggars Union’s dignity equal to a dog’s bone rolling on the ground in the street, and he was also a person without common sense or reasoning.

If he said that, he would get cursed back, ‘You incompetent beggars! Since you are so free, you might as well beg for food!’

“Kuak...”

Or, he could just try to hit him, unable to control his anger.

As he watched Chung Myung’s face, which was turning redder and redder, his hair began to stand on end.

It felt like he was watching fire rush toward a gunpowder barrel. The moment the fire touched the drum, it would surely explode.

That face?

No, it was the Branch Leader.

‘Please! Please! Hurry up and look right, you bastard!’

Since he requested it so urgently, the Thousand Miles Blue Pigeon of the Beggars Union must be flying in with its wings about to fall off, carrying a letter with information in its claws.

Whether the branch was destroyed or not depended solely on the speed of its wings.

“Ugh!”

Finally, Chung Myung’s eyes began to roll back, and Hong Dae-Kwang was considering whether he should get out without looking back, given the imminent situation.

Piiiik!

“Here it comes!”

Hong Dae-Kwang finally jumped up from his place at the sound of the chirp.

He wouldn’t be this happy to see his deceased grandfather’s face again. In fact, Hong Dae-Kwang didn’t even know his father’s face, let alone his grandfather’s, but anyway, let us get this over with!

“Where is it?”

“Here! It is here!”

At that moment, a blue-winged pigeon flew in like lightning through the open window.

“Okay!”

Hong Dae-Kwang quickly reached out and grabbed it with his hand, opening the small message tied to its ankle.

As soon as he took out the tightly rolled letter, Chung Myung quickly snatched it.

“Mine!”

Chung Myung’s eyes, which had opened the letter, moved swiftly over the words.

“What does it say?”

“That...”

“Uh?”

After reading the letter, Chung Myung raised his head and looked at Hong Dae-Kwang. His expression was hard to describe.

“Mr. Beggar.”

“What?”

“... It seems like you guys are ruined.”

“...What? W-what are you saying now?”

“No, I just... thought it wouldn’t be....”

Chung Myung laughed nonchalantly and scratched the back of his head.

“There he is, a crazy guy.”

“Uh?”

Hong Dae-Kwang blankly stared at Chung Myung with a face that had no idea what was happening.

The mouth of the Yangtze River.

A bleak tension flowed around Plum Blossom Island, which was completely taken over by the pirates.

The street, which was once full of life, was nowhere to be seen. It was hard to find people coming and going, and even the bars that had opened their doors wide and were eagerly soliciting customers had shut down.

Those who had gathered quietly in the bars on the outskirts of the city, which had been created along with Plum Blossom Island, couldn’t help but hold their breath as they watched the crowds move.

“... Is it really necessary to continue like this?”

A man who saw the backs of the pirates passing by through the crack in the window sighed.

“Actually, there cannot be a big problem, right? The pirates aren’t hurting anyone.”

“It isn’t that there are no big problems. It is just that no problems have happened yet, you idiot.”

One of the merchants beat his chest as if hearing frustrating words.

“When a tiger is full, doesn’t it just ignore passing people? Those pirates are quiet now, but how do we know when and what they will do?”

“That is true, but...”

The merchants’ faces were filled with concern.

It had been several days since the pirates took control of Plum Blossom Island, but no one had been harmed yet.

However, it was impossible to look at the pirates, whom they had feared their entire lives, with a calm mind. This was especially true for merchants who made a living by traveling the Yangtze River.

“We have to get along somehow. In the past, when you met the pirates on the ship, didn’t you pay a toll to them? If you think about it, this is the same....”

“How is it the same? We will inevitably meet them briefly once every few weeks or months. But how are we supposed to live when we have to face them every day?”

“....”

“And back then, we only had to pay for the goods we were moving. But now they even collect taxes on the goods in the warehouse. Damn it, business isn’t going well, and if we pay the money they demand, how are we supposed to make a living here?”

A sigh escaped the merchant’s mouth.

Mount Hua, who had established Plum Blossom Island, just rented the warehouse to those who needed them and didn’t charge any taxes on their items. So, in reality, the only money the merchants paid while using Plum Blossom Island was the toll for transporting the goods on the ships.

Of course, there were costs for using various facilities, but wasn’t that the same everywhere in the world?

But those bastards took control of Plum Blossom Island and immediately doubled the toll and threatened to charge taxes on the cargo waiting inside the island.

So they had to pay twice as much on the toll, but in reality, they ended up paying 3 to 4 times more.

“Do we really have to put up with this?”

“Man. Yesterday, the East River Merchants left Plum Blossom Island and rode past the river.”

“The East River Merchants? Ah, right. What about them?”

“... they are all dead.”

“What!? What does that mean!?”

“Those damned bastards seem to be using their brains. They’re sinking all the merchant ships trying to cross the river on their own, away from Plum Blossom Island. If you don’t want to die, you have to use Plum Blossom Island.”

“... Oh my.”

Everyone froze, unable to continue speaking.

In the past, even if they encountered pirates while crossing the river, it was rare for people to get hurt. This was because the toll fee usually solved the problem.

But if those damned pirates were acting like this, no one would dare cross the river.

Of course, the Yangtze River was so wide that it was not like they were guaranteed to be caught by the pirates. The chance of running into them was one in ten.

But what kind of person would risk their fortune and life on a one-in-ten chance?

“... Now it looks like we will be completely wiped out.”

“It was better when Mount Hua was here.”

Even if they didn’t know where they were, the pirates did, and the merchants realized once again how much Mount Hua had protected them by ruling the island.

“What can we do by looking for those who aren’t here? So? Is there any news from the Nine Great Sects? How long has it been since those pirates took over this place, and those people haven’t shown their faces yet?”

Now, the only way for the merchants to survive was for the Nine Great Sects to take over this place. If not, they could end up with pirates wiping the place across the Yangtze River again.

“... I heard there is still no movement.”

“Even now?”

One of the merchants couldn’t hold back his anger and raised his voice.

“Surely, the Nine Great Sects aren’t just going to hand over this place to the pirates, right?”

“H-How could they do that? This is the Northern lands.”

“Then why haven’t they shown us their noses yet?”

“That is...”

The merchants looked at each other with anxious eyes.

“Didn’t they already get scared and retreat once during the war? Are you sure they will not do the same thing again?”

“Eh, as if! No matter what, they are people called the Justice Sect....”

“Justice Sect? If they had such a tag on them, they should have moved three years ago! Isn’t it true that the Nine Sects guys just bowed their heads to the enemy because they were afraid of losing their lives?”

“...”

“Not this too....”

“Don’t speak nonsense! Wasn’t the situation bad back then? If the Nine Great Sects moved in earnest, not only the pirates but even the Black Dragon King would have lost his head. What are you talking...”

Kwang!

At that moment, the door to the bar opened violently as if it would break, and a man in blue walked in.

“....”

A face full of vigor and a tight blue dress known for the pirates.

The inside of the tavern became as quiet as a graveyard.

“Hmm.”

The man looked around with cold eyes. All the merchants who met his gaze froze, unable to even take a deep breath.

The pirate opened his mouth.

“In here...”

His cruel eyes flashed fiercely.

“I thought I heard someone talking about Nine Sects?”

“T-that...”

“The Black Dragon King’s head would fall off?”

The blood drained from the merchant’s face in an instant. Who knew better than merchants how cruel the pirates could be?

“This is why unneeded kindness shouldn’t be shown. The Black Dragon King has been so considerate. Still, you don’t even recognize the kindness and just carelessly speak your mouth.”

Anger flashed across the man’s contorted face.

“I guess I will just have to kill a few of you to set an example for the others to come to their senses.”

“T-that isn’t what...”

“No! You misunderstand things!”

The merchants waved their hands in haste to make excuses, but the man pretended not to hear and gestured to them.

“Drag them out.”

“Yes!”

As the pirates swarmed in, the merchants screamed in confusion.

“I-it is a misunderstanding! Let me go! Let me go!”

“Please spare us!”

“Ehh! W-we...!”

The merchants caught by the pirates cried and pleaded, but the hands pulling them were cruel.

“Drag them out and kill them. The sin of daring to insult our Black Dragon King, even with their lives, would not be enough!”

The man who shouted clicked his tongue.

As he walked out of the bar, he mumbled.

“I don’t know why the Black Dragon King is showing mercy to these guys. If he just killed them all and took the money, that would be the end of it, whether they are merchants or sects.”

“Kill them and take them?”

At that moment, a loud voice came from behind the man.

Annoyance flashed across the man’s face. He clicked his tongue and looked back. Another fearless bastard....

Crack!

But at that moment, a vicious hand grabbed the man’s neck.

“Kuak!”

The man stuck his tongue out in sudden pain as his throat tightened. It was an incredible pressure that seemed as if it would squeeze and burst his throat at any moment.

His face quickly turned red, and he hurriedly looked at the person who grabbed his neck.

The moment he saw who it was, shock filled his eyes.

A pure white robe, contrasting black hair.

His eyes were like those of a tiger, and his thick face reminded him of a lion.

But what caught his attention more were the two letters, ‘Blue Sky,’ engraved boldly on the right of the chest.

“N-nam...kuak! Namgung...”

Kwaaang!

He grabbed his neck and slammed the pirate to the ground in one go. The sound was unbelievable, like that of a person hitting the ground. The pirate who was hurting the merchants just a moment ago was now in a bloody mess.

“Pirates.”

The one who dealt with the pirate in a single breath.

The pirates who saw his face let out voices mixed with despair and shock.

How could they forget that face?

Three years ago, the name that was almost absolute when they stood on equal footing with the pirate, Black Dragon King, echoed in the air.

“E-emperor... Sword Emperor!”

“You...”

A roar erupted from Namgung Hwang’s mouth at that moment.

“You filthy bastards! How dare you step on the North of the river in front of me! I will kill you all!”

It was the moment when the lion of Anhui, who had been crouching for three years, finally roared towards the sky.

Chapter 917: There He Was, That Crazy Guy! (2)

“Namgung Hwang?”

“The Emperor Sword?”

The Five Swords, who had followed Chung Myung down the mountain, were taken aback.

“Namgung Hwang led the Blue Sky Sword troops and went to the river mouth?”

“Shaolin called, but instead of heading to Shaolin, he went to Plum Blossom Island?”

“Seems like it.”

Baek Cheon looked at Chung Myung with a blank expression. Baek Cheon usually controlled his emotions well, but this time, the shock was overwhelming.

“... Why?”

Chung Myung smiled.

“Sasuk, if you want to lead people in the future, there’s one thing you need to understand.”

“What is that?”

Even though Baek Cheon asked with an anxious face, Chung Myung replied as if it were nothing.

“There’s nothing more futile than trying to understand why a crazy person does something crazy.”

“...”

“You probably don’t understand, so let me explain it again...”

“No, Chung Myung. I understood it right away.”

“... Uh?”

Yoon Jong quickly nodded from the side.

“I understand, too.”

“I definitely understand, too.”

“Same here.”

“... But why are you guys talking while looking at me?”

Who else should they look at while talking?

It was his story.

Yoon Jong, who seemed to be choosing his words carefully, spoke.

“If you think about it, there’s a reason. The Namgung family was humiliated at the river back then. I only saw it briefly, but considering the personality of the Emperor’s Sword, Namgung Hwang...”

“Right. He must have been sharpening his sword... no, his blade for this.”

Everyone shivered as they recalled Namgung Hwang swinging his sword with his eyes wide open. Remembering the incredible sword technique that couldn’t be fully described by the term’ sword technique,’ their bodies trembled and ached.

“Then now...”

“Yes.”

Chung Myung nodded with a smile.

“Wouldn’t he be going crazy? Hehehehe.”

Kwaaang!

An entire pavilion was blown out.

It was an impossible sight that would not have occurred even if a dozen military cannons had fallen. Yet, the creator of this spectacle was an iron sword, not even four feet long.

“You wicked people of the Evil Sects!”

A lion’s roar erupted from Namgung Hwang’s mouth.

“Drive out all those bastards who dared to step on the Northern Lands!”

“Yes!”

Namgung Hwang’s command to the Blue Sky Sword troops spurred them into action, charging into the light.

Just by observing their momentum, one could surmise how intensely they had trained over the past three years.

Namgung Hwang’s eyes radiated a terrifying presence.

“Black Dragon King!”

Three years.

A long time for some a brief period for others.

For Namgung Hwang, those years felt like an eternity.

He swallowed his humiliation, shame, and self-disappointment every single day.

The only thing that helped him endure that painful time, which felt as if his intestines were being torn apart, was the hope that after three years, he could sever the heads of the abominable Jang Ilso and the Black Dragon King with his own sword.

Amidst all that, he heard the news that the Black Dragon King, of the Four Precepts Alliance, had entered the Northern Lands with his pirates.

“That fucking bitch!”

Crack!

Namgung Hwang gritted his teeth.

If the name Namgung had remained in his mind, he would not have dared to cross the Yangtze River in such a manner, not even with the Four Precepts Alliance, let alone by himself.

When he regained his senses after his anger exploded, he found himself charging with his sword.

“Do-Wei!”

“Yes, lord!”

Namgung Do-Wei.

The son of Namgung Hwang, who had endured literal bone-crushing training for the past three years, responded with a firm voice.

Three years was enough time for him to grow from a child into a potential full-grown swordsman and the next head of the Namgung family.

“Lead the Blue Sky Sword troops and turn all the evil sect bastards into fish food! Even if we suffered humiliation once, let the world know that the name of Blue Sky still holds justice and harmony!”

“Yes!”

As Namgung Do-Wei ran ahead, Namgung Hwang’s eyes stayed focused forward.

“Huh!”

Shaolin’s letter was definitely received. However, Namgung Hwang paid no attention to its contents.

‘Cowards!’

A person should know how to avoid death, but a warrior should not avoid death. Especially those who uphold the banner of justice and chivalry as their motto will lose everything the moment they step back because they fear losing their lives.

“You should have just died there.”

If those Wudang had not done such foolish things, if the Shaolin and Qingcheng Sect had not gone along with those foolish actions...

He would never have begged for his life so cowardly. And the brilliant name of the Blue Sky Namgung family would not have been dragged through the mud.

Kwang!

Namgung Hwang’s feet stomped on the ground.

“No, all of that is just an excuse.”

If Namgung Hwang had truly held the courage to disregard his life, if he wanted the Namgung family to become a sect that stakes everything in the name of justice, he would have raised the sword in that situation, too.

At the last moment, what weighed down on his sword and held it back was none other than Namgung Hwang’s weakness.

And that fact made Namgung Hwang more miserable.

“One cannot put back the spilled water. But!”

Namgung Hwang’s eyes shone brightly.

“We can pour new water in. Today, I will reclaim the name of Namgung!”

At that moment, he saw a horde of pirates rushing toward him with incredible momentum.

“Block them!”

“Damn it, do those bastards know where this is?”

“The Namgung family is nothing! Kill them all!”

Namgung Hwang, looking at the pirates rushing at him like a swarm of bees, grabbed his sword.

“I...”

Namgung Hwang stomped his foot hard on the ground. At the same time, the white-black qi emitted from the sword’s handle rose up.

The aura was so thick that it was hard to believe it was a black aura, completely enveloping Namgung Hwang’s sword and radiating a dazzling light.

Kwang!

It was the Step of the Emperor.

And that was the Sword of the Emperor’s Sword.

“Ohhhhhh!”

Namgung Hwang’s sword, raised above his head, came down in an instant.

Kwaaang!

One couldn’t believe that it was the sound of a sword being swung. It was literally like the sound of a cannon being fired.

But what was even more unbelievable was not the sound the sword made, but the size of the sword qi that it produced.

A white sword qi the size of a house was emitted from the tip of his sword. The faces of those who saw the huge sword qi that flew in, covering their entire field of vision, instantly lost their color.

“A-avoid...”

Before they could even finish the word ‘avoid’, the white sword qi covered the approaching enemies. Not only that, it extended beyond them, leaving a huge scar on the street.

Kwakwakwakng!

A power that you could not believe even if you saw it with your own eyes.

Even if you called it power, it was so amazing that they were dumbfounded. A human unleashed this tremendous qi from his sword?

It was a sight that forced them to understand why the Namgung family was called the Blue Sky Namgung family and was able to maintain its position as the leader of the Five Great Families, and why the head of the Namgung Family always proudly put his name on the list of candidates for the best swordsman in the world.

“You insignificant bastards of the Evil Sect! Do you know who I am and still choose to block the path?”

Namgung Hwang’s appearance, sweeping the front with a single sword and standing alone, roaring, made even the name of a Divine General seem so meaningless.

“Woahhh!”

“It is the Namgung family! The Namgung family has come to save us!”

“Blue Sky Namgung Family!”

“The Five Great Families have come!”

Those who had been hiding in relatively safe places and merely watching the scene unfold ran out and cheered when they witnessed it. Although the Namgung family had shown a disappointing appearance in the past, that was insignificant in this situation.

Seeing them come to the land and slaughter the pirates in person, all the ill feelings from the past were washed away, and faith began to fill its place.

“Do not come out! Those dirty pirates are still around! I will put your mind at ease soon!”

The people nodded their heads upon hearing those words and rushed back in. They instinctively felt that the wait wouldn’t be long.

“Hmm!”

When Namgung Hwang nodded and was about to walk ahead with a brisk step, a person who approached him cautiously opened his mouth.

“Hyung.”

“What?”

Namgung Hwang turned his head and looked at the person standing next to him.

The Sword of the Lion, Namgung Myung. He was the younger brother and leader of the Blue Sky Sword troops.

“Are you alright?”

“What are you talking about?”

“... I understand how you feel, hyung, but I feel like we acted too carelessly. I wonder if this could turn into something...”

“Namgung Myung!”

Namgung Hwang’s voice, which sounded like a cannon, made Namgung Myung shrink back.

“Since when did you turn into such a coward?”

“T-that isn’t what...”

“Just know this!”

Namgung Hwang looked at Namgung Myung with eyes that seemed like he wanted to kill.

“In a world where fools live, the only wise one is treated as an idiot.”

“... Uh?”

“What more reason does a swordsman need to defeat Evil bastards?”

There wasn’t a single doubt in the voice of Namgung Hwang. Just conviction.

“We suffered because we couldn’t take the obvious for granted! Because we couldn’t say what was right and what was wrong out loud!”

“L-Lord. I just...”

“Shut up!”

Namgung Hwang glared at Namgung Myung as if he wanted to devour him.

Of course, Namgung Hwang also understood Namgung Myung’s concern about the damage the Namgung family might suffer due to it.

But now he knew.

A sect that valued profit would only last a hundred years.

In order to continue its value for thousands or tens of thousands of years, something more than profit was needed. Those who were blinded by small things and could not see big things would eventually perish.

At that moment, the image of someone crossed Namgung Hwang’s mind.

A small swordsman covered in wounds.

A person who was defiant even with blood all over his body.

At that time, Namgung Hwang couldn’t look that young swordsman in the eye. He didn’t dare to look him in the eye. When the young swordsman looked back at him, he just unknowingly avoided the gaze.

What had pushed Namgung Hwang into true pain over the years was the shame he felt for not being able to look him in the eye at that moment.

“Never again...”

Namgung Hwang clenched his fist.

“I will never avoid looking into another’s eyes again. I will never do anything that I would be ashamed of! I am Namgung Hwang! The head of the Namgung family and the Emperor’s Sword!”

“Hyung...”

“If you have time to worry, then take your sword up. The Namgung family’s swordsmen speak with their swords and not their mouths!”

“Understood, lord!”

Namgung Myung’s eyes turned determined.

The right and wrong here were still unknown. But one thing was certain: his lord was now risking everything there.

Then, as his younger brother, a member of the Namgung family and the proud leader of the Blue Sky Sword troops, he had to respond to the will.

“Let’s go!”

“Yes!”

Namgung Hwang grabbed his sword as he watched the horde of black pirates rush toward him from the dock.

“Defeat the Evil Sect people!”

White sword qi shot out from his blade and soared high into the sky.

To the clearest of skies.

Chapter 918: There He Was, That Crazy Guy! (3)

The pirates, brandishing their weapons, clashed with the Blue Sky Swordsmen and shouted.

“These guys who begged for their lives and lived are shamelessly showing their faces here again! Those Justice faction guys don’t even know shame!”

“If it were me, I would bite my tongue and die!”

The pirates faced the Blue Sky Swordsmen, mocking and ridiculing the Namgung family. The Blue Sky Swordsmen, standing there with firm resolve, couldn’t help but blush at those words.

They still had vivid memories of the moment they suffered in this very place, the moment they begged for their lives from the despicable Evil Sect!

“Hold your ground!”

Just as their swords were about to exert excessive strength due to the humiliation, a loud voice erupted from behind them.

The Namgung family’s young lord, Namgung Do-Wei, was running forward like a ray of light, holding the Namgung family’s signature iron sword.

“It is shameful to commit a mistake, but it is even more shameful to be caught up in it and not make up for it! Don’t be ashamed of standing here; be thankful that you can stand here!”

At those words, the Blue Sky Swordsmen’s eyes sharpened like blades.

How could they forget the humiliation? That misery of having to break their will to survive?

For the past three years, the Blue Sky Swordsmen had swung their swords only for this moment. For the moment, to redeem the humiliation and restore the spirit of the Namgung family!

“DIEEEE!”

At that moment, a menacing pole sword flew towards Namgung Do-Wei’s face. Namgung Do-Wei’s dark eyebrows twitched.

Kwaaaah!

His sword, swung like lightning, instantly cut the flying pole sword in two and sank into the body of the pirate who wielded the weapon.

“Kuak!”

The pirate’s face was filled with shock for a moment.

Crack!

The sword, thrust with great force, instantly split the pirate’s body in two. The pirate’s torn body flew apart, splattering red blood everywhere.

Even as the blood rained down, Namgung Do-wei shouted without blinking.

“I will lead! The Blue Sky Swordsmen, follow me!”

“Yes!”

Namgung Do-wei charged forward without hesitation.

His determined face no longer showed the same fierceness as before.

‘I am a worthless person.’

He thought he could catch up, and that would be enough. He might be behind the Mount Hua Swordsmen now, but he was also a man of the Namgung family. He believed that if he tried and tried again, he would one day stand on the same level as that man.

He was confident he could work harder than anyone else and endure more persistently than anyone else.

So he thought that was enough.

Now, he believed he could run a little faster to catch up to the talent that Chung Myung had, and if he kept his bearing for the rest of his life, he would eventually stand in Chung Myung’s place.

But...

‘That isn’t even funny.’

Now, he knew how foolish that thought was.

‘That is not something you can do just because you have strength.’

If Namgung Do-wei had been as strong as Chung Myung, would he have been able to confront Jang Ilso there?

If he had been that strong, would he have fought alone in that place where everyone was paralyzed by fear for their own lives?

Not even a single answer would be ‘yes.’ It was impossible for Namgung Do-wei.

In fact, he froze, unable to act even when Chung Myung faced Jang Ilso, when peace was made, and when Chung Myung’s fist slammed into the face of Wudang’s sect leader.

It was an embarrassing thing for the sword of the Namgung family, which existed to protect the people’s morale.

‘He isn’t that kind of person because he is strong. And that is why he is strong.’

He could excuse being defeated in martial arts, but there was no room for excuses for being defeated as a human.

Whether they were strong or weak, they only had one life. Whether they could risk that life had nothing to do with being incompetent.

That day, Namgung Do-wei realized to his bones how foolish he had been.

“I will not say that I am the strongest!”

This was something he said to the person guarding him and, at the same time, to himself.

“It is okay to lose with your sword! But at least don’t lose your spirit! We are the Blue Sky Namgung Family!”

“Yes!”

The Blue Sky Swordsmen dashed ahead towards the pirates in one breath. Their sword, more resolute than ever, began to slaughter the pirates in an instant.

At the forefront, Namgung Do-wei’s sword was shining brighter than anyone else’s.

The corners of the abbot’s lips trembled. His eyes, looking at Beop Gye, were filled with shock and anger.

“...is this true?”

“Yes, abbot! I just got word that Namgung Hwang, the lord of the Namgung Family, is leading the Blue Sky Swordsmen and rushing to the mouth of the river.”

The Gentle Crow Beggar glanced towards the door. The beggar who had told Beop Gye of this news was leaning forward, unable to enter the room, and the moment his eyes met with Gentle Crow Beggar, he nodded.

It meant that it was all true.

“Namgung Hwang. Namgung Hwang is...”

The Gentle Crow Beggar, who had been mumbling softly, laughed. His face looked more empty than amused.

‘With that man’s temperament... Right, it is possible for him to do this.’

If he were to judge on his violent temper, Namgung Hwang was second to none in the world.

He must have been seething inside from the humiliation he had suffered in the Yangtze River War three years ago, and now that he had heard the news that the Four Precepts Alliance had crossed into the Yangtze River and set foot on the northern lands...

The Gentle Crow Beggar let out a deep sigh.

This was clearly a mistake on the Beggars Union’s side. Information by itself held no meaning. It was only meaningful when you could predict what would happen later based on that information.

He should have expected that something like this could happen when the Four Precepts Alliance set foot on the Northern lands, but he realized it belatedly after hearing the news that the Namgung family had moved.

‘No, no. How many people in the world could have predicted something like that in such an urgent situation?’

Since he had to pay attention to everything happening in the world, he had no choice but to postpone the reaction of the sect, which had gone into seclusion for a while.

As he was trying to soothe his bitter emotions by making excuses that weren’t excuses, the angry voice of the abbot pierced the ears of the Gentle Crow Beggar.

“What on earth is Lord Namgung thinking! He went to the Yangtze River! Is he going to fight the Four Precepts Alliance all by himself?”

The abbot looked at Beop Gye with a face that screamed his disbelief. Even if he yelled at him, the situation wouldn’t change, but he couldn’t handle the situation that was starting to run wild and break free of his control.

‘He is losing it.’

Where was this place, where one sect decided to attack on its own?

At the mouth of the river. No, Plum Blossom Island was literally a powder keg. The eyes of the entire Murim world were focused on it, and the conflicts of all the forces were focused there.

It was hard to understand how that insignificant island could become such an important place, but it was an undeniable fact that it had become a natural hotbed where the pride and interests of the Nine Great Sects and Four Precepts Alliance clashed.

But how could they attack such a place without any consultation with others?

If this incident were to lead to an all-out war between the Four Precepts Alliance and the Nine Great Sects, how would they handle the aftermath?

“What on earth was he thinking! Just what!”

Kakak!

The prayer beads in the abbot’s hands rubbed together with a sharp sound. It felt as if everything in the world was slipping out of his control.

“If this incident causes the entire world to suffer, the Namgung family will have to bear all the blame for the sins! How could someone who is the head of a family and the leader of the Five Great Families be so reckless!”

Angry voices erupted one after another, but no one dared to speak up.

However, the Gentle Crow Beggar couldn’t help but sigh.

Reckless?

Right. Maybe it was true.

But at least in the Murim world, such behavior wasn’t considered reckless.

‘It is called courageous.’

Wasn’t fighting evil without caring about one’s own harm or personal interests the very definition of valor?

The Namgung family did what those who used the name of Justice emphasized so much. Even so, if they started by criticizing him like this, it would be admitting that everything they had emphasized so far was nothing more than a façade.

The Gentle Crow Beggar, who couldn’t bear to listen to the abbot’s criticism any longer, opened his mouth.

“Abbot.”

The abbot looked at the Gentle Crow Beggar, unable to wipe the anger from his face.

“It is true that the Namgung family did something unexpected, but there is no point in blaming him now. What is important is what happens from now on.”

He paused for a moment so that the abbot could sort out his confusion. Then he spoke in a steady voice.

“What do you plan to do now?”

It was a question that pierced his heart.

“The Namgung family has opened up the floodgates. Now we must choose. Will we help them and destroy the pirates, or will we declare that it was the Namgung family’s arbitrary actions and stand aside?”

The abbot closed his eyes tightly at the question.

If he thought about it logically, supporting the Namgung family would be the right decision. But what if they supported the Namgung family, and then the Four Precepts Alliance came rushing into the Yangtze River?

‘From then on, it would truly be an all-out war.’

Once an all-out war started, they could not stop it with their own strength. Whether they won or lost, they would inevitably suffer enormous damage.

“... Elder.”

The abbot’s response was delayed, so Beop Gye spoke first.

“Isn’t it possible that all of this is Jang Ilso’s plan?”

“You think it is a plan?”

“Yes, a plan. Didn’t everyone get dragged into the Yangtze River three years ago with the movement of the same pirates?”

The Gentle Crow Beggar unconsciously frowned, but he wiped off the expression as if he had never shown it. His true feelings were not as such.

‘That is because they were blinded by ambition for fame and fortune and rushed to the Yangtze River without considering what was right or wrong!’

There was a lot he wanted to say, but he had no choice but to hold back. This was Shaolin, and the person in front of him was the abbot and elder of Shaolin.

“That probably won’t be the case.”

“Why? If it is Jang Ilso...”

“It would take too long to explain, but now is not the time to waste time talking about such things. In any case, everything that is happening now is most likely the Black Dragon King’s sole decision.”

At that moment, a hollow laugh escaped from the abbot’s mouth.

“Sole decision....”

It was ridiculous.

‘These two impatient people are driving the situation to hell.’

The Black Dragon King and the Emperor’s Sword.

It cannot be denied that both were significant figures with solid positions in their forces. However, that didn’t mean that the influence of those two people was great enough to shake up the situation in the Central Plains.

However, coincidentally, due to the unilateral actions of two individuals occurring simultaneously, both the Nine Great Sects and the Precepts Sects Alliance were drawn into an unwanted war at an inopportune time.

‘Is this what war is like?’

At least, that wasn’t what the abbot thought. The war he knew involved leaders clashing fiercely with their own forces.

But the current state of the world was entirely different from what he knew. Everyone was acting as they pleased. How on earth could these situations even lead to an all-out war?

“I don’t know why you are worried.”

At that moment, Peng Yeop frowned and said,

“I don’t like that the arrogant Namgung family is acting on its own, but if the situation has already unfolded, the only option left is to support them.”

However, Jong Ri-Hyung seemed to disagree with that idea.

“I think Lord Peng needs to calm down a little. If we rush to the Yangtze River now, the other Four Precepts Alliance members, not just the pirates, will have no choice but to move. Then it will be truly irreversible.”

“But if we just watch and the Four Precepts Alliance moves first, it will be too late to support. The Namgung family will collapse first.”

“I know. I know, but it isn’t something we can act on rashly.”

“How can this be rash!”

The two men’s argument quickly ignited, and the abbot’s face darkened.

Common sense dictated that the priority should be to support the Namgung family and drive out the pirates. If the Peng family and Kongtong helped, it wouldn’t be that difficult.

But what if the Four Precepts Alliance moved north, and an all-out war broke out?

Would the sects in this place really be able to deal with the Four Precepts Alliance? The Four Precepts Alliance, led by Jang Ilso?

‘We are lacking.’

If both the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families had supported Shaolin, there would be no need to hesitate. But right now, it was difficult to handle the Four Precepts Alliance.

Even if they somehow won, they would have to face the remnants who had been observing the situation and were planning to take on both sides.

The abbot, lost in thought, bit his lip tightly.

‘Why are you hesitating so much?’

Shame was his burden, but death was for others. Then, shouldn’t they, as Buddhists, naturally choose shame?

“Anyway...”

When he finally spoke, everyone’s eyes were on him. It seemed as if his words still held power.

“Even if we rush to the Yangtze River right now, it would be too late. By then, the decision would have been made between Namgung and the pirates.”

“Hmm. That is true.”

“Then, we will have to prepare for the aftermath.”

The abbot slowly rose from his place.

“Give me until the end of the day.”

“You said the end of the day?”

Gentle Crow Beggars looked at the abbot, puzzled. What could he change in a day in this situation?

“Yes. One day is enough.”

“Excuse me, abbot. What do you plan to do?”

“... I will head to Shaanxi.”

“S-Shaanxi? Could it be...?”

Everyone’s eyes widened. The abbot quietly nodded.

“Yes.”

His eyes were unwavering and firm.

“I must meet the Heavenly Friends Alliance and Mount Hua’s Sword Saint in person, right now.”

Chapter 919: There He Was, That Crazy Guy! (4)

Wooong!

White qi swirled from the tip of the sword. The enhanced qi erupted in an instant, sweeping away the front.

Kwaang!

Namgung Hwang shouted at the top of his lungs after sweeping away the pirates rushing in from the front with a single blow.

“Black Dragon King!”

A voice like the roar of a lion.

That voice instantly broke the enemies’ will to fight and raised the morale of his allies.

“Where are you, Black Dragon King! Did you run back in fear? Come out here right now! I want to settle the score!”

Kwaang!

The steps of Namgung Hwang shook the ground.

‘That monster!’

At that terrifying force, the face of the Underworld Old Monster, Yeo Dal, who was the guardian of the pirates, turned pale.

It was known that the Namgung family pursued the path of a blade, unlike any other sword sects that used the sword, but even in the Namgung family, the sword of Namgung Hwang was known to be unique and special.

It was like firing a cannon with a sword.

‘The path of the Peng family would be gentler!’

He had already seen the sword with his own eyes once, but it felt completely different then.

He didn’t know if it was because Namgung Hwang’s martial arts had improved dramatically over the past three years, or because he was not the Black Dragon King. Yeo Dal hadn’t yet reached the level where he could know that.

“Black Dragon KINGGGGG!”

Kwaang!

“D-damn it!”

Looking at the shattered and flying pavilion, Yeo Dal eventually shouted urgently.

“Retreat! Retreat right now!”

“G-Guardian! The Black Dragon King told us to defend this place no matter what!”

“You idiots! Even if we block them, we cannot defend them! And who is going to fight Namgung Hwang? The Black Dragon King isn’t here right now!”

“T-that...!”

“Shut up and tell them to retreat immediately! Before you all die!”

If the power of the pirates had gathered there, Yeo Dal wouldn’t have chosen to retreat. No matter how great the Namgung Family was, weren’t the pirates also one of the Five Sects in Shenzhou?

In a small-scale battle between warriors, it would be difficult to defeat the Namgung family. Still, in an all-out war, the pirates could actually overwhelm them.

However, they were currently lacking such troops.

The Black Dragon King was attacking merchant ships on the Yangtze River as soon as they appeared to increase the amount of goods going to and from Plum Blossom Island. In other words, there were more ships than usual on the Yangtze River.

Naturally, the number of ships guarding the place had to be small.

“Get back! Get back immediately! Get on the ship! We are retreating to the island!”

Yeo Dal clenched his teeth.

“This damned Namgung family!”

If he gave up the dock and retreated to Plum Blossom Island, the Black Dragon King would not leave him alone. It was clear that he had failed to carry out his orders.

‘Still, it is a whole lot better than dying.’

The wrath of the Black Dragon King in the distance was more frightening than the sword of Namgung Hwang in front of him.

Kwaang!

At that moment, several more pirates were sent flying, blood-drenched by the sword of Namgung Hwang.

“Retreat! Hurry upppp!”

At his order, the pirates turned around and ran.

In fact, even before he gave the order, they had all been thinking about running away somehow.

Namgung Hwang’s skills were truly overwhelming. In the first place, it was not something that the people gathered there could deal with.

‘How could these seclusion sect bastards even move this quickly?’

The reason the Black Dragon King decided to vacate the place, even though he knew there weren’t enough troops there, was because he thought it would take at least 10 days for the sect, who could barely move after talking for a long time, to proceed right.

But who would have imagined that the Namgung Family would suddenly appear at this time?

“Sir Guardian!”

“I know!”

Yeo Dal nodded. He couldn’t hesitate any longer. If he delayed any further, he risked losing his head.

“Start the ship! Get on board immediately...”

But Yeo Dal’s eyes, as he gave orders, suddenly widened in shock.

Through the large pavilions, on a different path from Namgung Hwang, warriors were rushing in. It was the Blue Sky Swordsmen.

At the forefront, a young warrior was seen swinging his sword like lightning. At first glance, he didn’t seem like someone who could deal with them.

“Abandon the ship, abandon that ship! Damn it, we will leave that ship behind! Just jump in! Swim to the island!”

There was no time to leisurely start the ship. Those who had launched the ship early managed to board it, but among the ships anchored at the dock, only two made it to the shore.

Realizing there was no time to board a ship, the pirates rushed into the water.

“Get out of the way!”

“Don’t grab me, you moron! Let go!”

“You bastards!”

It was a state of horror beyond words.

This was true for both the warriors and soldiers. When they have orders in their minds and something to protect, they do not spare their own lives.

But the moment that control disappears, all that remains are a few hundred cowards whose most important task is to survive.

The pirates jumped into the river, pulling and pushing their comrades. Many fell as they tried to get ahead of the others, and many reflexively swung their weapons to the side.

“Ackkk!”

“T-this son of a bitch, stabbed me?”

If they had retreated in an orderly manner, they would have already escaped. Still, they delayed the situation even more by trying to get ahead just a little. And the Namgung family’s swords coldly pierced their backs.

“ACKKKK!”

“Run away! The Namgung Family is coming!”

Splash! Splash!

The pirates who had jumped into the water skillfully swam towards Plum Blossom Island, moving swiftly without looking back. However, before the others could leap into the sea, those struck by the Namgung family’s swords screamed and collapsed helplessly on the spot.

Euk.

“Hmph!”

As Namgung Hwang’s feet touched the water’s edge, the pirates split into two groups: those who had jumped into the water and escaped quickly, and those left behind without reaching the water.

Namgung Hwang’s eyes were filled with anger.

“These fucking morons!”

A landslide victory.

It was a landslide victory that couldn’t even be described as one-sided. But that fact did not bring Namgung Hwang any joy. It only increased his disgust for having made peace with such incompetent individuals.

“This is...!”

Namgung Hwang’s eyes turned to the two ships, turning their bows.

“Get out of my way!”

Namgung Hwang, who had been guarding the side, pulled back his sword and raised it powerfully above his head.

Wooong!

The thick white sword qi swirled around his sword once again.

The sword qi, which had risen a little over a foot above his head, gathered around the sword.

“N-NO!”

“JUMPPP! NOWWWWW!”

The pirates who saw the scene from the ship screamed in confusion and jumped off the ship. They had already seen what happened when the white qi gathered around the sword.

Kwaang!

Namgung Hwang, who had stepped hard on the ground, immediately lowered the sword vertically. The qi that was released shot out like a cannon and flew straight towards the ship.

Kwaaang!
