It would have sounded better if they hadn’t gotten onto the carts beforehand.

Sect leader, you seem quite comfortable, right?

“There is no time! Hurry up and move.”

“....”

The disciples of Mount Hua walked with faces like cows being dragged to a slaughterhouse and grabbed the carts. At that moment, the familiar feeling they felt at their fingertips made them even more upset.

Ah... even after 3 years, this wasn’t changing.

“Hahahaha.”

Hwang Jongi, who was watching it from the side, laughed with satisfaction.

“Originally, the handle should have been bigger, but since it is a cart for the people of Mount Hua, I paid special attention to it. I made the handle the same thickness as the handle of the sword you use, so you won’t have any inconvenience in using it.”

... I am so grateful that it brings tears to my eyes.

You are so considerate, merchant lord.

“Okay!”

Chung Myung jumped slightly and climbed onto the cart.

“Okay, then we will go all the way to the Yangtze River! Let us go, run! Dong-Ryong!”

“Fuck!”

“Let’s go!”

As soon as Chung Myung’s words fell, the large and heavy carts rushed out at an incredible speed that made their weight seem insignificant.

“The last one to arrive will be used for wheels, so run as fast as you can!”

The groaning curses and screams echoed loudly. The carts, which had picked up speed like that, vanished into the distance.

“Hehehe.”

The remaining person, Hwang Jongi, burst into laughter.

“I wish you good luck.”

Hwang Jongi gave a deep bow towards the dust clouds in the distance.

Kwaaang!!

Namgung Do-Wei’s hands were shaking. His mouth was stinging, and his whole body felt heavy, like wet, soaked-up cotton.

But he wasn’t even given time to rest.

“Taaat!”

He jumped up from the place and knocked the flying cannon in one go.

Kwaang!

The cannon and the sword collided, creating a loud explosion.

“Kuak.”

That kind of cannon was nothing. If he had been in perfect condition, he could have thrown it away like a feather.

But that cannon was too heavy and burdensome for him right now.

‘Fucking shit...’

Now, he felt like he knew what it meant to starve and die.

They never rushed. They didn’t fire enough to clearly corner them, and they didn’t land more troops than necessary. They just attacked the riverside slowly enough that they didn’t have time to get our butts on the ground.

In short, they were very lukewarm attacks.

But as the attacks continued for several days, he couldn’t help but think he would rather fight them head-on than be defeated slowly and annihilated.

Slowly but surely, they were eating away at the Namgung Family.

Kwaang!

“Ackkkk!”

Someone’s scream was heard from somewhere.

Just two days ago, everyone would have turned their heads in panic at the scream and let out an angry shout.

But not now. They were used to such things now.

It had become a common sight on Plum Blossom Island for someone whose concentration was disturbed to be hit by a shelling they would normally avoid without much difficulty.

Namgung Do-Wei looked back with a contorted face.

The emotion visible in the expressions of the Blue Sky Swordsmen wasn’t despair or anger.

It was apathy.

The faces once filled with passion were no longer there. They were more like the faces of those dragged to an unknown land and forced to work there.

The resignation shown on the faces of the Blue Sky Swordsmen in the face of the helpless situation was evident.

‘We should have attacked.’

It wasn’t that he didn’t understand the decision of Namgung Hwang. However, even if he did understand it, it didn’t mean he thought it was the best option.

‘What should I have done?’

What should he do next?

At that moment, another shell fell on the island. Namgung Do-Wei caught sight of the Blue Sky Swordsmen standing helplessly, unable to even react.

It was the moment when the face of the archer turned pale as he discovered the shell coming towards them.

Before his mind even processed it, his body moved.

Kwaaaang!

Namgung Do-Wei rushed in like lightning and struck the cannon, gritting his teeth. Perhaps because he was swinging his sword in a hurry and was unable to properly use his internal qi, his hand holding the sword was torn, and blood dripped down.

“Y-Young lord...”

Tak!

Namgung Do-Wei grabbed the shoulder of the archer who had barely escaped the crisis.

The man, expecting to be reprimanded, tightly closed his eyes.

“Just endure it a bit more!”

However, instead of a shout, Namgung Do-Wei offered words of encouragement.

His voice, markedly different from Namgung Hwang’s, echoed across the island.

“Grit your teeth! We are the Namgung family! We may fight to the death, but we are not allowed to give up on our own!”

“Young lord!”

“We will get our chance! If the chance doesn’t come, we will make it! I will not let you die here without swinging your sword one last time! That is why!”

Namgung Do-Wei shouted with a voice full of rage.

“Trust me, and hold on a bit longer! Until the moment when we repay them for this pain and humiliation!”

The eyes of the Namgung family swordsmen, which had been lifeless, gradually brightened.

They nodded, and with bloodshot eyes, they glared at the ships surrounding the island.

Clench.

Namgung Do-Wei’s eyes glared at the Black Dragon Ship floating on the Yangtze River, biting his lip until blood was visible.

He was truly a dignified sight to behold.

But his fingertips, desperately hidden inside his long sleeves, were trembling.

‘Anyone, please... Please hurry up.’

Before this faint spark he had barely revived went out...

Chapter 940: Were You Expecting Anything? (5)

The Black Dragon King’s mouth twisted slightly as he stood on the deck, gazing at Plum Blossom Island.

“The situation is?”

“It is progressing steadily. Soon, all of their remaining energy to stand will be exhausted.”

“Hmm.”

The Black Dragon King snorted, clearly pleased with the report.

‘Emperor’s Sword.’

Sometimes, Namgung Hwang’s angry voice could be heard. Whenever he heard it, the Black Dragon King couldn’t suppress the smile that bloomed.

“What an idiot.”

How foolish must one be to dare to fight pirates on the water?

Now that they were on the water, they were no longer adversaries to the pirates. They were merely prey to be skillfully cooked and devoured.

“Black Dragon King. Since the enemies seem exhausted enough, why don’t we start tightening the reins...?”

“What nonsense.”

The Black Dragon King waved his hand dismissively.

“This is fishing.”

A mocking smile appeared on his lips.

“When catching a big fish, one shouldn’t show off strength. If you try to fight with force, the fishing line will break, and you will most likely lose the fish you have finally caught. What is important in fishing is not strength but patience.”

The Black Dragon King’s eyes glared at Plum Blossom Island. It was the gaze of a predator waiting for his moment.

“Just relax a little more. No matter how big the fish is, if it loses strength, it will be dragged up onto the ship.”

He gestured at Plum Blossom Island with his chin.

“Keep attacking! Just like releasing and tightening a fishing line, attack again when the tension is relieved.”

“Yes!”

“Hehehe.”

A triumphant air flowed from the Black Dragon King’s eyes.

‘Even so, he was just a young whelp raised in a secure greenhouse.’

He acknowledged the skills of Namgung Hwang. His courage and innate tyranny were enough to make even the Black Dragon King feel threatened.

However, this was the world of murim. It wasn’t a world where one’s strength alone could determine victory.

The Namgung family must be keenly aware of the feelings of a fish caught on the hook.

‘The more they resist, the deeper the needle digs in, and the more strength is lost.’

The needle called the Yangtze River was already deeply embedded in the Namgung family’s throat. There was no way to pull it out on one’s own.

Hunger, fatigue, fear, and faint hope.

All of that would be pushing the Namgung family to the bottom of hell right now.

‘Two days at most.’

It wasn’t good to drag things out too much. There was a time for everything. If the Black Dragon King’s calculations were right, if he just relaxed his strength for another two or more days, he could completely destroy the Namgung family without any damage, all by himself, without even waiting for support.

“Continue with the attack!”

“Yes!”

The Black Dragon King laughed softly.

“Namgung Hwang... I will personally cut off your head.”

His burning eyes looked as if they would gulp down Plum Blossom Island at any moment.

Meanwhile, Namgung Hwang’s eyes were dark and sunken.

The dark shadows cast under his narrowed eyes seemed to tell how deep the agony was at the moment.

“... You have no intention of attacking.”

“Lord....”

Namgung Hwang sneered at himself as he looked at the ships still surrounding the island. When he turned his gaze, he saw Namgung family members who had collapsed from exhaustion.

“...”

After staring at them silently for a long time, a sigh escaped Namgung Hwang’s mouth.

The attack finally stopped.

However, he knew. The reason they stopped attacking wasn’t because they lacked strength. They knew that it was much more effective to shake them up by repeatedly attacking and leaving them alone than to keep charging.

“Namgung Myung.”

“Yes... Hyung.”

Namgung Myung, who had briefly considered calling him Lord, ended up calling him ‘hyung’. This was because he guessed that the person Namgung Hwang was looking for wasn’t the leader of the Blue Sky warriors, but his younger brother Namgung Myung.

“Was I wrong?”

Namgung Hwang said in a voice which was mocking himself.

“What Do-Wei said. You have to kill Black Dragon King. Will you still have the strength to do it? if not, you will end up exhausted and die.”

“Hyung...”

“It is happening just as he said.”

The Namgung family swordsmen who had slumped down looked like they no longer had the strength to lift their swords. They were barely able to maintain their pride by not falling into the ground.

But how long would they last?

Everyone was already pushing to their limit. Even Namgung Hwang was dizzy from the fatigue and exhaustion that was coming, so how much worse must it be for them?

“Tell me, Namgung Myung.”

“...”

“Was I wrong?”

“H-Hyung!”

“That isn’t true. Your choice was the best. No lord of a family would risk his family’s life and go on an adventure!”

“....”

“It isn’t over yet. If you hold on a little longer... if you hold a little longer, support will definitely come!”

“Support huh...”

Namgung Hwang smiled softly.

“What if it doesn’t come?”

“T-that...”

Namgung Myung couldn’t bring himself to answer. He knew the result, and so did Namgung Hwang.

“Hehehe.”

Namgung Hwang’s shoulders shook.

“I cannot stand it. I cannot stand it...”

Crack.

He clenched his fists so hard that his joints popped.

“What I really cannot stand is not death. It is not the destruction of the Namgung family.”

“...”

“Those people, those people who trusted in me for the past three years and endured the painful training... I cannot let them die as a swordsman, and I might let them die like they are being hunted down... I feel so resentful of this and guilty.”

Namgung Hwang’s voice trembled.

He couldn’t suppress his resentment and anger, nor the disgust he felt towards himself.

“Hyung...”

“Did I really make that choice for the sake of this family’s future?”

“... Uh?”

Though the words were directed at Namgung Myung, they were not truly a question for him. It was a question for himself.

“Could it be that I was just afraid?”

Namgung Myung held his breath, unable to offer even a feeble consolation. The weight of Namgung Hwang’s voice was overwhelming.

“Could it be that I was afraid of risking my family’s fate and their lives on my sword?”

Namgung Myung bit his lip instead.

He had never seen Namgung Hwang look so vulnerable. Despite their similar ages, Namgung Hwang had always seemed as formidable as the Great Mountain.

But now, Namgung Hwang was crumbling.

“Hyung! You cannot be like this. Hyung, you are the Lord of the Namgung family! Even if everything else in the world can be shaken, you alone cannot be shaken!”

“... Right. Right, that is what it means to be the Lord of the family.”

Namgung Hwang closed his eyes tightly.

Family Lord. The weight of that title was now pressing on him endlessly.

“I...”

Namgung Hwang spoke self-deprecatingly.

“There have been more than a few times when I regretted being born in this era.”

“....”

It was a somewhat random statement, but Namgung Myung didn’t interrupt him. Namgung Hwang simply needed someone to listen.

“In this peaceful era, there is no need to wield my sword.”

Namgung Hwang’s gaze turned to the distant sky.

“A hundred years ago, if I had been born in a time of chaos... I would have gained a reputation incomparable to what it is now, and I would have been confident that I could become the one who saved the world. I would have cut off the head of the Heavenly Demon with my own sword.”

“Hyung...”

“But...”

Namgung Hwang’s mouth burst into laughter.

“They have been fighting this war for years... enduring a hell incomparable to this, watching their own family and disciples cough up blood and have their throats slit next to them, yet they just fought and fought...”

“...”

“How could that have happened? How...how could their will and hearts not be broken?”

Namgung Hwang’s voice was listless. It was the first time in his life that he had felt so small.

“I cannot even muster the courage to cut off the Black Dragon King’s head and save my family... and chaos? Heavenly Demon? Hehehe.”

He shook his head with a laugh.

“I was just a one-day-old puppy who didn’t know his rightful place. That is all I was. All I could do was...”

His fists clenched as if they were going to burst.

“Hyung...”

“I know.”

However, before Namgung Myung could offer words of comfort, Namgung Hwang immediately shook his head.

“I know this isn’t the time to be like this.”

“....”

“Blue Sky leader.”

“Yes, lord.”

“The pirates will launch an all-out attack within a few days. They will try to finish us off by using all their strength.”

“Yes.”

“Until then, somehow, let the men take turns resting.”

Namgung Hwang clenched his teeth.

“At least we shouldn’t die without swinging our swords even once.”

“I will follow the orders!”

Namgung Myung ran towards the men. Namgung Hwang, who was staring at them, let out another deep sigh.

The attacks continued without stopping.

The Namgung Family, who had lost a place to hide, changed their strategy and formed a circle in the center of the island. Those on the outside protected those on the inside, and they took turns resting a little.

It was hard to rest comfortably in a situation where the cannons were flying in, and the pirates were slowly attacking. Still, they were able to preserve their stamina just by not having to swing their swords.

But that wasn’t very good.

Those who gathered inside were able to face their surroundings more objectively than before, and they couldn’t help but feel the bitter reality of how desperate their situation was.

A faint hope.

A desperate but endless belief that someone would definitely come to save them was all that sustained them.

But... one day passed, two days passed, and the third day came...

The rescue didn’t come.

“....”

Namgung Do-Wei opened his eyes with difficulty and looked at the setting sun.

The warm sunlight was indeed comforting, but now, even this was too much for him to bear.

‘... it didn’t come?’

He instinctively turned his head to look at the riverside, but he still couldn’t see anyone. The riverside was so empty that it couldn’t be more despondent.

“Cough.”

A dry cough escaped him. Even though he had drunk enough water, his entire body felt dried up.

‘Can I endure any longer?’

No, it didn’t matter if he could. He had to endure it no matter what.

Some were having a harder time than him.

Namgung Do-Wei instinctively grabbed his sword and drove it into the ground. Then he barely managed to get up.

His eyes, gleaming with anger, landed on the ships of the pirates. How could those ships surrounding Plum Blossom Island be so hateful...

“...!”

At that moment, Namgung Do-Wei’s eyes widened further.

“Uh....”

His mouth fell open and his eyes trembled.

“G-get up! Get up, everyone!”

A voice squeezed out.

The ships that had been circling around Plum Blossom Island and killing the Namgung family were all turning their bows toward the island!

Blood burst out of Namgung Do-Wei’s mouth. But he didn’t even notice as he shouted like he was cursing.

“Here it comes! Attack!”

This was the final attack of the pirates, an all-out assault to cut off the Namgung family’s life.

The long and drawn-out final chapter of the Yangtze River was unfolding.

Chapter 941: Were You Expecting Anything? (6)

Chaaak!

The ship sliced through the water. The pure white foam created by the rapid maneuver and acceleration instantly dyed the Yangtze River white.

The sight of dozens of ships simultaneously turning their bows and advancing was truly a spectacle to behold.

No, the word spectacle might not be right. For the Namgung family on Plum Blossom Island, this sight was ‘terrifying’ itself.

“Ah...”

The pirates were approaching. Those who bore the name Namgung had never feared an enemy. Instead, they cheered.

But what they felt now was not fighting spirit but despair.

They had never imagined a situation where they would lose confidence, thinking that they couldn’t win not because the enemy was strong but because they were not in perfect condition.

None of them knew.

How vicious a war could be.

The scenario where they faced each other at their best and overcame an enemy that was difficult to defeat with their current skills through negotiation and perseverance only appeared in stories.

“Get up!”

At that moment, a loud roar burst from Namgung Hwang’s mouth.

“Do not forget! It isn’t me or my comrades standing behind you who will see your last look! But the enemies who will see it!”

That voice painfully pierced everyone’s ears.

“Do not let them remember the Namgung family’s last appearance as swordsmen as helpless and scared! The last thing you must protect is the awe that the enemy shows you!”

Was that effective?

It wasn’t known. But what was certain was that the moment Namgung Hwang’s words to get up were spoken, the Namgung family swordsmen, who had collapsed from exhaustion, somehow rose.

“Wouldn’t it be better! The enemy is coming. Now, there are those who can be cut with that sword of ours. Smile! Let us prove that Namgung’s sword truly shows its power when facing the enemy!”

It was truly an evil voice.

That was the moment when Namgung Hwang was gathering his final fighting spirit. Some of the ships were rushing to the island when they suddenly turned sharply to the side.

“Uh?”

Kwaaang!

Before the ship’s turn was even complete, a cannon fired into Plum Blossom Island. It wasn’t an attack meant to be precise. It would be nice if it hit, but it didn’t have to. The intention was to break their morale and heighten their anxiety.

However, even the cannon fire, which was fired without much expectation, posed a great threat to the Namgung family, already at its limit.

Kwaang! Kwaang!

The cannon fire poured down all over the island.

The sky was clear, yet the cannons were dotting it, and the dust they raised forcibly brought a sense of reality to the minds that had been extremely hazy.

“Kuak.”

The Namgung family’s swordsmen gritted their teeth and took up their swords.

Right.

A warrior proved his life by how he died.

They knew that, too. The chances of them surviving this battle were slim. So shouldn’t they prove their life for the last time?

Kwaang! Kwaang!

The bombardment continued, but the eyes of the Namgung family swordsmen grew even colder.

The moment that ship arrived at the shore was the start!

All of the Namgung family swordsmen looked at the approaching ship, took deep breaths, and began to strengthen their resolve. But at that moment.

Paat!

Before they could even prepare themselves mentally, dozens of pirates rose from the water surface near the island, water splashing.

“W-what!”

“T-They are coming! Damn it!”

The pirates, who rose up as if to pierce the enemy’s defenses, ran at full speed without even shouting.

The Black Dragon King was indeed an experienced man. He quickly dispatched the assault team to Plum Blossom Island as if he wouldn’t give the Namgung family time to gather their fighting spirit.

Paat!

Judging by the speed at which they were rushing, it was clear they were not just ordinary pirates.

The pirates, with complexions hardened like iron armor, charged in front of the swordsmen, emitting a presence colder than the eternal ice of the North Sea.

“Blue Sky Sword troops! Guard the front!”

“Yes!”

The training ingrained in their bodies was effective in any situation. The Blue Sky troops, even when facing the limits of their endurance, moved their feet the instant the leader’s command was given.

The Blue Sky Sword troops stepped forward instantly and formed a battle formation.

If Chung Myung had witnessed this, he would have nodded in approval. The power of a famous family sect wasn’t proven solely by their swordsmanship. It was a will that demonstrated their history and name.

The current Namgung family was proving with their will that the name they had built was not in vain.

Kwaaang!

The cannon fire continued unabated.

In the midst of the battlefield, heated by the cannons, the Namgung family’s Blue Sky Sword troops and the pirates of the Black Dragon King clashed head-on.

Kakang!

The swords and blades, swords and harpoons, sent sparks flying.

It was different from before. The battles that had continued until then were fought with the knowledge that each side had a fallback. The pirates had no intention of pushing the Namgung family to the brink, and the Namgung family recognized that the pirates wouldn’t completely cut off their retreat.

But now it was different.

All that was left was the annihilation of one side.

It was a battle where everything was at stake, and one side would not stop until the other was entirely suffocated.

Kakakakak!

The tendons in their arms strained against their weapons. It was a sight that showed their determination not to be pushed back even an inch.

The ones who should have been pushed back were the Black Dragon pirates.

Blue Sky Swords troops. Those who bore that name were the elite of the Namgung family. How could the swords of those who lead the Blue Sky Sword Namgung family be so light?

But.

Kakakak!

“Kuak....”

As the confrontation dragged on, the Namgung family’s sword was pushed back more and more.

As if to prove that no matter how strong one’s will was, one could not overcome the limits of the body, the pirates’ sword and spear began to push the swords of the Namgung family away.

“Kuaak!”

“Ackkk!”

The blade, which seized the opportunity, finally cruelly cut through the weakened body.

Blood splashed everywhere, and shrill screams spread out. However, even when the spear pierced their stomachs and cut their shoulders, the Blue Sky Sword troops didn’t easily retreat.

Because they knew without anyone shouting it out.

That this was where they were supposed to die.

“Don’t mess around!”

One of the members let out a desperate scream and swung his sword.

Paat!

The sword flew in like a ray of light and pierced the neck of the pirate rushing at him from ahead.

“Gruek!”

The sword, which dug into the front of the neck, pierced through to the back and stuck out. The pirate, who looked down at the sword stuck in his neck, trembled as if he couldn’t believe what had just happened.

“Such a fool...”

Puak!

But at that moment.

The spear of a pirate, whose neck had been pierced, pierced the lower abdomen of the swordsman, who let his guard down for a moment.

The sound of air escaped from the mouths of the Blue Sky Sword troops. Their wills may have been strong, but they were still inexperienced young warriors on the battlefield. They overlooked the obvious fact that they should not let their guard down until their opponent was fully dead.

“Kuak....”

Paaat!

At that moment, the blade that flew in from behind slashed the neck of a member of the Blue Sky Sword troops, who was twisting his body. The head, with its eyes wide open, spun around and rose into the air.

“K-Kwang!!!”

“Namgung Kwanggg!”

The family was incredibly close.

Unlike sects where people with no close relation gather and share the same values, family sects grow together from childhood.

In other words, the shock received when someone was sacrificed was incomparable to that of a normal sect.

To some, he was a younger sibling; to others, an older one; and to others, a son. His head, raised into the air with its eyes open, caused tears of blood to flow from the hearts of those who had to witness it.

“You bastards!!!”

“EUHHHHH!”

As hatred and anger grew, harmony and morale disappeared from the battlefield.

All that remained was the blatant hostility to tear apart those in front of them.

The softly crafted stories meant to instill dreams in those who grew up holding swords began to crumble, revealing the true face of the battlefield.

Victory gained by sacrificing a son’s head.

Glory that was given as a sacrifice of a brother’s heart.

Who spoke of sacrifice?

Who talked about the sorrow behind it?

There was no praise given to those who had died. Glory belongs entirely to the living.

Someone had to know. Who shed the blood behind that glory? Behind all the glory, there were countless forgotten deaths.

But unfortunately, those who stood there were simply consumed by hostility, fear, and hatred, rushing at the enemy before their eyes.

“DIEEE!”

“EUKKKK!”

The swordsman, with bloodshot eyes, swung his sword at the enemies, screaming.

It was a fierce rejection. It was an incredibly violent sword attack, unbelievable for someone entirely drained of energy.

But the Black Dragon pirates were not flustered.

A cornered rat would bite a cat, too. But would a cat die from a rat’s bite? They had already anticipated that the opponent would resist fiercely. The important thing was to completely corner the enemy.

Unlike their initial fearsome charge, the Black Dragon pirates loosened the reins of their attacks. They never rushed but took their time with the approach.

Kung! Thud!

At that moment, the ship advancing towards Plum Blossom Island crashed into the sandbar and anchored, making this eerie sound.

Euk.

The huge ship, made of a black substance—the Black Dragon Ship, which symbolized the Black Dragon King—finally arrived at Plum Blossom Island.

Kwaang!

The loud sound pierced the ears of everyone.

It was a noise so loud that it made one doubt whether the ship could safely dock at that speed. However, the ripple effect of that sound was certainly amazing.

The Namgung family members’ bodies shrank as they confirmed with their ears, not their eyes, that the main force of the pirates had arrived.

Kuung!

After a while, someone slowly appeared at the bow of the Black Dragon Ship.

A magnificent figure that made people feel intimidated.

Tiger-like eyes.

Not just his skills but his appearance alone made those who watched him cringe, revealing his majestic nature.

“Those fucking bugs....”

Finally, the Black Dragon King, who had entered Plum Blossom Island, looked down at the Namgung family and bared his teeth.

“All those who dare to oppose me in this river, turn them into food for fish!”

“Yes, lord!”

As soon as the order was given, the battleships, which had been docked, began to pour out like ants.

It was a shocking sight.

On the ships that had taken over all four sides of the island and in the water, the swarm of pirates was endlessly pushing towards the island.

“Black Dragon KINGGGGG!”

Namgung Hwang’s roar escaped his mouth like a lion’s roar.

“Emperor’s Sword.”

However, the Black Dragon King only twisted the corners of his lips at the sound. Namgung Hwang fixed his eyes, red with hatred, and shouted.

“If you are a warrior, step forward! You should have the courage to fight a battle!”

“You are still an idiot, I see, Namgung Hwang.”

Black Dragon King openly sneered.

“Did you dare think that you were even worthy of fighting me?”

“You...”

“Stay there and fight until you die. I will watch your end with my own two eyes!”

“BLACK DRAGON KINNGGGG!”

“Die.”

The pirates began to rush at the bleeding big fish. As if they were going to tear everything apart and eat it up, leaving not a single bit of flesh behind.

Chapter 942: I Need To Show You What True Despair Is. (1)

The pirates surged forward, filling the riverbank like a relentless tide.

Were they like a pack of hyenas smelling blood? No, it was more fitting to call them a pack of sharks driven by the scent of blood.

Their eyes were wild with madness as they shouted evil cries, targeting the flesh of the Namgung family.

The battle lines were drawn.

The sight of the pirates, engulfed in madness and charging ahead while screaming like animals rather than humans, was enough to fill the hearts of those barely holding on with despair.

“Kaahhaaa!”

The rushing pirates finally leaped over the Namgung family’s heads. Their forms rose up, blocking the sun and casting long shadows over the Namgung family members as if foreshadowing their fate.

“Ahhhh!”

But the ones who were struck were none other than the Namgung family.

Even with despair looming over them, their instincts naturally guided them to the sword movements. Before they could think, their swords struck the pirates first.

Puak! Puak!

The Namgung family’s iron swords pierced the pirates’ bodies in unison. Blood gushed from the wounds, pouring down like rain on the Namgung family swordsmen.

The sensation of hot blood on their faces jolted the swordsmen from their dazed state.

But this was just the beginning.

“DIEEEEE!”

“Put a harpoon through their throats!”

The pirates, undeterred by the bloodshed of their comrades, rushed at the Namgung family with sharp blades without a moment’s hesitation.

The madness of the battlefield.

A person alone could not face death with dignity. However, when the heavy breathing of another was felt from behind, they forgot their own mortality.

The pirates charged in, over corpses and blades.

They were overpowering.

The Namgung family swordsmen desperately swung their swords to fend them off, but the pirates, in a fit of rage, swung their spears until their heads were severed.

“Ackkkk!”

The battlefield was a land ruled by morale. The swordsmen, overwhelmed by the momentum, couldn’t find their way to move and wield their swords. If they were in perfect condition, the situation might have been different, but the Namgung family’s swordsmen had already exhausted all their qi.

So... all that was left was to collapse.

Crack!

A sharp spear dug into a shoulder and emerged through the side.

“Kuak...”

A burning pain spread through the body.

However, the pirates’ grasp didn’t even allow him time to slowly savor the approach of death.

Crack! Crack! Crack!

The relentless spears continued to pierce his collapsing body. With dozens of holes, the swordsman fell on the spot, leaving not even a corpse intact.

Slash!

A violently swung sword shattered everything and tore through a neck. Yet, in response, a sword thrust out, deflecting a spear to the side and piercing a spleen.

The battle line suddenly disrupted, and the two camps tangled into chaos.

“Hold the defense line! Damn it, you cannot mix in!”

Namgung Myung shouted as if coughing up blood.

The Namgung family was outnumbered compared to the pirates.

Of course, each was a warrior whom the pirates dared not approach. However, this was not such a place. No matter how great one’s skills were, screams and deaths were rampant on the battlefield. How could one stop the weapons flying from behind in such chaos?

The moment the battle line collapsed, annihilation was inevitable.

“Stop it! Stop it!”

As soon as Namgung Myung shouted, he drew his sword and rushed forward. He knew it was not the time to give orders from behind but to step ahead and repair the collapsing battle lines.

As he moved, the elders who had been waiting also sprang forward in an instant.

Paat!

The sword swung like a lightning bolt.

“You cannot get past here until you kill me!”

“Come! Dogs of the waters!”

The elders were quite desperate.

The ones dying in front of them now were none other than their sons, grandsons, and disciples. Their swords, which had been clenched and held down even in the face of their own relatives’ deaths, finally had the chance to move and cut through the bodies of the pirates in one go.

“Ackkk!”

“C-cough! T-this fucking... bastard...”

However, the situation didn’t change as easily as expected.

The warriors, who had reached considerable growth, were swept away by the many pirates. The number of pirates advancing was so great that it was shocking.

Namgung Myung’s eyes were red.

‘If only it wasn’t here.’

If only this place wasn’t an island with nothing but a barren plain, they wouldn’t have been crowded like this. However, there was no place to hide or lean on the island. And now, those pirates were attacking them from all sides.

Even the buildings where they could hide were completely destroyed by the cannon fires. All that was left for them were the iron swords in one hand and their comrades leaning against their shoulders.

No, NO!

They still had something left.

“OHHH!”

Along with the roar that seemed like it would burst their eyes, a dazzling white sword qi fell on the heads of the pirates rushing ahead like cannons.

“Ahhh....”

The eyes of the pirates who had been rushing in without looking back turned blank for a moment. When their focus, which had been lost for a moment in shock, returned, their eyes began to fill with shock and fear, not madness.

Kwakwakwakwang!

The flying white sword Qi was sweeping away and crushing the pirates.

Just one sword.

With that one sword, at least 20 pirates were turned into chunks of meat without leaving behind a proper corpse. The sword qi that exploded as it sank into the ground didn’t leave even a single drop of blood on the floor.

Kwaaang!

The aftermath of the explosion swept through the surroundings. The pirates were each surprised by the spectacle and stopped in their tracks.

“What the hell is this...?”

“E-Emperor’s Sword!”

No one wanted to be swept away by the sword qi. However, the battlefield didn’t give them a moment to grasp the reality.

“Get out of my way, you morons!”

“Euhhhhh!”

Those who couldn’t properly see the power of the sword qi because of the backs of their comrades, densely packed in front of them, just kicked away those who stopped and advanced ahead like moths rushing to a flame.

KWAAANG!

Once again, the white sword qi was released.

Emperor Sword, Namgung Hwang.

He was exhausted. However, his sword clearly proved why he was called the Emperor’s Sword.

“DIEEEE!”

However, he, too, had just one body to use. The pirates surged in from all directions like swarms of ants, and his sword felt too short and his body too small to block them all.

“AHHHH!”

A convulsive scream burst out from Namgung Hwang’s mouth.

Kwang! Kwang! Kwaang!

Every time the sword was swung, the fierce sword qi surged like a storm.

“Black Dragon KIINGGGG!”

Namgung Hwang’s eyes were red.

“Are you going to come out only after all your subordinates die? You coward! A coward like you is the king of the Yangtze River? The world will laugh at you!”

The content was mocking, but the voice felt earnest. It was filled with the desperation to somehow provoke and drag the Black Dragon King out.

But the Black Dragon King was unwavering.

He simply watched Namgung Hwang, who was running wild like a wolf among sheep, with a bitter sneer on his face.

“Uh...”

Even though they were close enough to feel each other’s gaze, Namgung Hwang couldn’t rush to the owner of that gaze. He intuitively felt that the moment he pulled away, the Namgung family would be annihilated regardless of their match.

“Euhhhhh!”

Namgung Hwang severed the waist of the pirate charging at him, his entire body drenched in blood, with internal organs spilling from the pirate’s torn midsection.

The heat and the stench of blood assaulted all his senses.

Amidst the chaos of the battlefield, Namgung Hwang screamed and screamed.

They were dying.

“Ahhhh!”

His disciple.

Another one was dying.

“Kuaak!”

His family.

The overwhelming difference in numbers was clear. For every Namgung family member that fell, more than ten pirates perished. It was a testament to the Namgung family’s prowess.

But who could find joy in that?

Who would willingly exchange their life for an enemy’s death?

“You bastardsss!”

Namgung Hwang witnessed it.

His son, Namgung Do-Wei, shed tears of blood as he cut down the pirates.

That sword was swift and powerful. Even in despair, it shone with a clear light—the light the Namgung family had pursued for so long.

But...

‘Even that sword will be defeated now.’

Namgung Hwang, sensing the family’s fate, finally shed hot tears.

Even if his arm were severed or a knife pierced his heart, this man would not cry. Yet, faced with his dying family and their doomed fate, he was nothing more than a weak human being.

It was all his fault.

All his fault.

If he could save even one more person, he would willingly descend into hell, where his body would turn to dust, and he would suffer for thousands of years. But heaven didn’t even grant him the chance to sacrifice himself.

As if his life was too insignificant.

The blood of the pirates and the Namgung mingled on the ground. Alive, they were different, but in death, they were nothing more than the same corpses.

“Ughhh! U-euk! Save meee!”

“I-I want to live more!”

Everything was crumbling.

Even though they had fought steadfastly in the face of death, their will couldn’t overcome the fear of the harpoon constantly aiming for them and the death of their comrades right next to them. One by one, they began to retreat, madly shedding tears and snot.

“Do not hold backkkk!”

“Hold the ground! Please! Please!”

The cries of Namgung Do-Wei and Namgung Myung echoed around, and the sky of Plum Blossom Island rang out fiercely.

At the last moment, he tried to protect the Namgung family’s name and wished to die like a warrior. But the death they were facing now wasn’t so romantic.

Name? Fame? Dignity?

What good would those things do to someone who had already become one with the ground?

They just wanted to live. Just one more moment. Even if it meant using their comrades as a shield instead of themselves, just one more moment...

“Uh... Uhhhhh!”

The roar of the lion burst from Namgung Hwang’s mouth, engulfed in anger and despair.

His majesty was like a tiger standing among wolves. Even wolves with sharp teeth wouldn’t dare to confront a tiger.

But there was a limit to what a tiger could do on the vast battlefield.

Peeng!

A head flew in from somewhere and landed on Namgung Hwang’s chest.

“....”

Namgung Hwang turned his head away, unable to bear the sight of that head. Dark blood tears poured down from his chest.

‘Yes. Let us all die here.’

He gritted his teeth and summoned all his strength. If he was going to die anyway, he had to take at least one more pirate down with him. He didn’t even think about hope.

“Uh....”

But at that moment.

An unbelievable scream rang in Namgung Hwang’s ears.

“Ughhh! They are here! They are hereee!”

He hurriedly looked in the direction of the sound.

“Shaolin! Shaolin is here! Shaolin has arrived!”

Shaolin?

When the news, like a shower of water, was delivered, the eyes of the Namgung family swordsmen, who were falling into despair, turned to shock and joy. Everyone looked across the river with desperate faces.

“Ah... AHHH....”

“AHHHHHHH!”

“Damn it! They are here! The reinforcements are here!”

The moment they confirmed that a group of people standing on the other side of the river were wearing yellow robes, all the Namgung family’s swordsmen screamed at the top of their lungs.

“Shaolin is coming to help!”

“Endure it! Endure a bit more!”

“You damned bastards! I will kill you all!”

The appearance of Shaolin was a joy to the Namgung family, but it wasn’t news that brought despair to the pirates.

“S-Shaolin?”

“Shaolin has come?”

The pirates, too, had eyes, so the moment they saw Shaolin on the other side, they began to regain their senses.

“W-why is Shaolin...”

“Damn it!”

They knew, too.

The river was too wide for ordinary people, but they were Shaolin monks. If they put their mind to it, they could cross the river in one breath and reach Plum Blossom Island.

The movements of the pirates paused for a moment.

Chaos and confusion continued to cover the Plum Blossom Island.

“Abbot!”

“Umm.”

The abbot nodded, biting down on his lip.

“It isn’t too late! No,... it is a little later, but not so much that it cannot be undone!”

If it had been even a moment later, there wouldn’t have been anyone alive.

It was painful that a battle had broken out before they arrived, but the Namgung family hadn’t been destroyed yet. Even if they could save half of their strength, they would definitely be able to talk about victory!

“Abbot! This isn’t the time to delay!”

“Yes!”

A loud roar burst from the abbot’s mouth.

“Listen, disciples! Cross the river and support the Namgung family! Drive the evil sect’s pirates off the island!”

“Yes!”

The abbot’s flag fluttered in the blowing wind.

‘We have won it this time!’

The abbot clenched his fist.

This fight was originally a contest to see who would arrive first. Since they arrived there first, the fight was all but decided. No matter how great the pirates were, they couldn’t handle Shaolin and Namgung at the same time.

It didn’t matter how bad the Namgung family was. The likes of the pirates could be dealt with by Shaolin alone. Namgung had done their job by simply tying their feet to the island.

“Now! Go and defeat the enemies...!”

It was right at the moment when the abbot burst into a brave roar.

Flinch.

He, who had been shouting, flinched for a moment and stopped talking. Then he started shouting urgently.

“S-Stop! Everyone halt where you are!”

The Shaolin monks who were about to jump into the river looked back in surprise at the urgent voice coming from behind them.

“A-Abbot?”

Beop Gye also looked at the abbot with a face that seemed incomprehensible. Where was there any time for them to delay their help?

“...Abbot?”

However, the moment he saw the face of the abbot, he flinched. The abbot looked pale as if he had seen a ghost.

“What is it...”

“A-Ami... Amitabha....”

He was trembling horribly.

“Why... How! Why!”

Watching him explode in anger, Beop Gye slowly turned his head as if he noticed something. His gaze was directed to the upper side of the river that the abbot had been staring at first.

“Ah...”

Only then did Beop Gye see it.

A large fleet finally began to appear along the greatly curved river.

“Kuak...”

He couldn’t have seen it wrong. He couldn’t ignore it.

He had no choice but to confirm it with his own eyes.

Among the dozens of ships that appeared at the same time, the largest ship at the front. And the figure of the man standing on its bow.

There was no way he couldn’t recognize it. How could he forget that figure? Anyone who saw that man even once would never forget him.

“J-Jang....”

An exhausted tone escaped from Beop Gye’s mouth.

“Jang Il...so.”

Jang Ilso, the Hegemony Lord.

He stood at the front of the fleet advancing to Plum Blossom Island and lifted one corner of his lips as he saw Shaolin lined up along the riverside.

“My, my. How fast must they have run here.”

He covered his face with his hand, adorned with colorful rings, and smiled wickedly.

“What? What were you even expecting? Hahahahah! AHAHAHAHAH! AHAHAHAHAHAHAHAHH!”

Even the Yangtze River held its breath at Jang Ilso’s laughter, which was full of joy and madness.

Chapter 943: I Need To Show You What True Despair Is. (2)

The abbot’s face turned pale.

“H-how...”

Even though he could clearly see the approaching fleet, he wanted to deny what he was seeing.

How could this be?

How could the Ten Thousand People clan arrive at the exact same time that Shaolin arrived here?

‘Coincidence?’

Coincidence?

There was no other explanation than that it was a trick of the heavens. The heavens wanted to see how desperate he was, so they created such a situation.

However, the abbot thought that none of this was a coincidence.

The reason was simple. The one who showed up at the river was none other than the Hegemony Lord.

Hegemony Lord, Jang Ilso.

He had shown a creepy, skin-crawling spirit since the Yangtze River war. Wasn’t the thrill he felt when he heard the news of the Yangtze River from faraway Hunan still vivid?

Coincidence? There was no such thing as coincidence for Jang Ilso. Absolutely not!

“A-abbot!”

“...”

Even as the abbot was staring at the ships as if in a trance, the ships were getting closer and closer to Plum Blossom Island.

“Abbot!”

Beop Gye urgently called out to the abbot once more. His voice, full of impatience, pierced the abbot’s ears.

“If we wait any longer, it will really be irreversible! If that ship arrives at Plum Blossom Island...!”

The abbot couldn’t ignore the meaning of those words.

Namgung had already passed their limit. Even the phrase, ‘a candle in the wind,’ felt peaceful compared to the Namgung family now. In that situation, if the Ten Thousand People clan, not Shaolin, arrived at Plum Blossom Island first, wouldn’t the result be obvious?

They must head to Plum Blossom Island right away. Before those ships blocked Shaolin’s path.

“We must arrive first! Before the Ten Thousand People clan!”

That ship was definitely fast, but there was still a long way to go. If they jumped into the water right away, they could arrive before the other ship. Weren’t the pirates, who would interfere with them, now occupied with Plum Blossom Island? So they hadn’t yet lost the lead. At least, Beop Gye thought so.

But...

“... what if we go first?”

“Uh?”

At that moment, Beop Gye heard the abbot’s words and turned around, not understanding what he was trying to say.

The abbot looked back and forth between the ship and Plum Blossom Island with a cold expression.

“We...”

He paused for a moment. That short silence felt incredibly long to Beop Gye.

“... what if we get to the island first?”

“That...”

Beop Gye couldn’t easily answer that.

Was it because he had nothing to say?

No, it was because he intuitively felt something in the abbot’s voice that he had never felt before.

“... why did the Namgung family end up like that? Wasn’t it because they couldn’t get off that island?”

“But abbot! If we leave them alone, the Namgung family will be annihilated!”

Annihilation.

Those words clearly caused conflict in the abbot’s mind. His trembling shoulders were proof of it.

But...

Clench.

The abbot soon bit down on his lip. His teeth dug in, and red blood flowed down his chin, dripping. His toes, which had been pushing down his body, were now digging into the floor. He said,

“Move back.”

“A-abbot!”

“Didn’t I say to move back? All the disciples of Shaolin, get out of the waters now! Wait here!”

“Abbot! What are you...!”

“Can you just shut your mouth?!”

Beop Gye tried to scream, but the abbot silenced him.

“Uh....”

He couldn’t resist, so a helpless groan of pain flowed out of Beop Gye’s mouth.

To him, the Namgung family wasn’t just a member of the Five Great Families. They were comrades who had suffered humiliation together in the Yangtze River war. But now, his abbot was telling him to witness the annihilation of such a comrade and his family?

The abbot’s fingers were trembling.

It seemed that it wasn’t an easy decision for him to make either.

“... if we head towards Plum Blossom Island like this, we will just fall into the Hegemony Lord’s trap. Maybe this whole situation is a plot by the man to draw us into Shaolin.”

Beop Gye really wanted to ask sincerely.

If the Hegemony Lord had planned such a scheme, why didn’t they attack Shaolin after they arrived at Plum Blossom Island?

But Beop Gye couldn’t bring himself to speak those words. He was afraid that the disciples around him would hear them.

And he thought that those words might reveal the true intentions of the abbot under this sky.

He didn’t want to know. Neither did he want to confirm it.

“We... the Shaolin.”

With lips stained in blood, Beop Gye made his decision.

“Will be... watching the situation from here.”

The last voice was faint and barely audible. However, no one was unaware of the decision the abbot had made, even if they didn’t have to hear it.

“... Amitabha.”

In contrast to that voice, the clear unfavorable remarks pierced the ears of Shaolin disciples who couldn’t bear to look straight at the island.

“...”

Namgung Hwang looked across the river with blank eyes.

The sight of Shaolin, who seemed ready to jump into the water and come to their rescue, hesitantly retreating and then forming a line again, was clearly visible.

“What....”

What were they doing?

Why were they stopping there? What on earth were they planning to do?

“Why...?”

Someone was clearly heard mumbling that.

Silence settled in.

A terrible silence descended on the island, making it hard to believe that just a moment ago, the mad beasts were biting and running wild.

“Why... why are they not coming?”

“Just...”

Until then, the Namgung family’s men still held onto a faint glimmer of hope. There must be a reason. It was a little delayed, but Shaolin would surely come across the river to support them.

They believed it.

No, there was no other way for them than to believe that.

Even Namgung Hwang had no doubt that Shaolin would cross the river to save them at that moment.

But...

“W-what is that ship?”

A single word that came out of someone’s mouth spread like ripples.

The gaze that Shaolin had suddenly taken away turned to the side. Only then did everyone’s eyes catch the sight of the fleet approaching the island.

At that moment, everyone there understood what was happening. It was a judgment based on sense and not reason.

The Namgung family’s eyes shook greatly. Filled with distrust and fear, they slowly turned their gaze back to the other side of the river. They trembled pitifully as if they couldn’t muster up the courage to confirm the reality they were facing.

However, the moment when they must face the truth would surely come.

When their anxious gazes finally turned back to Shaolin, they saw it. Shaolin was standing on the far-off riverside.

What made those who had seen the despair of the Namgung family was not the tip of Shaolin’s goals, as they took a step back like they had no intention of coming anymore.

What truly made them despair was the absence of their eyes.

The eyes of the Shaolin monks, who should have been staring at the island with sharp focus, weren’t visible.

Because they were not looking there.

They were called the great Shaolin of the world, the Big Dipper of the Murim world. Those very arhat monks of Shaolin, who never bowed their heads to any sect in the world, were now bowing their heads like sinners.

As if they couldn’t bear to look at each other.

Even with this much distance between them, they were turning away from the gaze of the Namgung family as if their guilt was too great to bear.

How could they not know?

Even an idiot would know what was happening there.

They were... abandoned.

“Ugh....”

“Uh....”

The thing that pushed people into the greatest despair was always hope.

Humans did not despair where there was no hope at all. They took that for granted.

But humans who found a sliver of hope would try to cling to it.

And when they realized that hope was nothing more than a mirage, humans finally crumbled.

“Uh... uh...”

An indescribable despair began to engulf the Namgung family.

The fact that they had been abandoned by their trusted allies, that Shaolin had turned a blind eye to their deaths, plunged them into a bottomless pit of despair they had never experienced before in their lives.

“Ughh....”

A groan, like a wounded animal panting, escaped their lips.

Maybe it wasn’t all that different after all. In reality, they were now wounded animals, and all that was left was death. With the lifeline called Shaolin cut off, all that was left was an endless fall.

They no longer had the will or the spirit to swing their swords. Their limbs felt heavy, as if they belonged to someone else. They just wanted to let everything go.

Frustration is scary because it makes people collapse. Everyone was being helplessly sucked into a deep abyss that was too dark to even dare to look into.

However, at that moment, only one person let out anger instead of despair.

“ABBOOTTTTTTT!”

A desperate scream burst out from the mouth of the wounded tiger, Namgung Hwang.

“Abbot! ABBOOTTTTTT! EUAHHHHHHH!”

A blood-boiling howl burst from his throat.

The blood vessels in both eyes burst out, and the rested throat let out this roar like twisted iron.

Just then, when everyone was groaning under such desperate frustration.

He finally arrived on the land, the one who considered all this muddy despair as mere fun.

Kuaaang!

The large battleship crashed into the sand.

Namgung and the pirates, even Namgung Hwang and the Black Dragon King, watched the scene.

“Eumm.”

A low nasal voice flowed out lightly. It was a very small sound, but it strangely spread clearly throughout the island.

Finally, a person appeared with light steps.

“....”

Namgung Hwang stared at the figure with red eyes, his blood vessels bursting.

It was the figure of the one who had ridiculed them in this very river three years ago.

That person hadn’t changed at all.

A gorgeous red robe with strange embroidery engraved with gold thread. Pure white skin, hair tied up so that not a single strand fell, a gorgeous crown, and even excessive ornaments.

Tak.

Jang Ilso, who lightly jumped from the ship to land, slowly straightened his slightly bent body.

Clank.

The sound of jewelry he wore clashing against each other forcibly brought back memories from three years ago. That sound awakened nightmares and brought in new fear.

Namgung Hwang’s gaze fell on Jang Ilso’s lips.

“So strange.”

Jang Ilso spoke softly with an extremely friendly face.

“It has been a while, but you don’t seem to be welcoming at all.”

His lips, red as blood, drew a curve like the moon. The words pouring out from those lips felt truly ominous and terrifying.

Namgung Hwang tightly shut his eyes.

“I am... glad to see you here. Hahahaha!”

And then he thought.

It might have been better to die at the hands of the Black Dragon King.

Instead of being toyed with by this devil....

Chapter 944: I Need To Show You What True Despair Is. (3)

He might not be the strongest.

Jang Ilso was called the best in the world, but no one knew whether his strength was truly the best. No, no one even wanted to know that.

The reason? It was quite simple.

That wasn’t even that important.

The position of everyone living in Kangho was determined by their strength. To build fame in the dangerous situations of Kangho, one had to prove their strength repeatedly.

But this guy... wasn’t the only one.

Jang Ilso, the Hegemony Lord.

That name was the only one that stood out in this Kangho.

Even if Jang Ilso’s strength was no more than that of a third-rate backstreet thug, everyone in the world would say it in unison.

This guy... was the most dangerous person in the current Kangho.

The viper carrying a deadly poison slowly walked across the sandy beach. His steps were light rather than threatening and elegant rather than serious. It was a leisurely gait as if he had come out for a walk.

But no one on Plum Blossom Island could take their eyes off that sight.

“...”

Dry saliva rolled down his face.

No one had ordered them to stop fighting. But the war naturally stopped.

At least Namgung Myung seemed to know why.

No rabbit would fight for territory in front of a wolf.

The moment Jang Ilso appeared, everyone here instinctively felt that now wasn’t the time to fight one another.

“Uhm.”

A low, nasal voice blew through the wind across Plum Blossom Island.

“This...”

Jang Ilso’s gaze turned to Namgung Hwang.

“It looks like it isn’t worth it...”

Namgung Hwang clenched his fists. It felt like a cool, scaly snake was strangling his neck.

In fact, this wasn’t just a feeling. Namgung Hwang’s eyes could see the ship that followed Jang Ilso, without docking at the island, leisurely turning its direction and blocking the river between Plum Blossom Island and Shaolin.

Now, even if Shaolin belatedly decided to come and help, it would be difficult to break through.

Unlike his peaceful steps, Jang Ilso was definitely squeezing their breath.

As if to confirm this, the warriors disembarked from the Ten Thousand People Clan and ran, following Jang Ilso. Ho Ga-Myung ran out and stood next to him as if guarding him.

Even though he knew this wasn’t the situation to laugh, Namgung Hwang felt like he would end up laughing in vain.

Jang Ilso and a guard? Could there be words that didn’t go well together?

At that moment, the corners of Jang Ilso’s mouth rose strangely.

“The Namgung family of the azure heavens... how did it end up like this? Uh?”

The snake-like gaze choked the breathing of the exhausted Namgung family’s swordsmen. Jang Ilso took a deep breath and laughed.

This was fun.

He liked this sight so much that it gave him goosebumps.

The Blue Sky’s Namgung Family.

Weren’t the eyes of those who shone with such splendid fame now filled with void and despair?

Some were so resentful they were ready to cry, and some were devastated by the fact that it was all over. Some were glaring at him with eyes full of venom, and others were looking at him with eyes that yearned for some mercy.

All of these different eyes were focused on Jang Ilso.

However, there was one thing in common among those eyes.

There was no more light of hope to be found.

Step. Step.

Jang Ilso stepped ahead.

The pirates blocking the Namgung family’s path moved to the left and right with expressions of fear. A surprisingly wide path opened up in a place where there was no room to step because the pirates were densely packed.

Jang Ilso walked along the path leisurely, as if it were natural.

The footsteps finally stopped in front of the Namgung family’s Blue Sky Sword troops. These were the ones guarding the battle line. They were the best swordsmen that the Namgung family boasted about.

They fought a short but fierce battle. As a result, the Blue Sky Sword troops who were still standing were covered in blood, cuts, and stab wounds.

As Jang Ilso approached, they instinctively raised their swords and aimed at him, baring their teeth like cornered beasts.

However, Jang Ilso continued to approach them with the same slow steps, as if he couldn’t even see the swords pointed at him.

Flinch.

Then, the tips of the swords of the Blue Sky Sword troops began to tremble.

It was a truly bizarre and eerie situation where the ones threatening with swords were trembling, while the one being threatened, with bare hands, was laughing.

“D-don’t...”

The member standing right in front of Jang Ilso barely managed to hold out a trembling sword. He threatened Jang Ilso by extending the tip of his sword further as if he would pierce his neck at any moment.

But Jang Ilso seemed to know that this sword would never pierce his neck... or perhaps he didn’t care even if his throat were cut.

When he finally stopped walking, the tip of the bloody sword was almost touching his neck.

All one had to do was stab it.

By channeling qi and pushing his arm forward, he could pierce that pale white neck. All he needed was a bit of courage.

But...

The sword never advanced any further. It trembled so pitifully that it was hard to believe it was held by a swordsman of the Namgung family. In fact, he backed away, even more intimidated.

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso smiled seductively and slowly reached out, placing his fingers on the trembling blade.

Swish.

At that moment, they saw.

Jang Ilso’s fingertips split slightly, and a drop of red blood flowed down the sword.

“....”

Before seeing this sight, everyone vaguely thought that such a hand would not be hurt by a common sword. That was why he felt so terrified of them.

But the moment they saw that drop of blood, their hearts clenched even harder.

Those who did not bleed and therefore did not fear death, and those who bled but did not fear death.

Which one was scarier?

Swish.

The white fingers adorned with ornaments slowly advanced along the pathetic, trembling blade. Gently caressing the bloody tip, they finally reached the hand of the swordsman, who was tightly gripping the handle of the sword.

Jang Ilso’s fingers, which climbed slowly and sharply up the arm like a snake, finally brushed the Blue Sky Sword troop member’s shoulder, stained his pale cheek with blood, and reached his forehead.

It was just a light and slow movement, but everyone could do nothing more than stare at the sight. It was as if they were possessed.

At that moment.

Jang Ilso’s hand, which had been spread out, suddenly opened.

At that moment, everyone flinched at the sudden change.

Tak.

Jang Ilso’s large hand covered the head of the Blue Sky Sword troop member as if he were stroking the head of a small child.

“...pity.”

Jang Ilso’s affectionate voice, filled with compassion, softly resonated.

“You all fought so hard....”

The Blue Sky Sword troops trembled all over at that. The Namgung family swordsmen, who were watching, bit their lips until they bled.

How should they describe this situation where they received comfort from the enemy and were momentarily comforted by that comfort?

When Jang Ilso appeared there, did even a single person expect such a thing to happen?

Jang Ilso slowly shook his head. Then he opened his mouth again.

“And yet in the end...”

He blurted out the words that the Namgung family members absolutely didn’t want to hear.

“You have been abandoned.”

At that moment, everyone felt breathless.

They had seen despair and then hope again and fell from the hill of hope to the pit of hell. Those were the words they absolutely didn’t want to hear.

“Such a poor thing... tsk tsk.”

Jang Ilso’s eyebrows lowered as if he truly felt pity.

Namgung Hwang gritted his teeth at the abominable sight. He could no longer bear to watch.

“Jang... Ilso!”

Jang Ilso slowly turned around, his expression filled with bewilderment as if he had forgotten Namgung Hwang was there.

“... do not mock us.”

“Mock?”

Jang Ilso repeated as if he had heard something amusing and then laughed.

“Right. Mock! A warrior would rather die than be mocked! It would be better to have a clean kill!”

Jang Ilso smiled at Namgung Hwang’s words.

“It is strange, you know... the more I hear it, the stranger it gets.”

“... what is so strange about that?”

“When did I ever make fun of you?”

“...”

His innocent expression seemed genuine, though everyone knew it was an act. But that wasn’t what mattered. That one expression, that one gesture, showed how much he could unsettle them.

“The one who had fun with you... wasn’t it Shaolin?”

“What kind of nonsense is that!”

“Am I wrong?”

Jang Ilso raised his hand, pointing to the sky before slowly lowering it to indicate the Shaolin across the river.

“See.”

“....”

“It is just a river. There is no reason for them not to cross it. Wouldn’t the name of Shaolin cry if they couldn’t cross the river with simple ships blocking the way?”

Namgung Hwang bit his lip.

“But what are they doing? They are simply watching. They are just observing your final moments as you die here.”

“Jang Ilso!”

“And that is...”

Jang Ilso lowered his hand, a blatant sneer forming on his lips.

“That is what mocking means.”

Namgung Hwang clenched his fists so hard that the skin around his bones turned white.

Jang Ilso continued.

“They came running as if they were going to save you as if they were some great saviors, but in the end, they have no intention of shedding blood to save you. That must be what they think your worth is.”

Namgung Hwang knew he wasn’t angry because he was being mocked.

The man’s words were not wrong at all, which was why they were so painful to him. A scream that he couldn’t bring himself to shout formed in his throat.

“... So? Are you trying to mock us who ended up in this situation?”

He gritted his teeth and spoke.

“Don’t make me laugh, Jang Ilso! The Namgung family will not be a joke to anyone! If death is meant to happen, I will fight until the last moment! Every one of us will! The fact that we were abandoned will not tarnish the name of the Namgung family!”

He spoke so carelessly that he didn’t even understand what he was saying.

But the anger in his voice seemed to have been clearly conveyed to those who used the name of Namgung. The eyes of those who were losing hope began to light up.

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso shook his head as if he was having a hard time.

“Come ahead, Jang Ilso! I will be the first to die!”

Namgung Hwang gathered all his strength.

He must be the first to show the Namgung warriors, who were trembling in fear, that a warrior proves himself through death.

Jang Ilso just stared blankly at Namgung Hwang, who was raising the spirits as if he would rush to fight at any moment.

At the very moment when Namgung Hwang was about to rush in, holding his sword, Jang Ilso slowly opened his mouth.

“... Should I do it then?”

Namgung Hwang’s body stopped. It was like he had been struck by lightning.

Disbelief, shock, frustration, and anticipation.

All those emotions surged through Namgung Hwang’s eyes like waves in a short moment.

“What... now...”

That was the devil’s whisper. He should never have heard it. But Namgung Hwang had no choice but to hear it.

“What... did you say?”

Jang Ilso smiled broadly. He was like the devil who crawled out of hell and spoke to a despicable human.

With red lips, he blurted out the same words from before in the same tone.

“Should I save you then?”

“....”

A deep and heavy silence descended on Plum Blossom Island.

“Hahahaha....”

And in the midst of that silence.

The devil laughed out.

“AHHAHAHAHAHAHAH! EHAHAHAHAHAHAH!”

Jang Ilso’s laughter and the jingling of the jewelry filled the temporary residents’ ears on Plum Blossom Island.

Chapter 945: I Need To Show You What True Despair Is. (4)

Who didn’t know?

This was nothing but a joke. The words coming out of that devil’s mouth were nothing more than dragging them down to the ground, trampling on them, mocking them, and laughing at them.

But... was there anyone who didn’t waver?

The moment the word “save” came out of Jang Ilso’s mouth, was there even a single person who didn’t harbor even a little bit of hope and longing in their hearts?

Namgung Do-Wei closed his eyes tightly.

‘I...’

The moment he heard Jang Ilso’s words, even Namgung Do-Wei, who was prepared to die, felt a strong impulse. The impulse to live. The fear of not wanting to die.

If there weren’t people watching him around, he might have knelt before Jang Ilso and held onto his legs.

Begging for his life. Not wanting to die. Not yet... death wasn’t right yet.

Who would be different from that?

No matter how upright you tried to stand, how could your desire to live be different? Jang Ilso’s one word easily dug up that desire that was hidden deep inside.

“This...”

Namgung Hwang clenched his teeth, unable to say anything.

He must have felt the same way as Namgung Do-Wei right then. The trembling of his hand wasn’t in anger at Jang Ilso but anger at himself for not being righteous at that moment.

In the end, Namgung Hwang shouted in a seething voice.

“You evil bastard!”

Jang Ilso’s eyes looked at him oddly.

“This time, evil.”

“...”

“Hmm. Well... that is what I like to say. Evil, mean, cowardly... well, whatever. Nice. But....”

Jang Ilso slowly shook his head.

“Not in this situation. Namgung Hwang. Now is not the time to curse at me.”

Namgung Hwang went silent. He had intended to briefly understand Jang Ilso’s real thoughts, but it wasn’t enough. With his skills, he could never see into that monster’s true feelings.

“It isn’t something to think only of as bad.”

Jang Ilso gently turned his body and slowly approached Namgung Hwang. Namgung Hwang’s sword still hadn’t let down its guard, but Jang Ilso remained calm and relaxed.

Jang Ilso leaned in and whispered softly in Namgung Hwang’s ear.

“Now... Namgung Hwang. Think about this, okay? You have to think desperately now. Being abandoned isn’t necessarily a bad thing, right? You are free now. Isn’t that true?”

“....”

Namgung Hwang bit his lip and glared at Jang Ilso. His blood-stained face, disheveled hair, and bloodshot eyes were nothing short of brutal.

But did Namgung Hwang know?

What did it mean for a swordsman, who must maintain his stance, to lose himself to words? That this action alone proved beyond doubt that he was wavering.

“You people....”

He spat out.

“Are you telling me to be your subordinate? Do you think the Blue Sky Namgung family will be the dogs of the Evil Sect?”

He shouted so that everyone shaken by the devil’s voice could hear it clearly.

“If we die, we die, but we will not be your dogs! Do not make me laugh now, Jang Ilso!”

Everyone agreed with that statement. Survival is important for everyone. However, there is clearly a value that cannot be exchanged for the sake of survival.

The Blue Sky Namgung family.

They could accept death, but they could never become subordinates to Jang Ilso. If they could, they would not have thrown away peace and invaded Plum Blossom Island.

At that moment, when strong antipathy towards Jang Ilso was about to engulf everyone.

“... dogs?”

Jang Ilso’s calm yet puzzled gesture, as he slightly tilted his head, forcibly drew everyone’s attention.

“Again... you speak of strange things which I don’t understand.”

Jang Ilso raised his eyebrows and exaggeratedly laughed at Namgung Hwang.

“Ga-Myung.”

“Yes, Alliance lord.”

“Did I say that to them?”

“You said nothing, lord.”

Ho Ga-Myung answered right away as if he didn’t have to think about it. His face, in stark contrast to Jang Ilso’s, was so expressionless that it was impossible to guess what he was thinking.

“Then why do they say such things?”

“Because everyone thinks within their own limitations, lord. It is only natural that they cannot keep up with the Alliance Lord’s thoughts. It isn’t something we can blame them for.”

“Hmm, right. It makes sense.”

Jang Ilso nodded leisurely and looked at Namgung Hwang with a big smile.

Namgung Hwang could only watch the conversation, which was like an opera.

From the moment Jang Ilso arrived there, everyone seemed to be moving according to the roles Jang Ilso had set up. Even breathing in and out, getting angry, and expressing frustrations.

“No, that isn’t it, Namgung Hwang. And...”

Jang Ilso’s gaze turned from Namgung Hwang to the Namgung family swordsmen.

“Namgung family.”

It seemed to tell him that the authority to decide this matter wasn’t just Namgung Hwang’s but also the family’s swordsmen.

“Why do you think I am deceiving you?”

“....”

“Because I am from the Evil Sect?”

“...”

“I am just telling the truth.”

They were drawn in.

To that voice. That tone. With that one gesture.

“I will tell you again. That is not what I want. You don’t have to be a dog, and you don’t have to join the Four Precepts Alliance. There is just one thing I want.”

His thin lips twisted.

“Just let the words’ please save me’ come out of your mouths. Right. That will do.”

Everyone stared at him, lost in thought.

Not only the Namgung family but also the pirates were the same. Their eyes were filled with bewilderment as they looked at him.

The faces of many people filling this island were dyed in the same color; it was truly a miracle.

Were they in their right mind? Is the man really saying such things now? Why? Just why? Why!?

Jang Ilso stood there among those eyes filled with disbelief and shock, giggling.

“It isn’t so hard, right? Is it?”

“....”

“That is fine. I will spare you the moment you say it. I will just simply let you go. Right there. To the north of the river, where you so desperately wanted to return. To the comfortable and cozy place where your home is.”

The devil’s whisper must have a deadly poison that they could not even guess.

But... even if they knew this fact, nothing would change.

How could they ignore such sweet words?

“I will even let you collect the bodies and take them away. Even though they died here, they deserve to be buried in their homeland, right? Right? Because they fought harder than anyone else.”

The Namgung family’s swordsmen turned their gaze, unable to overcome their instincts. The sight of their family and comrades dying without even having the chance to close their eyes was etched into their minds.

“It isn’t difficult.”

Jang Ilso said as if soothing a child.

“Just say one word. Just say one word, and everything will come true. In the name of mine, Jang Ilso, the Hegemony Lord....”

He paused for a moment.

Everyone waited for his words with thirst.

“... I promise.”

After the sweet and fatal words came, there was a brief silence.

And then someone spoke as if possessed.

“Save m....”

“NO!”

At that moment, Namgung Do-Wei, who was close by, reached out and covered the man’s mouth. Then he shouted with an angry face.

“Jang Ilso!”

Jang Ilso frowned slightly.

“Now, now. How dare such a great warrior close the mouth of someone who wants to live, only to send them to their death. How shameful, even the Evil Sect doesn’t do something like this.”

“Shut up, you fucking moron! What nonsense are you even talking about!”

“Hmm?”

“Don’t make fun of the Namgung family! We will not fall for your....”

At that moment, Jang Ilso’s eyes, which had appeared gentle until then, contorted immediately. The presence was so surprisingly fierce that Namgung Do-Wei unconsciously stopped talking and took a step back.

The pale eyes were as fierce as if blue flames were rising around them.

“You think I am a fool or something?”

Jang Ilso clenched his teeth.

“I will tell you, young lord. It isn’t me who is being a fool; it is you.”

“...”

“Shaolin has abandoned you. No, the Justice faction has abandoned you. I will ask now. Do you think any of the sects that followed Shaolin across the river would be willing to shed blood for you?”

No one could readily answer that question.

Of course, he wanted to shout. He wanted to yell and curse at them not to look down on the Alliance of the sects.

But hadn’t he already seen how the Shaolin sect, which protected the factions of the Central Plains, had abandoned them?

“Your lives are so insignificant to them. But... no, not them either. It is you who consider your lives as even more insignificant.”

“....”

“Life isn’t like that.”

Keeik.

Jang Ilso’s rings rubbed against one another, making an eerie sound.

“The value of life is something you decide for yourself. At least, that is how I am. I stabbed a knife into the stomach of someone I called a comrade, bit the neck of someone who called himself a friend, rolled around in filth, and bit the cliff with my teeth when my arms broke!”

“....”

“That is what it means to survive and live.”

Everyone knew that these words were neither persuasion nor threats. This was Jang Ilso’s true nature.

“But... you are telling me that I am being ridiculous here?”

Jang Ilso bared his teeth.

“If you don’t make fun of those who choose death because they couldn’t utter a single word to be saved, who else in the world can ridicule your actions? Why should I treat those who consider their worth lower than insects any better? Answer me, boy! What right do you have to decide the death of someone whose mouth you silenced?”

Namgung Do-Wei’s hands trembled.

If Jang Ilso had spoken one more word, Namgung Do-Wei might have collapsed right there.

But Jang Ilso relaxed his force at that moment. He slumped his shoulders as if exhausted.

“It isn’t difficult.”

“.....”

“No one remembers your death. The glory gained through death is nothing more than crumbs that the survivors throw to the dead. Useless crumbs taken from enormous loot.”

Someone gulped, and someone clenched their fists at that.

“It is so sad to die holding onto those crumbs... isn’t it?”

He almost nodded.

At those whispered words.

“So... tell me.”

Jang Ilso’s eyes, filled with momentum, took on that bright and mysterious smile again.

“Fight for your life. That will work. That will be enough for you to survive.”

Step. Step. Step.

Jang Ilso walked towards the Blue Sky Sword troops. He whispered in their ears, those who couldn’t even think of using their blades.

Softly but clearly and slowly so that everyone could hear it.

“Do you want to live?”

Those who heard that voice froze in place.

“You want to live. Everyone wants to live. But those who try to use their lives sparingly say it easily. A warrior must know how to throw away his life for pride. He must discard life like a rag toy for the sake of his side.”

Both eyes trembled. Jang Ilso recited the question that came to the minds of the swordsmen whose hearts were trembling.

“For what?”

“....”

“No, no, you will know when you live. Pride isn’t as great as you think. It is like the night that passes when you close your eyes for a moment. So... tell me, I want to live. I want to survive.”

“I-I....”

In the end, tears flowed from the eyes of the Blue Sky Sword troops.

A swordsman who had been trained through countless years of harsh trials, a member of the Namgung family, who was not afraid of death and was prepared to protect the name of Blue Sky with all his life, was crying like a child.

It was an unbearable pressure.

A glimmer of hope followed by great despair.

At the end of that, hope, will, and harmony were powerless in front of the one who grabbed their souls and twisted them.

“Rest now. Right?”

Red lips couldn’t resist the words.

“Say it now, with that mouth.”

The eyes of the swordsman, who didn’t know where to go, cried out pitifully with a hoarse voice.

His hands, trembling and unable to find their way, finally grabbed Jang Ilso’s sleeve.

“Save me....”

“Stop now, Hegemony Lord.”

At that moment, Namgung Hwang intervened.

His voice was weak as if it wasn’t his at all.

“... you have enjoyed it enough.”

It felt as if his soul had left him as if he had aged several years at once.

Namgung Hwang’s head slowly dropped.

“We....”

He spoke in a voice that showed no sign of pressure.

“We... lost.”

It was the moment when everything for Namgung Hwang had collapsed.

Chapter 946: I Need To Show You What True Despair Is. (5)

The abbot gazed at Plum Blossom Island with a bewildered expression.

The fleet of the Ten Thousand People clan, led by Jang Ilso, obstructed their view of the island. Still, it wasn’t difficult to see between the ships and assess the situation.

‘...What is happening?’

The war was in full swing. Jang Ilso had arrived at Plum Blossom Island amidst such a fierce situation. Even a three-year-old child could guess what would happen next.

But nothing was happening on the island. Not a single scream or clash of weapons could be heard.

It was quiet.

The excessive silence seemed to pierce the heart in an eerie way. The corners of the abbot’s lips trembled.

He could tell intuitively what the source of that silence was.

Jang Ilso, the Hegemony Lord.
